# <span class="deva-large">श</span> <span class="iast-large">śa</span>

<div class="dict-page-header">
  <span class="badge-count">975 entrées</span>
  <a href="../" class="back-link">← Index du dictionnaire</a>
</div>

---

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry--za">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">॰श</span> <i>-śa</i> (pour <a href="#entry-zaya" class="skt-link">śaya</a>) étendu sur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> à qui appartient, en qui est.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry--zas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">॰शस्</span> <i>-śas</i> forme des adv. de manière (comme fr.-ment) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> itère le numéral iic. (-fois).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-z_r_n">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शृण्</span> <i>śṛṇ</i> cf. <a href="#entry-zru" class="skt-link">śru</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-z_rfga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शृङ्ग</span> <i>śṛṅga</i> <a href="#entry-z_rfga" class="skt-link">n.</a> corne; défense (d'éléphant) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pic, pointe, sommet, objet pointu; not. seringue <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de l'arc Śṛṅga «Pointu» de <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a>; cf <a href="#entry-Uvai_s_navacaapa" class="skt-link">Vaiṣṇavacāpa</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>cornu</i>; ang. horn; fr. corne, seringue.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-z_rfgaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शृङ्गार</span> <i>śṛṅgāra</i> [<a href="#entry-z_rfga" class="skt-link">śṛṅga</a><a href="#entry--ra" class="skt-link">-ra</a>] a. <a href="#entry-z_rfgaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-z_rfgaara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-z_rfgaaraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śṛṅgārā</i> orné, joli — <a href="#entry-z_rfgaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> ornement, parure <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> amour sexuel <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. l'érotique, un des 9 modes artistiques [<a href="#entry-rasa" class="skt-link">rasa</a>]; on lui associe la couleur bleu nuit et la divinité <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-z_rfgaaraprakaaza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śṛṅgāraprakāśa</i> [<a href="#entry-prakaaza" class="skt-link">prakāśa</a>] <a href="#entry-z_rfgaaraprakaaza" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Śṛṅgāraprakāśa «Lumières sur la passion», ouvrage de poétique et de dramaturgie en 36 chapitres attribué à <a href="#entry-Ubhojadeva" class="skt-link">Bhojadeva</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-z_rfgaararasa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śṛṅgārarasa</i> [<a href="#entry-rasa" class="skt-link">rasa</a>] <a href="#entry-z_rfgaararasa" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. saveur érotique.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-z_rfgaaratilaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śṛṅgāratilaka</i> [<a href="#entry-tilaka" class="skt-link">tilaka</a>] <a href="#entry-z_rfgaaratilaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Śṛṅgāratilaka «Ornement d'érotique», recueil d'une trentaine de poèmes attribué à <a href="#entry-Ukaalidaasa" class="skt-link">Kālidāsa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-z_rfgaarazataka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śṛṅgāraśataka</i> [<a href="#entry-zataka" class="skt-link">śataka</a>] <a href="#entry-z_rfgaarazataka" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Śṛṅgāraśataka, recueil de 100 poèmes sur l'érotique de <a href="#entry-Ubhart_rhari_1" class="skt-link">Bhartṛhari_1</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-z_rfgaarazekhara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śṛṅgāraśekhara</i> [<a href="#entry-zekhara" class="skt-link">śekhara</a>] <a href="#entry-z_rfgaarazekhara" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. [<a href="#entry-Uvaasavadattaa" class="skt-link">Vāsavadattā</a>] np. du roi Śṛṅgāraśekhara, père de la princesse <a href="#entry-Uvaasavadattaa_1" class="skt-link">Vāsavadattā_1</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-z_rfgaarin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śṛṅgārin</i> [<a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] a. <a href="#entry-z_rfgaarin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-z_rfgaarin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-z_rfgaari_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śṛṅgāriṇī</i> passionné, amoureux <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> érotique, sexuel; orné, plaisant — <a href="#entry-z_rfgaarin" class="skt-link">m.</a> amant passionné — <a href="#entry-z_rfgaari_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śṛṅgāriṇī</i> épouse, maîtresse.<br> <a href="#entry-z_rfgaara" class="skt-link">śṛṅgāra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-z_rfgaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śṛṅgaka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-z_rfgaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-z_rfgaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> corne <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> corne du croissant de lune <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> seringue — <a href="#entry-z_rfgikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śṛṅgikā</i> ifc. corne de <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> mus. sorte de flûte.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-z_rfgavat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śṛṅgavat</i> [<a href="#entry--vat" class="skt-link">-vat</a>] <a href="#entry-z_rfgavat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-z_rfgavat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-z_rfgavatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śṛṅgavatī</i> cornu <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> (montagne) aux nombreux pics.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-z_rfgavera">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śṛṅgavera</i> [<a href="#entry-vera" class="skt-link">vera</a>] <a href="#entry-z_rfgavera" class="skt-link">m.</a> [«au corps en forme de corne»] (racine de) gingembre; cf. <a href="#entry-aardraka" class="skt-link">ārdraka</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> [<i>śṛṅgaverapura</i>] géo. np. de la ville de Śṛṅgavera ou Śṛṅgaverapura, sur les bords du Gange, près de mod. Allahabad; elle possédait un système hydraulique de filtration et stockage de ses eaux; myth. <a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a> y aurait commencé son exil de 14 ans; cf. <a href="#entry-Uguha_1" class="skt-link">Guha_1</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> fr. gingembre.<br> <a href="#entry-z_rfga" class="skt-link">śṛṅga</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-z_rfgeri">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शृङ्गेरि</span> <i>śṛṅgeri</i> var. <i>śṛṅgerī</i> [<a href="#entry-z_rfga" class="skt-link">śṛṅga</a>-<a href="#entry-giri" class="skt-link">giri</a>] <a href="#entry-z_rfgeri" class="skt-link">f.</a> géo. np. de Śṛṅgeri ou Śṛṅgerī «Mont Cornu», village du <a href="#entry-Ukar_naa_ta" class="skt-link">Karṇāṭa</a> sur les berges de la rivière <a href="#entry-Utufgaa" class="skt-link">Tuṅgā</a> (à 100 km au nord-est de Mangalore), site du <a href="#entry-dak_si_naamnaaya" class="skt-link">dakṣiṇāmnāya</a> <a href="#entry-ma_tha" class="skt-link">maṭha</a> de <a href="#entry-Uzafkara_1" class="skt-link">Śaṅkara_1</a>, qui y passa 12 ans à prêcher à ses disciples; on y trouve le temple de Vidyāśaṅkara (14⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-z_rfgi_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शृङ्गिण</span> <i>śṛṅgiṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-z_rfgin" class="skt-link">śṛṅgin</a>] a. <a href="#entry-z_rfgi_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-z_rfgi_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> cornu — <a href="#entry-z_rfgi_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> bélier.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-z_rfgin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शृङ्गिन्</span> <i>śṛṅgin</i> [<a href="#entry-z_rfga" class="skt-link">śṛṅga</a><a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] a. <a href="#entry-z_rfgin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-z_rfgin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-z_rfgi_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śṛṅgiṇī</i> qui a des cornes.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-z_rfkhala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शृङ्खल</span> <i>śṛṅkhala</i> <a href="#entry-z_rfkhala" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-z_rfkhala" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-z_rfkhalaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śṛṅkhalā</i> chaîne; fers; not. fers pour entraver les éléphants <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> chaîne à mesurer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. chaîne de charactères mnémotechnique dans un texte.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-z_rgaala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शृगाल</span> <i>śṛgāla</i> var. <i>sṛgāla</i> <a href="#entry-z_rgaala" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. zoo. chacal <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> (péj.) canaille — <a href="#entry-z_rgaalii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śṛgālī</i> natu. chacal femelle.<br> <i>na</i> <i>tvā</i> <i>śṛgālaṃ</i> <i>manye</i> Tu ne vaux pas mieux qu'un chacal.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-z_rr">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">शॄ</span> <i>śṝ</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">9</span>] pr. <i>(śṛṇāti)</i> aor. [<span class="skt-grammar">5</span>] <i>(aśarīt)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>śīrṇa</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>parā</i>, <i>vi</i><i>)</i> casser, briser; broyer — ps. <i>(śīryate)</i> se rompre, se briser, éclater; se détacher, tomber <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> se gâter, se flétrir; pourrir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-za_ms">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">शंस्</span> <i>śaṃs</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(śaṃsati)</i> pft. <i>(śaśaṃsa)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>śasta</i><i>)</i> pfp. <i>(</i><i>śaṃsya</i>, <i>śasya</i>, <i>śastavya</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>ā</i>, <i>pra</i><i>)</i> indiquer, montrer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> raconter, dire; proclamer, révéler; prononcer (not. un serment) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> véd. réciter (comme <a href="#entry-hot_r" class="skt-link">hotṛ</a>) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> louer, célébrer — ca. <i>(śaṃsayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>śaṃsita</i><i>)</i> id. <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>carmen</i>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-za_msa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शंस</span> <i>śaṃsa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-za_ms" class="skt-link">śaṃs</a>] <a href="#entry-za_msa" class="skt-link">m.</a> récitation; proclamation <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> véd. not. récitation du <a href="#entry-U_rgveda" class="skt-link">Ṛgveda</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> incantation; invocation; promesse, vœu; imprécation.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-za_msin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शंसिन्</span> <i>śaṃsin</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-za_ms" class="skt-link">śaṃs</a>] a. <a href="#entry-za_msin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-za_msin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-za_msinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śaṃsinī</i> qui indique; qui annonce, qui proclame; qui récite.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-za_msita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शंसित</span> <i>śaṃsita</i> [ca. pp. <a href="#entry-za_ms" class="skt-link">śaṃs</a>] a. <a href="#entry-za_msita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-za_msita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-za_msitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śaṃsitā</i> déclaré, dit; célébré, loué.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-za_msya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शंस्य</span> <i>śaṃsya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-za_ms" class="skt-link">śaṃs</a>] a. <a href="#entry-za_msya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-za_msya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-za_msyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śaṃsyā</i> à réciter <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> digne d'éloge <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> désirable — <a href="#entry-za_msya" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. feu sacrificiel de l'Est.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-za_myu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शंयु</span> <i>śaṃyu</i> [<a href="#entry-zam_1" class="skt-link">śam_1</a><a href="#entry--yu" class="skt-link">-yu</a>] a. <a href="#entry-za_myu" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-za_myu" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-za_myu" class="skt-link">f.</a> bénéfique, bienveillant <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Śaṃyu «Bienveillant», fils de <a href="#entry-Ub_rhaspati" class="skt-link">Bṛhaspati</a> et <a href="#entry-Utaaraa" class="skt-link">Tārā</a>; il préside au sacrifice véd.; on le dit parfois père d'<a href="#entry-Uagni" class="skt-link">Agni</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-za_n_da">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शण्ड</span> <i>śaṇḍa</i> <a href="#entry-za_n_da" class="skt-link">m.</a> lait caillé, fromage frais <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Śaṇḍa, fils de <a href="#entry-Uzukra" class="skt-link">Śukra</a>, prêtre des <a href="#entry-asuraas" class="skt-link">asurās</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-za_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शण</span> <i>śaṇa</i> <a href="#entry-za_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. bot. <i>Cannabis sativa</i>, chanvre <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>cannabis</i>; fr. chanvre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-za_spa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शष्प</span> <i>śaṣpa</i> <a href="#entry-za_spa" class="skt-link">n.</a> herbe tendre <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> poils pubiens.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-za_tha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शठ</span> <i>śaṭha</i> a. <a href="#entry-za_tha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-za_tha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-za_thaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śaṭhā</i> faux, trompeur; perfide, traître.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">शा</span> <i>śā</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">3</span>] pr. <i>(śiśāti)</i> pr. md. <i>(śiśīte)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>śita</i>, <i>śāta</i><i>)</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] pr. <i>(śyati)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>ni</i><i>)</i> véd. aiguiser <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>catus</i>; ang. hone.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaa_dvala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शाड्वल</span> <i>śāḍvala</i> [<a href="#entry-zaadvala" class="skt-link">śādvala</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zaa_dvala" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zaa_dvala" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zaa_dvalaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śāḍvalā</i> verdoyant, couvert d'herbe — <a href="#entry-zaa_dvala" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zaa_dvala" class="skt-link">n.</a> (sg. ou pl.) prairie — <a href="#entry-zaa_dvala" class="skt-link">m.</a> taureau.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaa_n_dilya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शाण्डिल्य</span> <i>śāṇḍilya</i> <a href="#entry-zaa_n_dilya" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. de Śāṇḍilya, patr. d'un auteur d'aphorismes dévotionnels [<a href="#entry-Ubhaktisuutra" class="skt-link">Bhaktisūtra</a>], de date inconnue.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaa_na_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शाण</span> <i>śāṇa_1</i> [instr. <a href="#entry-zaa" class="skt-link">śā</a>] <a href="#entry-zaa_na_1" class="skt-link">m.</a> pierre de touche; pierre à aiguiser.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaa_na_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शाण</span> <i>śāṇa_2</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-za_na" class="skt-link">śaṇa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zaa_na_2" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zaa_na_2" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zaa_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śāṇā</i> fait de chanvre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaa_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शाट</span> <i>śāṭa</i> <a href="#entry-zaa_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> pièce d'étoffe, robe, jupe; sari — <a href="#entry-zaa_tii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śāṭī</i> id. — <a href="#entry-zaa_ti" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śāṭi</i> id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaa_taka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śāṭaka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-zaa_taka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zaa_taka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zaa_tikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śāṭikā</i> id.<br> <a href="#entry-zaa_ta" class="skt-link">śāṭa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaa_thya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शाठ्य</span> <i>śāṭhya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-za_tha" class="skt-link">śaṭha</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] <a href="#entry-zaa_thya" class="skt-link">n.</a> tromperie; fausseté, fraude, malhonnêteté.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaabara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शाबर</span> <i>śābara</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-zabara" class="skt-link">śabara</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zaabara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zaabara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zaabaraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śābarā</i> méchant <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> relatif à <a href="#entry-Uzabara_1" class="skt-link">Śabara_1</a> — <a href="#entry-zaabara" class="skt-link">m.</a> offense, injure — <a href="#entry-zaabarii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śābarī</i> langue tribale.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaabarabhaa_sya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śābarabhāṣya</i> [<a href="#entry-bhaa_sya" class="skt-link">bhāṣya</a>] <a href="#entry-zaabarabhaa_sya" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Śābarabhāṣya, commentaire <a href="#entry-Umiimaa_msaasuutrabhaa_sya" class="skt-link">Mīmāṃsāsūtrabhāṣya</a> de <a href="#entry-Uzabara_1" class="skt-link">Śabara_1</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-zaabara" class="skt-link">śābara</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaabda">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शाब्द</span> <i>śābda</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-zabda" class="skt-link">śabda</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zaabda" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zaabda" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zaabdii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śābdī</i> sonore <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> concernant le son <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> oral, verbal; reposant sur l'autorité du <a href="#entry-Uveda" class="skt-link">Veda</a> — <a href="#entry-zaabda" class="skt-link">m.</a> grammairien <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. le signifiant (opp. <a href="#entry-aartha" class="skt-link">ārtha</a> le signifié) — <a href="#entry-zaabdii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śābdī</i> myth. np. de Śābdhī «Signifiante», épith. de <a href="#entry-Usarasvatii" class="skt-link">Sarasvatī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaabdabodha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śābdabodha</i> [<a href="#entry-bodha" class="skt-link">bodha</a>] <a href="#entry-zaabdabodha" class="skt-link">m.</a> gram. perception du sens d'une phrase; sémantique; cf. <a href="#entry-abhidhaa_2" class="skt-link">abhidhā_2</a>, <a href="#entry-lak_sa_na" class="skt-link">lakṣaṇa</a>, <a href="#entry-vya_njanaa" class="skt-link">vyañjanā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaabdabodhiiya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śābdabodhīya</i> [<a href="#entry--iiya" class="skt-link">-īya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zaabdabodhiiya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zaabdabodhiiya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zaabdabodhiiyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śābdabodhīyā</i> phil. (sens) obtenu par la compréhension d'une phrase.<br> <a href="#entry-zaabdabodha" class="skt-link">śābdabodha</a> <br> <a href="#entry-zaabda" class="skt-link">śābda</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaabdika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शाब्दिक</span> <i>śābdika</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-zabda" class="skt-link">śabda</a><a href="#entry--ika" class="skt-link">-ika</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zaabdika" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zaabdika" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zaabdikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śābdikā</i> sonore; qui emet un son <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui concerne les sons ou les mots; verbal — <a href="#entry-zaabdika" class="skt-link">m.</a> [«expert en mots»] grammairien, lexicographe.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaabdikaabhara_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śābdikābharaṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-aabhara_na" class="skt-link">ābharaṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-zaabdikaabhara_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> gram. np. du Śābdikābharaṇa, grammaire et lexique de racines [<a href="#entry-Udhaatuv_rtti" class="skt-link">Dhātuvṛtti</a>] dû à <a href="#entry-Udharmakiirti" class="skt-link">Dharmakīrti</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-zaabdika" class="skt-link">śābdika</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaada">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शाद</span> <i>śāda</i> <a href="#entry-zaada" class="skt-link">m.</a> véd. herbe — <a href="#entry-zaadaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śādā</i> brique.<br> <i>śaraśādam</i> du roseau et de l'herbe.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaadvala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शाद्वल</span> <i>śādvala</i> var. <i>śāḍvala</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-zaada" class="skt-link">śāda</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zaadvala" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zaadvala" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zaadvalaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śādvalā</i> verdoyant, couvert d'herbe — <a href="#entry-zaadvala" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zaadvala" class="skt-link">n.</a> (sg. ou pl.) prairie — <a href="#entry-zaadvala" class="skt-link">m.</a> taureau.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaadvalin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śādvalin</i> [<a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zaadvalin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zaadvalin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zaadvalinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śādvalinī</i> couvert d'herbe.<br> <a href="#entry-zaadvala" class="skt-link">śādvala</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaafkaayana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शाङ्कायन</span> <i>śāṅkāyana</i> <a href="#entry-zaafkaayana" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. patr. de Śāṅkāyana «fils de Śaṅka», auteur du <a href="#entry-Ukau_siitaki" class="skt-link">Kauṣītaki</a> <a href="#entry-braahma_na" class="skt-link">brāhmaṇa</a> et de deux formulaires de rituel [<a href="#entry-suutra" class="skt-link">sūtra</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaafkaayanaara_nyaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śāṅkāyanāraṇyaka</i> [<a href="#entry-aara_nyaka" class="skt-link">āraṇyaka</a>] <a href="#entry-zaafkaayanaara_nyaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Śāṅkāyanāraṇyaka.<br> <a href="#entry-zaafkaayana" class="skt-link">śāṅkāyana</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaafkaayanabraahma_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śāṅkāyanabrāhmaṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-braahma_na" class="skt-link">brāhmaṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-zaafkaayanabraahma_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Śāṅkāyanabrāhmaṇa, épith. du <a href="#entry-Ukau_siitaki" class="skt-link">Kauṣītaki</a> <a href="#entry-braahma_na" class="skt-link">brāhmaṇa</a> «dû à <a href="#entry-Uzaafkaayana" class="skt-link">Śāṅkāyana</a>».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaafkara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शांकर</span> <i>śāṃkara</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-zafkara" class="skt-link">śaṃkara</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zaa_mkara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zaa_mkara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zaa_mkaraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śāṃkarā</i> relatif à <a href="#entry-Uzafkara" class="skt-link">Śaṅkara</a>-<a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> relatif à <a href="#entry-Uzafkaraacaarya" class="skt-link">Śaṅkarācārya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaaha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शाह</span> <i>śāha</i> <a href="#entry-zaaha" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. np. du Śāha, région du <a href="#entry-Ukazmiira" class="skt-link">Kaśmīra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaahi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शाहि</span> <i>śāhi</i> [<a href="#entry-zaaha" class="skt-link">śāha</a>] <a href="#entry-zaahi" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. de la dynastie Śāhi du <a href="#entry-Ukazmiira" class="skt-link">Kaśmīra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaaka_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शाकट</span> <i>śākaṭa</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-zaka_ta" class="skt-link">śakaṭa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zaaka_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zaaka_ta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zaaka_tii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śākaṭī</i> relatif au chariot, à la charrette <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui va dans un chariot; qui tire un chariot; qui remplit un chariot — <a href="#entry-zaaka_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> animal de trait; charretée.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaaka_taayana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śākaṭāyana</i> [<a href="#entry-aayana" class="skt-link">āyana</a>] <a href="#entry-zaaka_taayana" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du grammarien antique Śākaṭāyana, mentionné par <a href="#entry-Upaa_nini" class="skt-link">Pāṇini</a>; certains lui attribuent les <a href="#entry-u_naadisuutraa_ni" class="skt-link">uṇādisūtrāṇi</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. patr. du grammairien jaïne Śākaṭāyana_1; il vivait à la cour <a href="#entry-Uraa_s_trakuu_ta" class="skt-link">Rāṣṭrakūṭa</a> à la fin du 9⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle; il écrivit la grammaire <a href="#entry-Uzabdaanuzaasana" class="skt-link">Śabdānuśāsana</a>; il professait que tout substantif dérive d'une racine [<a href="#entry-vyutpattipak_sa" class="skt-link">vyutpattipakṣa</a>].<br> <a href="#entry-zaaka_ta" class="skt-link">śākaṭa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaaka_tika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शाकटिक</span> <i>śākaṭika</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-zaka_ta" class="skt-link">śakaṭa</a><a href="#entry--ika" class="skt-link">-ika</a>] <a href="#entry-zaaka_tika" class="skt-link">m.</a> charretier.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaaka_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शाक</span> <i>śāka_1</i> <a href="#entry-zaaka_1" class="skt-link">n.</a> légume, végétal — <a href="#entry-zaaka_1" class="skt-link">m.</a> [<i>śākavṛkṣa</i>] natu. bot. <i>Tectona grandis</i>, arbre teck <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> [<i>śākadvīpa</i>] myth. l'un des 7 continents [<a href="#entry-dviipa" class="skt-link">dvīpa</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaaka_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शाक</span> <i>śāka_2</i> [act. <a href="#entry-zak" class="skt-link">śak</a>] <a href="#entry-zaaka_2" class="skt-link">m.</a> puissance, force; secours <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> aide, ami.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaaka_3">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शाक</span> <i>śāka_3</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-zaka" class="skt-link">śaka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zaaka_3" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zaaka_3" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zaakaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śākā</i> relatif aux <a href="#entry-Uzakaas" class="skt-link">Śakās</a> — <a href="#entry-zaaka_3" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zaaka_3" class="skt-link">n.</a> cf. <a href="#entry-zaakakaala" class="skt-link">śākakāla</a> — <a href="#entry-zaaka_3" class="skt-link">m.</a> pl. peuple des Śākās.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaakabhak_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śākabhakṣa</i> [<a href="#entry-bhak_sa" class="skt-link">bhakṣa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zaakabhak_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zaakabhak_sa" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zaakabhak_saa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śākabhakṣā</i> végétarien.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaakadviipa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śākadvīpa</i> [<a href="#entry-dviipa" class="skt-link">dvīpa</a>] <a href="#entry-zaakadviipa" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. du Śākadvīpa «Continent du teck», entouré d'un océan de lait.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaakadviipiiya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śākadvīpīya</i> [<a href="#entry--iiya" class="skt-link">-īya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zaakadviipiiya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zaakadviipiiya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zaakadviipiiyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śākadvīpīyā</i> myth. appartenant au <a href="#entry-Uzaakadviipa" class="skt-link">Śākadvīpa</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. qualifie une classe de brâhmanes d'origine Scythe [<a href="#entry-zaka" class="skt-link">śaka</a>].<br> <a href="#entry-zaakadviipa" class="skt-link">śākadvīpa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaakakaala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śākakāla</i> [<a href="#entry-kaala_1" class="skt-link">kāla_1</a>] <a href="#entry-zaakakaala" class="skt-link">m.</a> ère des <a href="#entry-Uzakaas" class="skt-link">Śakās</a>, commençant en l'an 78<br> <a href="#entry-zaaka_3" class="skt-link">śāka_3</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaakala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शाकल</span> <i>śākala</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-zakala" class="skt-link">śakala</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zaakala" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zaakala" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zaakalaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śākalā</i> teint avec du <a href="#entry-zakala" class="skt-link">śakala</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> concernant une portion, un éclat, etc. <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> habitant de <a href="#entry-Uzaakala" class="skt-link">Śākala</a>; qui suit <a href="#entry-Uzaakalya" class="skt-link">Śākalya</a> — <a href="#entry-zaakala" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zaakala" class="skt-link">n.</a> morceau, portion; fragment, éclat, tesson; écaille — <a href="#entry-zaakala" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. texte de <a href="#entry-Uzaakalya" class="skt-link">Śākalya</a> — <a href="#entry-zaakala" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. np. de la ville de Śākala, ville du pays <a href="#entry-Umadra" class="skt-link">Madra</a> (mod. Sialkot au Pañjāb du Pakistan), capitale du roi <a href="#entry-Umilinda" class="skt-link">Milinda</a>; sa déesse tutélaire était <a href="#entry-Usaavitrii" class="skt-link">Sāvitrī</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. <i>śākalās</i> les habitants de <a href="#entry-Uzaakala" class="skt-link">Śākala</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaakalasa_mhitaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śākalasaṃhitā</i> [<a href="#entry-sa_mhitaa" class="skt-link">saṃhitā</a>] <a href="#entry-zaakalasa_mhitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de la Śākalasaṃhitā, compilation du <a href="#entry-U_rgveda" class="skt-link">Ṛgveda</a> due à <a href="#entry-Uzaakalya" class="skt-link">Śākalya</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-zaakala" class="skt-link">śākala</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaakalya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शाकल्य</span> <i>śākalya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-zakala" class="skt-link">śakala</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] <a href="#entry-zaakalya" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. patr. de l'Érudit [<a href="#entry-Uvidagdha" class="skt-link">Vidagdha</a>] Śākalya, grammairien qui vivait au nord du <a href="#entry-Ubihaara" class="skt-link">Bihāra</a> vers 800 ant.; premier compilateur du <a href="#entry-U_rgveda" class="skt-link">Ṛgveda</a>, il en fixa le texte segmenté [<a href="#entry-Upadapaa_tha" class="skt-link">Padapāṭha</a>]; sa recension comprend l'<a href="#entry-Uaitareya" class="skt-link">Aitareya</a> <a href="#entry-braahma_na" class="skt-link">brāhmaṇa</a>; l'autre recension [<a href="#entry-zaakhaa" class="skt-link">śākhā</a>] est due à <a href="#entry-Ubaa_skala" class="skt-link">Bāṣkala</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaakam">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शाकम्</span> <i>śākam</i> [acc. <a href="#entry-zaaka_1" class="skt-link">śāka_1</a>] iic.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaakambharii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śākaṃbharī</i> var. <i>śākambharī</i> [<a href="#entry-bhara" class="skt-link">bhara</a>] <a href="#entry-zaaka_mbharii" class="skt-link">f.</a> myth. np. de Śākaṃbharī «la Potagère», épith. de <a href="#entry-Udurgaa" class="skt-link">Durgā</a> qui fit pousser des légumes de son propre corps pour sauver le monde d'une sécheresse de 100 ans; [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] son sanctuaire se trouve près du gué Triśulakhāta <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> géo. capitale des princes <a href="#entry-caahuvaa_na" class="skt-link">cāhuvāṇa</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-zaakam" class="skt-link">śākam</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaakapaarthiva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śākapārthiva</i> [<a href="#entry-paarthiva" class="skt-link">pārthiva</a>] <a href="#entry-zaakapaarthiva" class="skt-link">m.</a> roi végétarien.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaakaprati">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śākaprati</i> [<a href="#entry-prati" class="skt-link">prati</a>] adv. un petit peu de légumes.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaakhaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शाखा</span> <i>śākhā</i> <a href="#entry-zaakhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> rameau, branche, division; espèce; extrémité, doigt <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> véd. école rituelle, secte; édition d'un texte sectaire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaakhaam_rga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śākhāmṛga</i> [<a href="#entry-m_rga" class="skt-link">mṛga</a>] <a href="#entry-zaakhaam_rga" class="skt-link">m.</a> singe [«antilope de branche»].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaakhin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śākhin</i> [<a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] <a href="#entry-zaakhin" class="skt-link">m.</a> arbre [«qui a des branches»].<br> <a href="#entry-zaakhaa" class="skt-link">śākhā</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaakika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śākika</i> [<a href="#entry--ika" class="skt-link">-ika</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zaakika" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zaakika" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zaakikii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śākikī</i> qui ramasse des légumes.<br> <a href="#entry-zaaka_1" class="skt-link">śāka_1</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaakin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शाकिन्</span> <i>śākin</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-zak" class="skt-link">śak</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zaakin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zaakin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zaakinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śākinī</i> puissant — <a href="#entry-zaakinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śākinī</i> myth. [tantr.] démon femelle assistant <a href="#entry-Udurgaa" class="skt-link">Durgā</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-haakinii" class="skt-link">hākinī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaakta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शाक्त</span> <i>śākta</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-zakta" class="skt-link">śakta</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zaakta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zaakta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zaaktaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śāktā</i> relatif au pouvoir ou à l'énergie <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. relatif à la puissance divine, à la Désse [<a href="#entry-Uzakti" class="skt-link">Śakti</a>] — <a href="#entry-zaakta" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. [tantr.] sectateur de la Déesse, adepte du tantrisme, qui suit les enseignements des <a href="#entry-tantra" class="skt-link">tantra</a>; on distingue ceux qui suivent le rituel orthodoxe ou droit [<a href="#entry-dak_si_naacaara" class="skt-link">dakṣiṇācāra</a>] de ceux qui suivent le rituel impur ou gauche [<a href="#entry-vaamaacaara" class="skt-link">vāmācāra</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaakuntala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शाकुन्तल</span> <i>śākuntala</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-zakuntalaa" class="skt-link">śakuntalā</a>] <a href="#entry-zaakuntala" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Śākuntala, drame de <a href="#entry-Ukaalidaasa" class="skt-link">Kālidāsa</a>, aussi appelé <a href="#entry-Uabhij_naanazakuntalaa" class="skt-link">Abhijñānaśakuntalā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaakya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शाक्य</span> <i>śākya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-zaka" class="skt-link">śaka</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zaakya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zaakya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zaakyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śākyā</i> qui concerne le peuple des <a href="#entry-Uzakaas" class="skt-link">Śakās</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. np. de famille identifiant l'origine Indo-Scythe [<a href="#entry-zaka" class="skt-link">śaka</a>]; il était assimilé à la caste [<a href="#entry-var_na" class="skt-link">varṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-k_satriya" class="skt-link">kṣatriya</a>; la tradition dit d'un prince <a href="#entry-zaakya" class="skt-link">śākya</a> qu'il descend d'<a href="#entry-Uik_svaaku" class="skt-link">Ikṣvāku</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> bd. np. de famille du <a href="#entry-Ubuddha" class="skt-link">Buddha</a>; son clan [<a href="#entry-gotra" class="skt-link">gotra</a>] est <a href="#entry-Ugautama" class="skt-link">Gautama</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pali <i>sākiya</i>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaakyabhik_su">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śākyabhikṣu</i> [<a href="#entry-bhik_su" class="skt-link">bhikṣu</a>] <a href="#entry-zaakyabhik_su" class="skt-link">m.</a> bd. moine bouddhiste.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaakyabhik_suka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śākyabhikṣuka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-zaakyabhik_suka" class="skt-link">m.</a> bd. moine bouddhiste — <a href="#entry-zaakyabhik_sukii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śākyabhikṣukī</i> bd. nonne bouddhiste <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. (en lit.) entremetteuse.<br> <a href="#entry-zaakyabhik_su" class="skt-link">śākyabhikṣu</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaakyamuni">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śākyamuni</i> [<a href="#entry-muni" class="skt-link">muni</a>] <a href="#entry-zaakyamuni" class="skt-link">m.</a> bd. np. de Śākyamuni, épith. du <a href="#entry-Ubuddha" class="skt-link">Buddha</a> «Sage des <a href="#entry-Uzakaas" class="skt-link">Śakās</a>».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaakyasi_mha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śākyasiṃha</i> [<a href="#entry-si_mha" class="skt-link">siṃha</a>] <a href="#entry-zaakyasi_mha" class="skt-link">m.</a> bd. np. de Śākyasiṃha, épith. du <a href="#entry-Ubuddha" class="skt-link">Buddha</a> «Lion parmi les <a href="#entry-Uzakaas" class="skt-link">Śakās</a>».<br> <a href="#entry-zaakya" class="skt-link">śākya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शाल</span> <i>śāla</i> a. <a href="#entry-zaala" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zaala" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zaalaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śālā</i> qui reste chez soi — <a href="#entry-zaala" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. bot. <i>Shorea robusta</i> ou Śāl, grand arbre utilisé en charpenterie; sa résine [<a href="#entry-raala" class="skt-link">rāla</a>] est utilisée comme laque, pour l'encens, et pour la médecine; de son amande on extrait le beurre d'illipé utilisé en cosmétique; ses feuilles servent à faire des assiettes jetables; le <a href="#entry-zaala" class="skt-link">śāla</a> est sacré à <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a>; bd. c'est contre cet arbre que la mère de <a href="#entry-Ubuddha" class="skt-link">Buddha</a> s'appuya pour lui donner naissance, et c'est sous cet arbre qu'il passa en <a href="#entry-parinirvaa_na" class="skt-link">parinirvāṇa</a> — f. cf. <a href="#entry-zaalaa" class="skt-link">śālā</a>.<br> <i>śālam</i> adv. à la maison; chez soi.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaalaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शाला</span> <i>śālā</i> [f. <a href="#entry-zaala" class="skt-link">śāla</a>] <a href="#entry-zaalaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> hutte, maison; étable <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pièce, salle; salle d'étude — <a href="#entry-zaala" class="skt-link">n.</a> <i>śāla</i> ifc. maison de <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> fr. salle.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaalaakya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शालाक्य</span> <i>śālākya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-zalaakaa" class="skt-link">śalākā</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] <a href="#entry-zaalaakya" class="skt-link">m.</a> méd. chirurgien; not. oculiste — <a href="#entry-zaalaakya" class="skt-link">n.</a> méd. la chirurgie de l'œil et de l'oreille, une section de l'<a href="#entry-Uaayurveda" class="skt-link">Āyurveda</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaalaav_rka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śālāvṛka</i> [<a href="#entry-v_rka" class="skt-link">vṛka</a>] <a href="#entry-zaalaav_rka" class="skt-link">m.</a> chacal [«loup d'étable»].<br> <a href="#entry-zaalaa" class="skt-link">śālā</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaalabha_njikaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śālabhañjikā</i> [<a href="#entry-bha_njikaa" class="skt-link">bhañjikā</a>] <a href="#entry-zaalabha_njikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. femme ou nymphe sylvestre [<a href="#entry-yak_sii" class="skt-link">yakṣī</a>] d'une telle beauté qu'elle fait fleurir un arbre qu'elle touche du pied; en sculpture on la représente déhanchée devant un arbre <a href="#entry-zaala" class="skt-link">śāla</a>, touchant de la main un rameau fleuri <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> courtisane.<br> <a href="#entry-zaala" class="skt-link">śāla</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaalafkaayana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शालङ्कायन</span> <i>śālaṅkāyana</i> <a href="#entry-zaalafkaayana" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. patr. du <a href="#entry-_r_si" class="skt-link">ṛṣi</a> Śālaṅkāyana ou <a href="#entry-Usaalafkaayana" class="skt-link">Sālaṅkāyana</a>, fils de <a href="#entry-Uvizvaamitra" class="skt-link">Viśvāmitra</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. nom de <a href="#entry-gotra" class="skt-link">gotra</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. hist. np. de la dynastie <a href="#entry-braahma_na" class="skt-link">brāhmaṇa</a> des Śālaṅkāyanās, qui régnèrent en <a href="#entry-Uandhra" class="skt-link">Andhra</a> sur la région de <a href="#entry-Uvefgii" class="skt-link">Veṅgī</a> de 300 à 440; ils étaient vassaux des <a href="#entry-Upallavaas" class="skt-link">Pallavās</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaalagraama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śālagrāma</i> [<a href="#entry-graama" class="skt-link">grāma</a>] <a href="#entry-zaalagraama" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. np. de Śālagrāma, village sacré aux dévots de <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a> [<a href="#entry-vai_s_nava" class="skt-link">vaiṣṇava</a>], situé sur la rivière Gaṇḍakī <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. (provenant souvent de <a href="#entry-Uzaalagraama" class="skt-link">Śālagrāma</a>) coquille d'ammonite fossile, utilisée comme image dévotionnelle du <a href="#entry-cakra" class="skt-link">cakra</a> de <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a>; on lui attribue des vertus magiques; cf. <a href="#entry-tulasii" class="skt-link">tulasī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaalapar_nii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śālaparṇī</i> [<a href="#entry-par_nii" class="skt-link">parṇī</a>] <a href="#entry-zaalapar_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> natu. bot. <i>Pseudarthria viscida</i>, arbuste de la famille des papillionacées, trifoliée, aux fleurs roses; c'est l'une des 5 racines médicinales mineures [<a href="#entry-hrasvapa_ncamuula" class="skt-link">hrasvapañcamūla</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaali">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शालि</span> <i>śāli</i> <a href="#entry-zaali" class="skt-link">m.</a> riz <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. <i>śālayas</i> grains de riz <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. du <a href="#entry-yak_sa" class="skt-link">yakṣa</a> Śāli.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaalicuur_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śālicūrṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-cuur_na" class="skt-link">cūrṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-zaalicuur_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> farine de riz.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaalihotra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śālihotra</i> [<a href="#entry-hotra" class="skt-link">hotra</a>] <a href="#entry-zaalihotra" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. «qui reçoit le riz en offrande», nom de cheval en poésie <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. de Śālihotra, auteur antique d'un traité vétérinaire éponyme; il vécut à <a href="#entry-Uzraavastii" class="skt-link">Śrāvastī</a> — <a href="#entry-zaalihotra" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Śālihotram, son œuvre; cf. <a href="#entry-Uzaalihotrasa_mhitaa" class="skt-link">Śālihotrasaṃhitā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaalihotrasa_mhitaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śālihotrasaṃhitā</i> [<a href="#entry-sa_mhitaa" class="skt-link">saṃhitā</a>] <a href="#entry-zaalihotrasa_mhitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de la Śālihotrasaṃhitā, ouvrage vétérinaire de <a href="#entry-Uzaalihotra" class="skt-link">Śālihotra</a>; il traite not. des chevaux et des éléphants.<br> <a href="#entry-zaalihotra" class="skt-link">śālihotra</a> <br> <a href="#entry-zaali" class="skt-link">śāli</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaalin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शालिन्</span> <i>śālin</i> [<a href="#entry-zaalaa" class="skt-link">śālā</a><a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zaalin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zaalin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zaalinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śālinī</i> qui possède une maison, propriétaire <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> sédentaire (opp. <a href="#entry-yaayaavara" class="skt-link">yāyāvara</a>) — ifc. qui possède; plein de, doué de, bien muni de.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaalivaahana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śālivāhana</i> [<a href="#entry-vaahana" class="skt-link">vāhana</a>] <a href="#entry-zaalivaahana" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. titre Śālivāhana «qui a <a href="#entry-Uzaali" class="skt-link">Śāli</a> comme monture» du roi indo-scythe [<a href="#entry-zaka" class="skt-link">śaka</a>] <a href="#entry-Ugautamiiputra" class="skt-link">Gautamīputra</a> <a href="#entry-Uzaatakar_nii" class="skt-link">Śātakarṇī</a> (78–102); il était <a href="#entry-k_satrapa" class="skt-link">kṣatrapa</a> d'<a href="#entry-Uujjayinii" class="skt-link">Ujjayinī</a>; il fonda l'ère <a href="#entry-Uzaka" class="skt-link">Śaka</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. <i>śālivāhana</i> hist. np. du peuple et de la dynastie <a href="#entry-Uandhra" class="skt-link">Andhra</a> des Śālivāhanās; cf. <a href="#entry-Usaatavaahanaas" class="skt-link">Sātavāhanās</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaalmala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शाल्मल</span> <i>śālmala</i> <a href="#entry-zaalmala" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. bot. <i>Bombax heptaphyllum</i> ou <i>Salmalia malabarica</i>, cotonnier ou arbre à kapok, à fleurs rouges; on dit que les épines de son tronc sont utilisées pour torturer les damnés en enfer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. l'un des 7 continents [<a href="#entry-dviipa" class="skt-link">dvīpa</a>] — <a href="#entry-zaalmalii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śālmalī</i> id. <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de la Śālmalī, une des rivières de l'enfer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> var. <i>śālmali</i> id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaalva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शाल्व</span> <i>śālva</i> <a href="#entry-zaalva" class="skt-link">m.</a> pl. nom du peuple des Śālvās (à l'ouest des Aravalli) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. de Śālva, roi de <a href="#entry-Usaubha" class="skt-link">Saubha</a>, qui s'opposa sans succès au rapt par <a href="#entry-Ubhii_sma" class="skt-link">Bhīṣma</a> des filles du roi de <a href="#entry-Ukaazi" class="skt-link">Kāśi</a> qu'il devait épouser; <a href="#entry-Ubhii_sma" class="skt-link">Bhīṣma</a> lui rendit l'aînée <a href="#entry-Uambaa" class="skt-link">Ambā</a> qui s'était promise à lui, mais il la répudia; [BhP.] <a href="#entry-Uzaalva" class="skt-link">Śālva</a>, ami de <a href="#entry-Uzizupaala" class="skt-link">Śiśupāla</a>, fit le serment à la mort de celui-ci d'éliminer les <a href="#entry-Uyaadavaas" class="skt-link">Yādavās</a>; par ses austérités il obtint de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> la construction par <a href="#entry-Umaya" class="skt-link">Maya</a> d'une indestructible citadelle de fer magique; il attaqua <a href="#entry-Udvaarakaa" class="skt-link">Dvārakā</a>, défendue par <a href="#entry-Upradyumna" class="skt-link">Pradyumna</a>, mais seul <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a> put le tuer avec son <a href="#entry-cakra" class="skt-link">cakra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaalveya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शाल्वेय</span> <i>śālveya</i> [<a href="#entry-zaalva" class="skt-link">śālva</a><a href="#entry--iiya" class="skt-link">-īya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zaalveya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zaalveya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zaalveyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śālveyā</i> pl. géo. np. des Śālveyās, peuple des <a href="#entry-Uzaalvaas" class="skt-link">Śālvās</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> leur roi.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaam">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शाम्</span> <i>śām</i> [ca. <a href="#entry-zam_2" class="skt-link">śam_2</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(śāmayati)</i> observer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaamba">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शाम्ब</span> <i>śāmba</i> cf. <a href="#entry-saamba" class="skt-link">sāmba</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaambhava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शाम्भव</span> <i>śāmbhava</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-zambhu" class="skt-link">śambhu</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zaambhava" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zaambhava" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zaambhavii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śāmbhavī</i> myth. concernant ou dérivant de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>; sacré à <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaamitra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शामित्र</span> <i>śāmitra</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-zamit_r" class="skt-link">śamitṛ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zaamitra" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zaamitra" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zaamitraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śāmitrā</i> relatif au sacrificateur — <a href="#entry-zaamitra" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. feu du sacrifice — <a href="#entry-zaamitra" class="skt-link">n.</a> son emplacement <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> abattoir; lieu d'exécution.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaanac">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शानच्</span> <i>śānac</i> nota. gram. affixe primaire [<a href="#entry-k_rt_2" class="skt-link">kṛt_2</a>] marquant le participe présent moyen.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaanta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शान्त</span> <i>śānta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-zam_1" class="skt-link">śam_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zaanta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zaanta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zaantaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śāntā</i> calme, apaisé, tranquille <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gentil, propice <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> inoffensif; mort — <a href="#entry-zaanta" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. le paisible, un des 9 modes artistiques [<a href="#entry-rasa" class="skt-link">rasa</a>]; on lui associe la couleur jasmin et la divinité <a href="#entry-Unaaraaya_na" class="skt-link">Nārāyaṇa</a> — <a href="#entry-zaantaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śāntā</i> myth. [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] np. de Śāntā «Paisible», fille du roi <a href="#entry-Uromapaada" class="skt-link">Romapāda</a>; elle épousa l'ascète <a href="#entry-U_rzyaz_rfga" class="skt-link">Ṛśyaśṛṅga</a> — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. <i>(śāntayati)</i> apaiser.<br> <i>śāntam</i> interj. paix! du calme! tais toi! <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> à Dieu ne plaise (se dit pour écarter un mauvais présage).<br> <i>śāntaṃ</i> <i>pāpam</i> <i>vade retro, satanas</i>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaantaatman">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śāntātman</i> [<a href="#entry-aatman" class="skt-link">ātman</a>] <a href="#entry-zaantaatman" class="skt-link">n.</a> phil. [KU.] le vrai soi en paix.<br> <a href="#entry-zaanta" class="skt-link">śānta</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaantanu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शांतनु</span> <i>śāṃtanu</i> var. <i>śāntanu</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-zam_3" class="skt-link">śam_3</a>-<a href="#entry-tanuu" class="skt-link">tanū</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zaa_mtanu" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zaa_mtanu" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zaa_mtanu" class="skt-link">f.</a> salubre, sain pour le corps — <a href="#entry-zaa_mtanu" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. du roi mythique Śāṃtanu ou <a href="#entry-Uzaantanu" class="skt-link">Śāntanu</a> «Bien-portant», 21⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ roi de la lignée lunaire [<a href="#entry-somava_mza" class="skt-link">somavaṃśa</a>], descendant de <a href="#entry-Ubharata" class="skt-link">Bharata</a> par <a href="#entry-Ukuru" class="skt-link">Kuru</a> et fils de <a href="#entry-Upratiipa" class="skt-link">Pratīpa</a>; il usurpa le trône de son frère aîné <a href="#entry-Udevaapi" class="skt-link">Devāpi</a> quand celui-ci se fit ermite; par la déesse <a href="#entry-Ugafgaa" class="skt-link">Gaṅgā</a> il engendra <a href="#entry-Udevavrata" class="skt-link">Devavrata</a>, le futur <a href="#entry-Ubhii_sma" class="skt-link">Bhīṣma</a>, patriarche du <a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mahābhārata</a>; il tomba amoureux de la belle <a href="#entry-Usatyavatii" class="skt-link">Satyavatī</a>, dont le père adoptif exigea qu'il lui promît la descendance royale, et qui engendra de lui <a href="#entry-Ucitraafgada" class="skt-link">Citrāṅgada</a> et <a href="#entry-Uvicitraviirya" class="skt-link">Vicitravīrya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaantarak_sita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śāntarakṣita</i> [<a href="#entry-rak_sita" class="skt-link">rakṣita</a>] <a href="#entry-zaantarak_sita" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du <a href="#entry-pa_n_dita" class="skt-link">paṇḍita</a> bd. Śāntarakṣita (8⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle); on lui doit le compendium philosophique <a href="#entry-Utattvasafgraha" class="skt-link">Tattvasaṃgraha</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaantarasa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śāntarasa</i> [<a href="#entry-rasa" class="skt-link">rasa</a>] <a href="#entry-zaantarasa" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. sentiment de paix; [<a href="#entry-Uabhinavagupta" class="skt-link">Abhinavagupta</a>] le paisible comme expérience esthétique libératoire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaanti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शान्ति</span> <i>śānti</i> [act. <a href="#entry-zam_1" class="skt-link">śam_1</a>] <a href="#entry-zaanti" class="skt-link">f.</a> paix, calme, repos; paix du cœur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> prospérité, bénédiction, bonheur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. rite propritiatoire préalable à un sacrifice; rite d'apaisement d'un démon de possession; pouvoir [<a href="#entry-siddhi" class="skt-link">siddhi</a>] d'élimination des dangers <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> destruction, fin, mort; repos éternel <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-zaivasiddhaanta" class="skt-link">śaivasiddhānta</a>] aspect paisible du <a href="#entry-bindu" class="skt-link">bindu</a>, associé à <a href="#entry-Usadaaziva" class="skt-link">Sadāśiva</a>-<a href="#entry-Utatpuru_sa" class="skt-link">Tatpuruṣa</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Śānti «Paix», fille de <a href="#entry-Udak_sa" class="skt-link">Dakṣa</a>, épouse de <a href="#entry-Udharma" class="skt-link">Dharma</a> — <a href="#entry-zaanti" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de l'<a href="#entry-afgiras" class="skt-link">aṅgiras</a> Śānti_1 «Paisible», fils d'<a href="#entry-Uafgiraa" class="skt-link">Aṅgirā</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de nombreux autres personnages.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaantideva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śāntideva</i> [<a href="#entry-deva" class="skt-link">deva</a>] <a href="#entry-zaantideva" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du philosophe et poète Śāntideva (~685–763), bd. d'obédience <a href="#entry-maadhyamika" class="skt-link">mādhyamika</a>; selon la tradition, ayant reçu l'apparition de <a href="#entry-Uma_njuzrii" class="skt-link">Mañjuśrī</a>, il renonça à son destin princier, s'enfuit de son palais et reçut l'ordination à <a href="#entry-Unaalandaa" class="skt-link">Nālandā</a>; on lui doit le <a href="#entry-Ubodhicaryaavataara" class="skt-link">Bodhicaryāvatāra</a> et le <a href="#entry-Uzik_saasamuccaya" class="skt-link">Śikṣāsamuccaya</a>; il est particulièrement révéré au Tibet.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaantinaatha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śāntinātha</i> [<a href="#entry-naatha" class="skt-link">nātha</a>] <a href="#entry-zaantinaatha" class="skt-link">m.</a> jn. np. de Śāntinātha «Maître de la Paix», 16⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ <a href="#entry-tiirthafkara" class="skt-link">tīrthaṅkara</a> mythique; son corps est jaune d'or, son symbole est un daim ou une tortue.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaantiparvan">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śāntiparvan</i> [<a href="#entry-parvan" class="skt-link">parvan</a>] <a href="#entry-zaantiparvan" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. 12⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ livre du <a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>, «de la pacification».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaantivacana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śāntivacana</i> [<a href="#entry-vacana" class="skt-link">vacana</a>] <a href="#entry-zaantivacana" class="skt-link">n.</a> invocation d'apaisement, placée en tête d'un ouvrage.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaantva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शान्त्व</span> <i>śāntva</i> [<a href="#entry-zam_1" class="skt-link">śam_1</a><a href="#entry--tva" class="skt-link">-tva</a>] <a href="#entry-zaantva" class="skt-link">n.</a> consolation, réconfort <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. <i>śāntvāni</i> paroles apaisantes, bonnes paroles.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaantyatiita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śāntyatīta</i> [<a href="#entry-atiita" class="skt-link">atīta</a>] <a href="#entry-zaantyatiita" class="skt-link">n.</a> phil. [<a href="#entry-zaivasiddhaanta" class="skt-link">śaivasiddhānta</a>] principe [<a href="#entry-tattva" class="skt-link">tattva</a>] transcendant, aspect suprême du <a href="#entry-bindu" class="skt-link">bindu</a> associé à <a href="#entry-Usadaaziva" class="skt-link">Sadāśiva</a>-<a href="#entry-Uiizaana" class="skt-link">Īśāna</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaantyudaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śāntyudaka</i> [<a href="#entry-udaka" class="skt-link">udaka</a>] <a href="#entry-zaantyudaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> soc. eau consacrée, utilisée dans le rite <a href="#entry-zaanti" class="skt-link">śānti</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaantyudakavidhi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śāntyudakavidhi</i> [<a href="#entry-vidhi" class="skt-link">vidhi</a>] <a href="#entry-zaantyudakavidhi" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. [Atharvaveda] prescriptions concernant l'eau du rite <a href="#entry-zaanti" class="skt-link">śānti</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-zaantyudaka" class="skt-link">śāntyudaka</a> <br> <a href="#entry-zaanti" class="skt-link">śānti</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaap">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शाप्</span> <i>śāp</i> [ca. <a href="#entry-zap" class="skt-link">śap</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(śāpayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>śāpita</i><i>)</i> faire jurer par ⟨i.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaapa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शाप</span> <i>śāpa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-zaap" class="skt-link">śāp</a>] <a href="#entry-zaapa" class="skt-link">m.</a> malédiction, imprécation, anathème.<br> <i>śāpaṃ</i> <i>dā</i> proférer une malédiction contre ⟨loc. g. dat. <a href="#entry-prati" class="skt-link">prati</a>-acc.⟩.<br> <i>śāpaṃ</i> <i>vac</i> id.<br> <i>kubera</i> <i>śāpena</i> adv. par la malédiction de <a href="#entry-Ukubera" class="skt-link">Kubera</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaapamok_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śāpamokṣa</i> [<a href="#entry-mok_sa" class="skt-link">mokṣa</a>] <a href="#entry-zaapamok_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> délivrance d'une malédiction.<br> <a href="#entry-zaapa" class="skt-link">śāpa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaapita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शापित</span> <i>śāpita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-zaap" class="skt-link">śāp</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zaapita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zaapita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zaapitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śāpitā</i> que l'on a fait jurer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शार</span> <i>śāra</i> <a href="#entry-zaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> pièce, pion (au jeu de dés).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaarada">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शारद</span> <i>śārada</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-zarad" class="skt-link">śarad</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zaarada" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zaarada" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zaaradii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śāradī</i> automnal; mûr <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> timide, modeste — <a href="#entry-zaarada" class="skt-link">m.</a> année; nuage — <a href="#entry-zaarada" class="skt-link">n.</a> grain, fruit (produits d'automne) — f. cf. <a href="#entry-zaaradaa" class="skt-link">śāradā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaaradaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शारदा</span> <i>śāradā</i> [f. <a href="#entry-zaarada" class="skt-link">śārada</a>] <a href="#entry-zaaradaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> sorte de luth [<a href="#entry-vii_naa" class="skt-link">vīṇā</a>] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. écriture utilisée au <a href="#entry-Ukazmiira" class="skt-link">Kaśmīra</a> à partir du 8⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle, évoluée de l'écriture <a href="#entry-braahmii" class="skt-link">brāhmī</a>; l'écriture <a href="#entry-gurmukhii" class="skt-link">gurmukhī</a> en est issue <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Śāradā «Luth», incarnation de <a href="#entry-Usarasvatii" class="skt-link">Sarasvatī</a>, représentant la connaissance et la joie; elle est vénérée not. à <a href="#entry-Uz_rfgerii" class="skt-link">Śṛṅgerī</a>; dans son sanctuaire du <a href="#entry-Ukazmiira" class="skt-link">Kaśmīra</a> elle avait une idole miraculeuse qui fut détruite par Zain-ul-<i>ābidīn</i> pour lui avoir refusé son <a href="#entry-darzana" class="skt-link">darśana</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> géo. np. d'une importante université ancienne du <a href="#entry-Ukazmiira" class="skt-link">Kaśmīra</a>, située dans la vallée de la rivière Neelum.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaaradaatanaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śāradātanaya</i> [<a href="#entry-tanaya" class="skt-link">tanaya</a>] <a href="#entry-zaaradaatanaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. de Śāradātanaya «Fils de <a href="#entry-Uzaaradaa" class="skt-link">Śāradā</a>», originaire d'Inde du Nord (entre le 12⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ et le 14⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècles); il est l'auteur du <a href="#entry-Ubhaavaprakaaza" class="skt-link">Bhāvaprakāśa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaaradaatilaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śāradātilaka</i> [<a href="#entry-tilaka" class="skt-link">tilaka</a>] <a href="#entry-zaaradaatilaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Śāradātilaka, texte <a href="#entry-kaula" class="skt-link">kaula</a> tardif (15⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle).<br> <a href="#entry-zaaradaa" class="skt-link">śāradā</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaaradvata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शारद्वत</span> <i>śāradvata</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-zaradvat" class="skt-link">śaradvat</a>] <a href="#entry-zaaradvata" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Śāradvata, disciple de <a href="#entry-Uka_nva" class="skt-link">Kaṇva</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaarduula">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शार्दूल</span> <i>śārdūla</i> <a href="#entry-zaarduula" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. zoo. tigre — ifc. le meilleur parmi — <a href="#entry-zaarduulii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śārdūlī</i> natu. tigresse <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Śārdūlī, fille de <a href="#entry-Ukazyapa" class="skt-link">Kaśyapa</a> et <a href="#entry-Ukrodhavazaa" class="skt-link">Krodhavaśā</a>; elle engendra la race des tigres.<br> <i>muniśārdūla</i> meilleur parmi les sages.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaarduulakar_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śārdūlakarṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-kar_na" class="skt-link">karṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-zaarduulakar_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> bd. np. de Śārdūlakarṇa «Oreille de tigre», fils du chef <a href="#entry-ca_n_daala" class="skt-link">caṇḍāla</a> <a href="#entry-Utrizafku_1" class="skt-link">Triśaṅku_1</a>, que ce dernier voulait marier à la fille du brâhmane Puṣkarasārī.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaarduulakar_naavadaana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śārdūlakarṇāvadāna</i> [<a href="#entry-avadaana_2" class="skt-link">avadāna_2</a>] <a href="#entry-zaarduulakar_naavadaana" class="skt-link">n.</a> bd. np. du Śārdūlakarṇāvadāna «Conte de <a href="#entry-Uzaarduulakar_na" class="skt-link">Śārdūlakarṇa</a>», texte bd. réfutant l'existence naturelle des classes et des castes.<br> <a href="#entry-zaarduulakar_na" class="skt-link">śārdūlakarṇa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaarduulavikrii_dita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śārdūlavikrīḍita</i> [<a href="#entry-vikrii_dita" class="skt-link">vikrīḍita</a>] <a href="#entry-zaarduulavikrii_dita" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. [«jeu du tigre»] mètre poétique; il consiste en 4 vers de 19 syllabes, avec coupe après la 12⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩; son rythme évoque la marche du tigre; on l'utilise not. pour les souhaits à des jeunes mariés.<br> <a href="#entry-zaarduula" class="skt-link">śārdūla</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaarfga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शार्ङ्ग</span> <i>śārṅga</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-z_rfga" class="skt-link">śṛṅga</a>] <a href="#entry-zaarfga" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zaarfgii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śārṅgī</i> natu. sorte d'oiseau.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaarfgadhara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śārṅgadhara</i> [<a href="#entry-dhara" class="skt-link">dhara</a>] <a href="#entry-zaarfgadhara" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du poète Śārṅgadhara; il compila l'anthologie <a href="#entry-Uzaarfgadharapaddhati" class="skt-link">Śārṅgadharapaddhati</a> en 1363 <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. du médeçin Śārṅgadharācārya (14⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle); cf. <a href="#entry-Uzaarfgadharasa_mhitaa" class="skt-link">Śārṅgadharasaṃhitā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaarfgadharapaddhati">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śārṅgadharapaddhati</i> [<a href="#entry-paddhati" class="skt-link">paddhati</a>] <a href="#entry-zaarfgadharapaddhati" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de la Śārṅgadharapaddhati, anthologie de <a href="#entry-subhaa_sita" class="skt-link">subhāṣita</a> due à <a href="#entry-Uzaarfgadhara" class="skt-link">Śārṅgadhara</a> (1363).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaarfgadharasa_mhitaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śārṅgadharasaṃhitā</i> [<a href="#entry-sa_mhitaa" class="skt-link">saṃhitā</a>] <a href="#entry-zaarfgadharasa_mhitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> méd. np. de la Śārṅgadharasaṃhitā, somme de connaissances de la pharmacopée <a href="#entry-aayurveda" class="skt-link">āyurveda</a> du 14⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle.<br> <a href="#entry-zaarfgadhara" class="skt-link">śārṅgadhara</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaarfgaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śārṅgaka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-zaarfgaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> pl. myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] petits de <a href="#entry-zaarfga" class="skt-link">śārṅga</a> (en fait fils d'un sage) incapables de voler; dans l'incendie de la forêt <a href="#entry-Ukhaa_n_dava" class="skt-link">Khāṇḍava</a>, ils sont épargnés par <a href="#entry-Uagni" class="skt-link">Agni</a>, satisfait de leur récitation du <a href="#entry-Uveda" class="skt-link">Veda</a>, et qui leur accorde un vœu; ils demandent que tous les chats soient brûlés.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaarfgarava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śārṅgarava</i> [<a href="#entry-rava" class="skt-link">rava</a>] <a href="#entry-zaarfgarava" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. d'un disciple de <a href="#entry-Uka_nva" class="skt-link">Kaṇva</a> «qui crie comme un <a href="#entry-zaarfga" class="skt-link">śārṅga</a>».<br> <a href="#entry-zaarfga" class="skt-link">śārṅga</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaaribaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शारिबा</span> <i>śāribā</i> <a href="#entry-zaaribaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> natu. bot. <i>Hemidesmus Indicus</i>, salsepareille indienne, liane gentianée; la racine de cet arbuste sarmenteux est utilisée en médecine; syn. <a href="#entry-anantamuula" class="skt-link">anantamūla</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaariira">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शारीर</span> <i>śārīra</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-zariira" class="skt-link">śarīra</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zaariira" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zaariira" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zaariiraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śārīrā</i> corporel — <a href="#entry-zaariira" class="skt-link">n.</a> méd. physiologie, anatomie.<br> <i>śārīra</i> <i>daṇḍa</i> châtiment corporel.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaariiraka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शारीरक</span> <i>śārīraka</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-zariira" class="skt-link">śarīra</a><a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zaariiraka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zaariiraka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zaariirakaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śārīrakā</i> corporel — <a href="#entry-zaariiraka" class="skt-link">n.</a> phil. l'esprit incarné <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> doctrine sur sa nature.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaariirakamiimaa_msaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śārīrakamīmāṃsā</i> [<a href="#entry-miimaa_msaa" class="skt-link">mīmāṃsā</a>] <a href="#entry-zaariirakamiimaa_msaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de la Śārīrakamīmāṃsā «Investigation sur l'esprit incarné», épith. du <a href="#entry-Ubrahmasuutra" class="skt-link">Brahmasūtra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaariirakamiimaa_msaabhaa_sya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śārīrakamīmāṃsābhāṣya</i> [<a href="#entry-bhaa_sya" class="skt-link">bhāṣya</a>] <a href="#entry-zaariirakamiimaa_msaabhaa_sya" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Śārīrakamīmāṃsābhāṣya de <a href="#entry-Uzafkara" class="skt-link">Śaṅkara</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Ubrahmasuutrabhaa_sya" class="skt-link">Brahmasūtrabhāṣya</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. épith. du <a href="#entry-Ubhaaskarabhaa_sya" class="skt-link">Bhāskarabhāṣya</a>, autre glose du <a href="#entry-Ubrahmasuutra" class="skt-link">Brahmasūtra</a> due à <a href="#entry-Ubhaaskara_1" class="skt-link">Bhāskara_1</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-zaariirakamiimaa_msaa" class="skt-link">śārīrakamīmāṃsā</a> <br> <a href="#entry-zaariiraka" class="skt-link">śārīraka</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaariputra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शारिपुत्र</span> <i>śāriputra</i> <a href="#entry-zaariputra" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. de Śāriputra, l'un des disciples préférés du <a href="#entry-Ubuddha" class="skt-link">Buddha</a>; il est fréquemment mentionné dans la <a href="#entry-Ujaatakamaalaa" class="skt-link">Jātakamālā</a>, avec <a href="#entry-Umahaamaudgalyaayana" class="skt-link">Mahāmaudgalyāyana</a>; il est le héros du Śāriputraprakaraṇa.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaariputraprakara_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śāriputraprakaraṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-prakara_na" class="skt-link">prakaraṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-zaariputraprakara_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Śāriputraprakaraṇa «geste de <a href="#entry-Uzaariputra" class="skt-link">Śāriputra</a>», pièce de théâtre dûe à <a href="#entry-Uazvagho_sa" class="skt-link">Aśvaghoṣa</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-zaariputra" class="skt-link">śāriputra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaarvarii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शार्वरी</span> <i>śārvarī</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-zarvarii" class="skt-link">śarvarī</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zaarvarii" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zaarvarii" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zaarvarii" class="skt-link">f.</a> nocturne <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pernicieux; lugubre — <a href="#entry-zaarvarii" class="skt-link">f.</a> nuit — <a href="#entry-zaarvarii" class="skt-link">n.</a> obscurité.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaarvarin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शार्वरिन्</span> <i>śārvarin</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-zarvara" class="skt-link">śarvara</a><a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] <a href="#entry-zaarvarin" class="skt-link">m.</a> astr. np. de Śārvarī «Crépuscule», personnifiant l'an 34 d'un cycle de vie [<a href="#entry-sa_mvatsara" class="skt-link">saṃvatsara</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaaryaata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शार्यात</span> <i>śāryāta</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-zaryaati" class="skt-link">śaryāti</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zaaryaata" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zaaryaata" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zaaryaatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śāryātī</i> myth. descendant de <a href="#entry-Uzaryaati" class="skt-link">Śaryāti</a> — <a href="#entry-zaaryaata" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. roi de sa dynastie; cf. <a href="#entry-Uhaihaya" class="skt-link">Haihaya</a>, Tālajaṅgha <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. myth. le peuple des Śāryātās.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">शास्</span> <i>śās</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] pr. <i>(śāsti)</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(śāsati)</i> pft. <i>(śaśāsa)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>śiṣṭa</i>, <i>śāsita</i>, <i>śāsta</i><i>)</i> pfp. <i>(</i><i>śiṣya</i>, <i>śāsya</i><i>)</i> abs. <i>(</i><i>śāsitvā</i>, <i>-śiṣya</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>anu</i>, <i>ā</i>, <i>pra</i><i>)</i> punir, châtier <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> ordonner, régir, commander, gouverner <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> éduquer, instruire — ps. <i>(śiṣyate)</i> être puni, être commandé, être instruit — ps. <i>(śāṣyate)</i> id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaasa_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
