    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शास</span> <i>śāsa_1</i> [act. <a href="#entry-zaas" class="skt-link">śās</a>] <a href="#entry-zaasa_1" class="skt-link">m.</a> ordre, commande <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> <i>śāsa</i> commandant, gouverneur; punisseur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. du <a href="#entry-_r_si" class="skt-link">ṛṣi</a> Śāsa <a href="#entry-Ubhaaradvaaja" class="skt-link">Bhāradvāja</a>, auteur d'hymnes véd.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaasa_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शास</span> <i>śāsa_2</i> [instr. <a href="#entry-zas" class="skt-link">śas</a>] <a href="#entry-zaasa_2" class="skt-link">m.</a> couteau de boucher.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaasahasta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śāsahasta</i> [<a href="#entry-hasta" class="skt-link">hasta</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zaasahasta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zaasahasta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zaasahastaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śāsahastā</i> qui tient un couteau de boucher en main.<br> <a href="#entry-zaasa_2" class="skt-link">śāsa_2</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaasaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शासक</span> <i>śāsaka</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-zaas" class="skt-link">śās</a>] <a href="#entry-zaasaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> maître, précepteur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaasana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शासन</span> <i>śāsana</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-zaas" class="skt-link">śās</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zaasana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zaasana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zaasanii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śāsanī</i> qui punit; qui commande; qui enseigne, précepteur — <a href="#entry-zaasana" class="skt-link">n.</a> punition, châtiment; ordre, édit royal, décret <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> contrat écrit; titre de propriété; écrit faisant autorité, livre sacré <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> enseignement, instruction; doctrine, religion <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. not. enseignement de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> à la Déesse dans ses révélations [<a href="#entry-zaivaagama" class="skt-link">śaivāgama</a>] — ifc. pouvoir sur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaasit_r">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शासितृ</span> <i>śāsitṛ</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-zaas" class="skt-link">śās</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zaasit_r" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zaasit_r" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zaasitrii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śāsitrī</i> qui punit; qui dirige; qui instruit <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gouverneur, commandant <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> instructeur, enseignant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaasita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शासित</span> <i>śāsita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-zaas" class="skt-link">śās</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zaasita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zaasita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zaasitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śāsitā</i> dirigé, gouverné; instruit <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> contrôlé, restreint <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> réprimandé, puni.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaasitavya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शासितव्य</span> <i>śāsitavya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">3</span>] <a href="#entry-zaas" class="skt-link">śās</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zaasitavya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zaasitavya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zaasitavyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śāsitavyā</i> à enseigner, à prescrire <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui doit être dirigé ou instruit.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaast_r">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शास्तृ</span> <i>śāstṛ</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-zaas" class="skt-link">śās</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zaast_r" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zaast_r" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zaastrii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śāstrī</i> qui punit, qui châtie, qui commande; qui instruit <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> maître, instituteur, gouverneur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Śāstā «Justicier», épith. de <a href="#entry-Uhariharaputra" class="skt-link">Hariharaputra</a>-Ayyanār portant l'épée et le fouet; <a href="#entry-Upu_skalaa" class="skt-link">Puṣkalā</a> et <a href="#entry-Upuur_naa_1" class="skt-link">Pūrṇā_1</a> sont ses épouses.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaasti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शास्ति</span> <i>śāsti</i> [act. <a href="#entry-zaas" class="skt-link">śās</a>] <a href="#entry-zaasti" class="skt-link">f.</a> correction, punition; commande, ordre; commandement <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> sceptre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaastra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शास्त्र</span> <i>śāstra</i> [instr. <a href="#entry-zaas" class="skt-link">śās</a>] <a href="#entry-zaastra" class="skt-link">n.</a> ordre, loi; précepte, règle <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> enseignement impératif, instruction; commandement <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> règle, théorie (opp. <a href="#entry-prayoga" class="skt-link">prayoga</a> la pratique); doctrine, science; connaissance <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> [«instrument d'instruction»] traité, ouvrage d'enseignement, livre de préceptes; texte sacré — ifc. science de.<br> <i>anantaṃśāstram</i> <i>alpaṃjīvitam</i> Le savoir est sans fin, mais la vie est courte.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaastraartha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śāstrārtha</i> [<a href="#entry-artha" class="skt-link">artha</a>] <a href="#entry-zaastraartha" class="skt-link">m.</a> scolastique, érudition <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. dialectique interprétative du système de <a href="#entry-Upaa_nini" class="skt-link">Pāṇini</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-zaastra" class="skt-link">śāstra</a>
  </div>
</div>

  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaastracarcasadas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śāstracarcasadas</i> [<a href="#entry-sadas" class="skt-link">sadas</a>] <a href="#entry-zaastracarcasadas" class="skt-link">n.</a> débat de discussion sur les textes sacrés.<br> <a href="#entry-zaastracarca" class="skt-link">śāstracarca</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaastrad_r_s_ta">

  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaastrakaavya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śāstrakāvya</i> [<a href="#entry-kaavya" class="skt-link">kāvya</a>] <a href="#entry-zaastrakaavya" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. [«poésie instructive»] cf. <a href="#entry-Uraava_navadha" class="skt-link">Rāvaṇavadha</a>, <a href="#entry-Uhitopadeza" class="skt-link">Hitopadeśa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaastrapaddhati">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śāstrapaddhati</i> [<a href="#entry-paddhati" class="skt-link">paddhati</a>] <a href="#entry-zaastrapaddhati" class="skt-link">f.</a> phil. interprétation canonique des textes sacrés.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaastraprameyapariccheda">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śāstraprameyapariccheda</i> [<a href="#entry-prameya" class="skt-link">prameya</a>-<a href="#entry-pariccheda" class="skt-link">pariccheda</a>] <a href="#entry-zaastraprameyapariccheda" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Śāstraprameyapariccheda de <a href="#entry-Uraamaanujaacaarya" class="skt-link">Rāmānujācārya</a>, partie de son <a href="#entry-Utantrarahasya" class="skt-link">Tantrarahasya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaastravid">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śāstravid</i> [<a href="#entry-vid_3" class="skt-link">vid_3</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zaastravid" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zaastravid" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zaastravid" class="skt-link">f.</a> qui connaît les textes sacrés.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaastri">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शास्त्रि</span> <i>śāstri</i> iic. de <a href="#entry-zaastrin" class="skt-link">śāstrin</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaastriiya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शास्त्रीय</span> <i>śāstrīya</i> [<a href="#entry-zaastra" class="skt-link">śāstra</a><a href="#entry--iiya" class="skt-link">-īya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zaastriiya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zaastriiya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zaastriiyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śāstrīyā</i> conforme aux enseignements, prescrit par les textes; légal, correct.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaastriiyatva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śāstrīyatva</i> [<a href="#entry--tva" class="skt-link">-tva</a>] <a href="#entry-zaastriiyatva" class="skt-link">n.</a> fait d'être prescrit par les textes.<br> <a href="#entry-zaastriiya" class="skt-link">śāstrīya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaastrin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शास्त्रिन्</span> <i>śāstrin</i> [<a href="#entry-zaastra" class="skt-link">śāstra</a><a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zaastrin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zaastrin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zaastri_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śāstriṇī</i> versé dans les écritures; lettré, érudit; savant — <a href="#entry-zaastrin" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. np. de Śāstrī, titre de lettré.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaastripari_sad">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śāstripariṣad</i> [<a href="#entry-pari_sad" class="skt-link">pariṣad</a>] <a href="#entry-zaastripari_sad" class="skt-link">f.</a> soc. communauté scientifique.<br> <a href="#entry-zaastri" class="skt-link">śāstri</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaasya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शास्य</span> <i>śāsya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-zaas" class="skt-link">śās</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zaasya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zaasya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zaasyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śāsyā</i> punissable; qu'on doit punir <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui doit être gouverné ou dirigé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaata_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शात</span> <i>śāta_1</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-zaa" class="skt-link">śā</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zaata_1" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zaata_1" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zaataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śātā</i> aiguisé, tranchant <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> mince, maigre, émacié; faible.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaata_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शात</span> <i>śāta_2</i> [act. <a href="#entry-zad" class="skt-link">śad</a>] <a href="#entry-zaata_2" class="skt-link">m.</a> chute, perte (ongles, cheveux).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaataatapa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śātātapa</i> [<a href="#entry-aatapa" class="skt-link">ātapa</a>] <a href="#entry-zaataatapa" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du légiste Śātātapa, compositeur de <a href="#entry-sm_rti" class="skt-link">smṛti</a>, de date incertaine.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaataatapasm_rti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śātātapasmṛti</i> [<a href="#entry-sm_rti" class="skt-link">smṛti</a>] <a href="#entry-zaataatapasm_rti" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de la Śātātapasmṛti, recueil de lois dû à <a href="#entry-Uzaataatapa" class="skt-link">Śātātapa</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-zaataatapa" class="skt-link">śātātapa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaatakar_nin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शातकर्णिन्</span> <i>śātakarṇin</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-zatakar_na" class="skt-link">śatakarṇa</a><a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] <a href="#entry-zaatakar_nin" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. de Śātakarṇī, patr. d'une lignée des <a href="#entry-Usaatavaahanaas" class="skt-link">Sātavāhanās</a>; not. d'un empereur des <a href="#entry-Usaatavaahanaas" class="skt-link">Sātavāhanās</a>, qui effectua deux sacrifices de cheval [<a href="#entry-azvamedha" class="skt-link">aśvamedha</a>] et conquit de grands territoires au 2⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle ant.; sa capitale était <a href="#entry-Uamaraavatii_1" class="skt-link">Amarāvatī_1</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. de Gautamīputra <a href="#entry-Uzaatakar_nii" class="skt-link">Śātakarṇī</a>, qui reconquit l'empire en 78 sous le nom de <a href="#entry-Uzaalivaahana" class="skt-link">Śālivāhana</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaatana_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शातन</span> <i>śātana_1</i> [act. <a href="#entry-zaa" class="skt-link">śā</a>] <a href="#entry-zaatana_1" class="skt-link">n.</a> fait d'aiguiser <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> acuité, finesse; maigreur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaatana_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शातन</span> <i>śātana_2</i> [act. <a href="#entry-zad" class="skt-link">śad</a>] <a href="#entry-zaatana_2" class="skt-link">n.</a> fait de faire tomber; coupe, polissage; arrachage, destruction.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaatodara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śātodara</i> [<a href="#entry-udara" class="skt-link">udara</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zaatodara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zaatodara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zaatodaraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śātodarā</i> mince, à la taille fine.<br> <a href="#entry-zaata_1" class="skt-link">śāta_1</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaava_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शाव</span> <i>śāva_1</i> <a href="#entry-zaava_1" class="skt-link">m.</a> petit d'animal.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaava_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शाव</span> <i>śāva_2</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-zava" class="skt-link">śava</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zaava_2" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zaava_2" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zaavaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śāvā</i> cadavérique; relatif au cadavre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaavaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śāvaka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-zaavaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> id.<br> <a href="#entry-zaava_1" class="skt-link">śāva_1</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaay">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शाय्</span> <i>śāy</i> [ca. <a href="#entry-zii_1" class="skt-link">śī_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(śāyayati)</i> faire dormir, envoyer dormir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शाय</span> <i>śāya</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-zii_1" class="skt-link">śī_1</a>] <a href="#entry-zaaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> qui dort, qui se repose.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaayaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शायक</span> <i>śāyaka</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-zii_1" class="skt-link">śī_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zaayaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zaayaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zaayikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śāyikā</i> qui dort, qui se repose — <a href="#entry-zaayikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śāyikā</i> posture de sommeil <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> tour de repos.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaayin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शायिन्</span> <i>śāyin</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-zii_1" class="skt-link">śī_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zaayin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zaayin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zaayinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śāyinī</i> gisant, étendu; reposant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaazvata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शाश्वत</span> <i>śāśvata</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-zazvat" class="skt-link">śaśvat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zaazvata" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zaazvata" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zaazvatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śāśvatī</i> éternel, perpétuel; continuel, incessant, ininterrompu; tout <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> imminent — <a href="#entry-zaazvata" class="skt-link">n.</a> continuité; éternité — <a href="#entry-zaazvatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śāśvatī</i> la Terre.<br> <i>śāśvatam</i> éternellement, perpétuellement.<br> <i>śāśvatīḥ</i> <i>samāḥ</i> adv. pour toujours [«pendant une suite ininterrompue d'années»].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaazvatika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शाश्वतिक</span> <i>śāśvatika</i> [<a href="#entry-zaazvata" class="skt-link">śāśvata</a><a href="#entry--ika" class="skt-link">-ika</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zaazvatika" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zaazvatika" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zaazvatikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śāśvatikā</i> éternel, permanent; constant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaazvatikataa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śāśvatikatā</i> [<a href="#entry--taa" class="skt-link">-tā</a>] <a href="#entry-zaazvatikataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> éternité.<br> <a href="#entry-zaazvatika" class="skt-link">śāśvatika</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zabala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शबल</span> <i>śabala</i> a. <a href="#entry-zabala" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zabala" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zabalaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śabalā</i> <a href="#entry-zabalii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śabalī</i> bigarré, tacheté; teinté de ⟨iic. i.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. [«au pelage bigarré»] np. de Śabala, chien gardien des enfers de <a href="#entry-Uyama" class="skt-link">Yama</a>, au regard perçant [<a href="#entry-caturak_sa" class="skt-link">caturakṣa</a>]; il est né de <a href="#entry-Usaramaa" class="skt-link">Saramā</a>, comme son frère le noir <a href="#entry-Uzyaama" class="skt-link">Śyāma</a>; il symbolise le Soleil-jour, comme son frère la Lune-nuit; du. ils sont collectivement désignés comme la paire <i>śabalau</i>; cf. <a href="#entry-Ukaalaka_njaas" class="skt-link">Kālakañjās</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> changé, altéré — <a href="#entry-zabalaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śabalā</i> var. <i>śabalī</i> myth. np. de Śabalā ou Śabalī «Bigarrée», vache d'abondance de <a href="#entry-Up_rthu" class="skt-link">Pṛthu</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] épith. de <a href="#entry-Unandinii" class="skt-link">Nandinī</a>, vache d'abondance de <a href="#entry-Uvasi_s_tha" class="skt-link">Vasiṣṭha</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gr. κερβερος; fr. Cerbère.<br> <i>śabalau</i> m. du. myth. <a href="#entry-Uzabala" class="skt-link">Śabala</a> et <a href="#entry-Uzyaama" class="skt-link">Śyāma</a>, les deux chiens du ciel de <a href="#entry-Uyama" class="skt-link">Yama</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zabara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शबर</span> <i>śabara</i> <a href="#entry-zabara" class="skt-link">m.</a> peuple tribal du Deccan; sauvage, barbare <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Śabara, épith. de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> «Chasseur sauvage» <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. de Śabara_1 <a href="#entry-Usvaamii" class="skt-link">Svāmī</a>, philosophe du 5⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle, auteur du <a href="#entry-Umiimaa_msaasuutrabhaa_sya" class="skt-link">Mīmāṃsāsūtrabhāṣya</a>; son œuvre servit de fondement aux systèmes de <a href="#entry-Ukumaarila" class="skt-link">Kumārila</a> et <a href="#entry-Uprabhaakara" class="skt-link">Prabhākara</a> — <a href="#entry-zabarii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śabarī</i> femme tribale.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zabda">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शब्द</span> <i>śabda</i> <a href="#entry-zabda" class="skt-link">m.</a> bruit, son; mot, parole; discours, langage <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> nom, titre, appellation <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> tradition orale <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. mot déclinable; déclinaison; morphème <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-saafkhya" class="skt-link">sāṃkhya</a>] la perception [<a href="#entry-tanmaatra" class="skt-link">tanmātra</a>] de l'ouïe [<a href="#entry-zrotra" class="skt-link">śrotra</a>]; il est régi [<a href="#entry-niyant_r" class="skt-link">niyantṛ</a>] par les <a href="#entry-Udizas" class="skt-link">Diśas</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-nyaaya" class="skt-link">nyāya</a>] le Verbe, autorité de la parole sacrée, un moyen de connaissance [<a href="#entry-pramaa_na" class="skt-link">pramāṇa</a>] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. [<a href="#entry-Ubhart_rhari" class="skt-link">Bhartṛhari</a>] le langage, sous les deux formes de signal sonore articulé [<a href="#entry-dhvani" class="skt-link">dhvani</a>] et de sens [<a href="#entry-artha" class="skt-link">artha</a>] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. mot dénotant; <a href="#entry-Upata_njali_1" class="skt-link">Patañjali_1</a> en distingue 4 catégories: <a href="#entry-jaatizabda" class="skt-link">jātiśabda</a> nom commun (dénotant une espèce), <a href="#entry-gu_nazabda" class="skt-link">guṇaśabda</a> adjectif (dénotant une propriété), <a href="#entry-kriyaazabda" class="skt-link">kriyāśabda</a> verbe (dénotant une action) et <a href="#entry-yad_rcchaazabda" class="skt-link">yadṛcchāśabda</a> nom propre (dénotant un individu) — ifc. gram. le mot «⟨iic.⟩» — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. md. <i>(śabdāyate)</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. <i>(śabdayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>śabdita</i><i>)</i> faire du bruit.<br> <i>śabdaṃ</i> <i>kṛ</i> appeler à haute voix.<br> <i>śabdaṃ</i> <i>dā</i> élever la voix, appeler.<br> <i>saśabdam</i> adv. avec fracas.<br> <i>śabdapūrveṇa</i> <i>yogena</i> phil. <a href="#entry-yoga" class="skt-link">yoga</a> fondé sur la parole impérissable [<a href="#entry-zabdatattva" class="skt-link">śabdatattva</a>].<br> <i>kvaśabda</i> m. gram. le mot «<a href="#entry-kva" class="skt-link">kva</a>».<br> <i>dvitīyagāmī</i> <i>na</i> <i>hi</i> <i>śabda</i> <i>eṣa</i> <i>naḥ</i> ceci est notre titre et ne s'applique à personne d'autre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zabdaabdhi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śabdābdhi</i> [<a href="#entry-abdhi" class="skt-link">abdhi</a>] <a href="#entry-zabdaabdhi" class="skt-link">m.</a> gram. np. du lexique Śabdābdhi «océan de mots».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zabdaadhyaasavaada">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śabdādhyāsavāda</i> [<a href="#entry-adhyaasa" class="skt-link">adhyāsa</a>-<a href="#entry-vaada" class="skt-link">vāda</a>] <a href="#entry-zabdaadhyaasavaada" class="skt-link">m.</a> gram. doctrine exprimant que les mots sont superimposés sur leur sens, postulant l'identité de <a href="#entry-zabda" class="skt-link">śabda</a> et <a href="#entry-artha" class="skt-link">artha</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zabdaadvaitavaada">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śabdādvaitavāda</i> [<a href="#entry-advaita" class="skt-link">advaita</a>-<a href="#entry-vaada" class="skt-link">vāda</a>] <a href="#entry-zabdaadvaitavaada" class="skt-link">m.</a> gram. doctrine exprimant que tous les concepts sont associés à un signifiant linguistique.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zabdaalafkaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śabdālaṃkāra</i> [<a href="#entry-alafkaara" class="skt-link">alaṃkāra</a>] <a href="#entry-zabdaala_mkaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. [«embellissement phonétique»] figure de style euphonique: équilibre rythmique, répétition [<a href="#entry-yamaka" class="skt-link">yamaka</a>], allitération [<a href="#entry-anupraasa" class="skt-link">anuprāsa</a>]; opp. <a href="#entry-arthaalafkaara" class="skt-link">arthālaṃkāra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zabdaalafkaaravicaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śabdālaṃkāravicāra</i> [<a href="#entry-vicaara" class="skt-link">vicāra</a>] <a href="#entry-zabdaala_mkaaravicaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Śabdālaṃkāravicāra, chapitre de la <a href="#entry-Ukaavyaalafkaarav_rtti" class="skt-link">Kāvyālaṃkāravṛtti</a> de <a href="#entry-Uvaamana_2" class="skt-link">Vāmana_2</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-zabdaalafkaara" class="skt-link">śabdālaṃkāra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zabdaanuk_rti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śabdānukṛti</i> [<a href="#entry-anuk_rti" class="skt-link">anukṛti</a>] <a href="#entry-zabdaanuk_rti" class="skt-link">f.</a> gram. onomatopée.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zabdaanuzaasana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śabdānuśāsana</i> [<a href="#entry-anuzaasana" class="skt-link">anuśāsana</a>] <a href="#entry-zabdaanuzaasana" class="skt-link">n.</a> gram. enseignement de la langue, règles de grammaire; traité de grammaire <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. np. de la grammaire Śabdānuśāsana «Traité sur la langue» de <a href="#entry-Uzaaka_taayana_1" class="skt-link">Śākaṭāyana_1</a>; elle comporte un commentaire <a href="#entry-Uamoghav_rtti" class="skt-link">Amoghavṛtti</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zabdaartha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śabdārtha</i> [<a href="#entry-artha" class="skt-link">artha</a>] <a href="#entry-zabdaartha" class="skt-link">m.</a> gram. la signification d'un mot; la signification analytique d'une phrase décomposée en mots (préalable à la glose <a href="#entry-bhaavaartha" class="skt-link">bhāvārtha</a>) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. énergie magique de la parole <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> du. <i>śabdārthau</i> le son et le sens (d'un mot, d'une phrase).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zabdabhaavanaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śabdabhāvanā</i> [<a href="#entry-bhaavanaa" class="skt-link">bhāvanā</a>] <a href="#entry-zabdabhaavanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> phil. [<a href="#entry-miimaa_msaa" class="skt-link">mīmāṃsā</a>] réalisation de la parole.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zabdabodha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śabdabodha</i> [<a href="#entry-bodha" class="skt-link">bodha</a>] <a href="#entry-zabdabodha" class="skt-link">m.</a> connaissance induite d'un témoignage oral <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> compréhension du sens d'une énonciation; analyse logique d'une phrase <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. sémantique communicative; il y a 3 méthodes d'analyse de la langue, suivant les écoles <a href="#entry-vyaakara_na" class="skt-link">vyākaraṇa</a>, <a href="#entry-nyaaya" class="skt-link">nyāya</a> et <a href="#entry-miimaa_msaa" class="skt-link">mīmāṃsā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zabdabrahman">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śabdabrahman</i> [<a href="#entry-brahman" class="skt-link">brahman</a>] <a href="#entry-zabdabrahman" class="skt-link">n.</a> phil. np. de Śabdabrahman, le <a href="#entry-Uveda" class="skt-link">Veda</a> comme Verbe révélé, érigé en Être suprême.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zabdakalpa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śabdakalpa</i> [<a href="#entry-kalpa" class="skt-link">kalpa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zabdakalpa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zabdakalpa" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zabdakalpaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śabdakalpā</i> gram. propre à traiter la langue.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zabdakalpadruma">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śabdakalpadruma</i> [<a href="#entry-druma" class="skt-link">druma</a>] <a href="#entry-zabdakalpadruma" class="skt-link">m.</a> gram. encyclopédie <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. np. du Śabdakalpadruma, lexique encyclopédique du sanskrit avec indications grammaticales de plusieurs lettrés bengalis, à l'initiative du Rājā Rādhākāntadeva.<br> <a href="#entry-zabdakalpa" class="skt-link">śabdakalpa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zabdakalpataru">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śabdakalpataru</i> [<a href="#entry-taru" class="skt-link">taru</a>] <a href="#entry-zabdakalpataru" class="skt-link">m.</a> gram. lexique.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zabdakaustubha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śabdakaustubha</i> [<a href="#entry-kaustubha" class="skt-link">kaustubha</a>] <a href="#entry-zabdakaustubha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zabdakaustubha" class="skt-link">n.</a> gram. np. du Śabdakaustubha «Joyau de linguistique», œuvre de <a href="#entry-Ubha_t_toji" class="skt-link">Bhaṭṭoji</a> <a href="#entry-Udiik_sita" class="skt-link">Dīkṣita</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zabdakoza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śabdakośa</i> [<a href="#entry-koza" class="skt-link">kośa</a>] <a href="#entry-zabdakoza" class="skt-link">m.</a> dictionnaire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zabdama_njarii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śabdamañjarī</i> [<a href="#entry-ma_njarii" class="skt-link">mañjarī</a>] <a href="#entry-zabdama_njarii" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de la Śabdamañjarī «Bouquet linguistique», grammaire due à <a href="#entry-Unaaraaya_na" class="skt-link">Nārāyaṇa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zabdana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शब्दन</span> <i>śabdana</i> [<a href="#entry-zabda" class="skt-link">śabda</a><a href="#entry--na" class="skt-link">-na</a>] <a href="#entry-zabdana" class="skt-link">n.</a> bruit, son; fait de parler.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zabdanityatva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śabdanityatva</i> [<a href="#entry-nityatva" class="skt-link">nityatva</a>] <a href="#entry-zabdanityatva" class="skt-link">n.</a> phil. [<a href="#entry-miimaa_msaa" class="skt-link">mīmāṃsā</a>] doctrine de l'éternité du son.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zabdaruupa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śabdarūpa</i> [<a href="#entry-ruupa" class="skt-link">rūpa</a>] <a href="#entry-zabdaruupa" class="skt-link">n.</a> nature ou qualité d'un son <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. [<a href="#entry-Ukaazikaa" class="skt-link">Kāśikā</a>] structure du signifiant; mot grammatical.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zabdatattva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śabdatattva</i> [<a href="#entry-tattva" class="skt-link">tattva</a>] <a href="#entry-zabdatattva" class="skt-link">n.</a> phil. [«essence-parole»] la parole impérissable et créatrice du monde.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zabdavat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śabdavat</i> [<a href="#entry--vat" class="skt-link">-vat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zabdavat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zabdavat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zabdavatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śabdavatī</i> qui émet un son; bruyant — adv. bruyamment.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zabdavidyaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śabdavidyā</i> [<a href="#entry-vidyaa" class="skt-link">vidyā</a>] <a href="#entry-zabdavidyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> grammaire; linguistique; philologie.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zabdavyaapaaravicaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śabdavyāpāravicāra</i> [<a href="#entry-vyaapaara" class="skt-link">vyāpāra</a>-<a href="#entry-vicaara" class="skt-link">vicāra</a>] <a href="#entry-zabdavyaapaaravicaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Śabdavyāpāravicāra «Investigation de l'emploi de la parole», ouvrage de sémantique de <a href="#entry-Umamma_ta" class="skt-link">Mammaṭa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zabdazakti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śabdaśakti</i> [<a href="#entry-zakti" class="skt-link">śakti</a>] <a href="#entry-zabdazakti" class="skt-link">f.</a> phil. sémantique lexicale, pragmatique; pouvoir de la parole <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. pouvoir d'évocation de sens d'un mot; on distingue 4 niveaux: le sens littéral [<a href="#entry-abhidhaa_2" class="skt-link">abhidhā_2</a>], le sens figuratif [<a href="#entry-lak_sa_naa" class="skt-link">lakṣaṇā</a>], le sens suggestif [<a href="#entry-vya_njanaa" class="skt-link">vyañjanā</a>] et le sens que souhaite transmettre le locuteur [<a href="#entry-taatparya" class="skt-link">tātparya</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zabdazaktimuula">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śabdaśaktimūla</i> [<a href="#entry-muula" class="skt-link">mūla</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zabdazaktimuula" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zabdazaktimuula" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zabdazaktimuulaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śabdaśaktimūlā</i> gram. [«qui a son origine dans le pouvoir des mots»] se dit d'un sens suggéré [<a href="#entry-dhvani" class="skt-link">dhvani</a>] issu du signifiant; opp. <a href="#entry-arthazaktimuula" class="skt-link">arthaśaktimūla</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zabdazaktiprakaazikaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śabdaśaktiprakāśikā</i> [<a href="#entry-prakaazikaa" class="skt-link">prakāśikā</a>] <a href="#entry-zabdazaktiprakaazikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> phil. np. de la Śabdaśaktiprakāśikā «Révélation du pouvoir de la parole», ouvrage de sémantique [<a href="#entry-navyanyaaya" class="skt-link">navyanyāya</a>] dû à <a href="#entry-Ujagadiiza_1" class="skt-link">Jagadīśa_1</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zabdazaktisvaabhaavya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śabdaśaktisvābhāvya</i> [<a href="#entry-svaabhaavya" class="skt-link">svābhāvya</a>] <a href="#entry-zabdazaktisvaabhaavya" class="skt-link">n.</a> gram. autonomie du pouvoir de la parole (justifiant les sens idiomatiques).<br> <a href="#entry-zabdazakti" class="skt-link">śabdaśakti</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zabdenduzekhara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śabdenduśekhara</i> [<a href="#entry-induzekhara" class="skt-link">induśekhara</a>] <a href="#entry-zabdenduzekhara" class="skt-link">m.</a> gram. np. du Śabdenduśekhara, commentaire de la <a href="#entry-Usiddhaantakaumudii" class="skt-link">Siddhāntakaumudī</a> dû à <a href="#entry-Unaagoji" class="skt-link">Nāgoji</a> <a href="#entry-Ubha_t_ta" class="skt-link">Bhaṭṭa</a>; on distingue le commentaire complet <a href="#entry-Ub_rhacchabdenduzekhara" class="skt-link">Bṛhacchabdenduśekhara</a> et son résumé, le <a href="#entry-Ulaghuzabdenduzekhara" class="skt-link">Laghuśabdenduśekhara</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-zabda" class="skt-link">śabda</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zabdita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शब्दित</span> <i>śabdita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-zabda" class="skt-link">śabda</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zabdita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zabdita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zabditaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śabditā</i> (bruit) émis; énoncé, crié; communiqué; enseigné; invoqué — <a href="#entry-zabdita" class="skt-link">n.</a> bruit, tumulte; braiement.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zacii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शची</span> <i>śacī</i> [<a href="#entry-zak" class="skt-link">śak</a>] <a href="#entry-zacii" class="skt-link">f.</a> assistance, secours, grâce <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> éloquence <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Śacī «Grâce», fille de <a href="#entry-Upulomaa_1" class="skt-link">Pulomā_1</a>, épouse d'<a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaciipati">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śacīpati</i> [<a href="#entry-pati" class="skt-link">pati</a>] <a href="#entry-zaciipati" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Śacīpati «Maître de la Grâce», épith. d'<a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-zacii" class="skt-link">śacī</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaciitiirtha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śacītīrtha</i> [<a href="#entry-tiirtha" class="skt-link">tīrtha</a>] <a href="#entry-zaciitiirtha" class="skt-link">n.</a> myth. lieu dit du pélerinage de <a href="#entry-Uzacii" class="skt-link">Śacī</a>, où <a href="#entry-Uzakuntalaa" class="skt-link">Śakuntalā</a> s'arrêta sur le chemin du palais de <a href="#entry-Udu_syanta" class="skt-link">Duṣyanta</a>; on l'appelle aussi <a href="#entry-Uzakraavataara" class="skt-link">Śakrāvatāra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zad">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">शद्</span> <i>śad</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. md. <i>(śīyate)</i> fut. <i>(</i><i>śatsyati</i><i>)</i> pft. <i>(śaśāda)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>śanna</i><i>)</i> tomber — ca. <i>(śātayati)</i> faire tomber — ca. <i>(śādayati)</i> conduire le bétail (devant soi).<br> <i>bilvaṃ</i> <i>bilvena</i> <i>śātaya</i> [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] Brise la noix de bel avec la noix de bel.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zafk">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">शङ्क्</span> <i>śaṅk</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. md. <i>(śaṅkate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>śaṅkita</i><i>)</i> pfp. <i>(</i><i>śaṅkya</i>, <i>śaṅkitavya</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>ā</i>, <i>pari</i>, <i>vi</i><i>)</i> soupçonner, se défier de ⟨acc.⟩; craindre qqc. de qqn. ⟨abl.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hésiter, douter; se douter de, conjecturer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>cunctor</i>.<br> <i>śaṅke</i> adv. je suppose; à mon avis, probablement; peut-être <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> on peut le craindre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zafkaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शङ्का</span> <i>śaṅkā</i> [act. <a href="#entry-zafk" class="skt-link">śaṅk</a>] <a href="#entry-zafkaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> inquiétude, appréhension, peur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soupçon, méfiance, crainte; suspicion de ⟨abl. loc. <a href="#entry-prati" class="skt-link">prati</a>-acc.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> doute, hésitation — ifc. croyance en; fait de supposer que; suspicion de, présomption de — iic. id.<br> <i>brahmahatyākṛtā</i> <i>śaṅkā</i> peur d'avoir provoqué la mort d'un brâhmane.<br> <i>pāpaśaṅkā</i> <i>na</i> <i>kartavyā</i> [KSs.] Il n'y a pas lieu de suspecter qu'un péché ait été commis.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zafkaasthaana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śaṅkāsthāna</i> [<a href="#entry-sthaana" class="skt-link">sthāna</a>] <a href="#entry-zafkaasthaana" class="skt-link">n.</a> cause de crainte; objet de suspicion.<br> <a href="#entry-zafkaa" class="skt-link">śaṅkā</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zafkara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शंकर</span> <i>śaṃkara</i> var. <i>śaṅkara</i> [<a href="#entry-zam_3" class="skt-link">śam_3</a>-<a href="#entry-kara_1" class="skt-link">kara_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-za_mkara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-za_mkara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-za_mkaraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śaṃkarā</i> bienfaisant, qui apporte le bonheur ou la prospérité (np.) — <a href="#entry-za_mkara" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Śaṅkara ou <a href="#entry-Uzafkara" class="skt-link">Śaṃkara</a>, épith. de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> «accordant sa bénédiction» <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. de Śaṅkara_1 ou <a href="#entry-Uzafkara_1" class="skt-link">Śaṃkara_1</a> ou Ādi Śaṅkarācārya «Maître bienfaisant» (788–820), philosophe né à Kālaṭi (près de mod. Kochi au <a href="#entry-Ukerala" class="skt-link">Kerala</a>); il était disciple de <a href="#entry-Ugovindanaatha" class="skt-link">Govindanātha</a>; il vécut à <a href="#entry-Uvaaraa_nasii" class="skt-link">Vārāṇasī</a>, où il prêcha le <a href="#entry-vedaanta" class="skt-link">vedānta</a> fondé sur la doctrine <a href="#entry-advaita" class="skt-link">advaita</a>; on lui attribue de très nombreux ouvrages, not. <a href="#entry-Uvivekacuu_daama_ni" class="skt-link">Vivekacūḍāmaṇi</a>, <a href="#entry-Udigvijaya" class="skt-link">Digvijaya</a>, <a href="#entry-Uprapa_ncasaara" class="skt-link">Prapañcasāra</a>, <a href="#entry-Umohamudgara" class="skt-link">Mohamudgara</a>, <a href="#entry-Uupadezasaahasrii" class="skt-link">Upadeśasāhasrī</a>, <a href="#entry-Ubrahmasuutrabhaa_sya" class="skt-link">Brahmasūtrabhāṣya</a>/<a href="#entry-Uzaariirakamiimaa_msaabhaa_sya" class="skt-link">Śārīrakamīmāṃsābhāṣya</a>; il fonda 4 monastères [<a href="#entry-ma_tha" class="skt-link">maṭha</a>], resp. à <a href="#entry-Upurii" class="skt-link">Purī</a>-<a href="#entry-Ugovardhana_3" class="skt-link">Govardhana_3</a>, <a href="#entry-Uz_rfgerii" class="skt-link">Śṛṅgerī</a>, <a href="#entry-Udvaarakaa" class="skt-link">Dvārakā</a> et <a href="#entry-Ubadariinaatha" class="skt-link">Badarīnātha</a> (<a href="#entry-jyotirma_tha" class="skt-link">jyotirmaṭha</a>); leurs supérieurs sont les pontifes [<a href="#entry-jagadguru" class="skt-link">jagadguru</a>] de l'ordre <a href="#entry-dazanaamiida_n_din" class="skt-link">daśanāmīdaṇḍin</a> de la doctrine <a href="#entry-advaita" class="skt-link">advaita</a>; il fonda le système des 6 doctrines [<a href="#entry-U_sa_nmatasthaapakaacaarya" class="skt-link">Ṣaṇmatasthāpakācārya</a>] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. de Śaṅkara_2 ou <a href="#entry-Uzafkara_2" class="skt-link">Śaṃkara_2</a> <a href="#entry-Umizra" class="skt-link">Miśra</a>, poète, dramaturge et érudit du 15⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle; on lui doit le <a href="#entry-Uka_naadarahasya" class="skt-link">Kaṇādarahasya</a>, qui décrit les catégories du <a href="#entry-vaize_sika" class="skt-link">vaiśeṣika</a> avec les méthodes de <a href="#entry-navyanyaaya" class="skt-link">navyanyāya</a> — <a href="#entry-za_mkarii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śaṃkarī</i> myth. np. de Śaṃkarī «Épouse de <a href="#entry-Uzafkara" class="skt-link">Śaṃkara</a>», épith. de la Déesse — <a href="#entry-za_mkaraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śaṃkarā</i> natu. zoo. <i>Sturnia pagodarum</i> «Étourneau des pagodes» ou mainate brâhmane, joli oiseau beige au capuchon noir d'allure sereine; on le compare aux renonçantes errantes [<a href="#entry-parivraajikaa" class="skt-link">parivrājikā</a>].<br> <i>śaṃkarā</i> <i>nāma</i> <i>śakunikā</i> l'oiseau appelé mainate brâhmane.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zafkaraacaarya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śaṃkarācārya</i> [<a href="#entry-aacaarya" class="skt-link">ācārya</a>] <a href="#entry-za_mkaraacaarya" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. le Maître <a href="#entry-Uzafkara_1" class="skt-link">Śaṅkara_1</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. l'un des supérieurs des 4 monastères [<a href="#entry-ma_tha" class="skt-link">maṭha</a>] fondés par lui.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zafkaraananda">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śaṃkarānanda</i> [<a href="#entry-aananda" class="skt-link">ānanda</a>] <a href="#entry-za_mkaraananda" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du philosophe Śaṃkarānanda; il fut le <a href="#entry-guru" class="skt-link">guru</a> de <a href="#entry-Usaaya_na" class="skt-link">Sāyaṇa</a>; on lui doit l'<a href="#entry-Uaatmapuraa_na" class="skt-link">Ātmapurāṇa</a> et de nombreux commentaires sur les leçons [<a href="#entry-upani_sad" class="skt-link">upaniṣad</a>].<br> <a href="#entry-zafkara" class="skt-link">śaṃkara</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zafkarara_nya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śaṅkararaṇya</i> [<a href="#entry-ra_nya" class="skt-link">raṇya</a>] <a href="#entry-zafkarara_nya" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du renonçant <a href="#entry-Usvaamii" class="skt-link">Svāmī</a> Śaṅkararaṇya «Plaisir de la bienfaisance», frère aîné de <a href="#entry-Ucaitanya" class="skt-link">Caitanya</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Uvizvaruupa_2" class="skt-link">Viśvarūpa_2</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zafkarasena">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śaṅkarasena</i> [<a href="#entry-senaa" class="skt-link">senā</a>] <a href="#entry-zafkarasena" class="skt-link">m.</a> méd. np. du médecin Śaṃkarasena, auteur du <a href="#entry-Unaa_diiprakaaza" class="skt-link">Nāḍīprakāśa</a> (18⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle?).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zafkarasvaamin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śaṅkarasvāmin</i> [<a href="#entry-svaamin" class="skt-link">svāmin</a>] <a href="#entry-zafkarasvaamin" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. de Śaṅkarasvāmī, logicien auteur du <a href="#entry-Unyaayapraveza" class="skt-link">Nyāyapraveśa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zafkaravarman">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śaṅkaravarman</i> [<a href="#entry-varman" class="skt-link">varman</a>] <a href="#entry-zafkaravarman" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du roi du <a href="#entry-Ukazmiira" class="skt-link">Kaśmīra</a> Śaṅkaravarmā (883–902); cf. <a href="#entry-Uaagama_dambara" class="skt-link">Āgamaḍambara</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zafkaravijaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śaṅkaravijaya</i> [<a href="#entry-vijaya" class="skt-link">vijaya</a>] <a href="#entry-zafkaravijaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Śaṅkaravijaya «Victoire décisive de <a href="#entry-Uzafkara_1" class="skt-link">Śaṅkara_1</a>», œuvre d'Ānandagiri réfutant les hérésies.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zafkha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शङ्ख</span> <i>śaṅkha</i> <a href="#entry-zafkha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zafkha" class="skt-link">n.</a> conque; coquille, coquillage <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. conque-attribut de <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a>, symbolisant l'appel divin; cf. <a href="#entry-Upaa_ncajanya" class="skt-link">Pāñcajanya</a> — <a href="#entry-zafkha" class="skt-link">n.</a> math. dix billions <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>concha</i>; fr. conque.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zafkha_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शङ्खण</span> <i>śaṅkhaṇa</i> <a href="#entry-zafkha_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] np. du roi d'<a href="#entry-Uayodhyaa" class="skt-link">Ayodhyā</a> Śaṅkhaṇa, fils de <a href="#entry-Uprav_rddha" class="skt-link">Pravṛddha</a>, père de <a href="#entry-Usudarzana_2" class="skt-link">Sudarśana_2</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zafkhaasura">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śaṅkhāsura</i> [<a href="#entry-asura" class="skt-link">asura</a>] <a href="#entry-zafkhaasura" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. du démon marin Śaṅkhāsura «Génie de la conque»; cf. <a href="#entry-Upa_ncajana" class="skt-link">Pañcajana</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-zafkha" class="skt-link">śaṅkha</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zafkhadvaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śaṅkhadvāra</i> [<a href="#entry-dvaara" class="skt-link">dvāra</a>] <a href="#entry-zafkhadvaara" class="skt-link">n.</a> arch. seuil de sanctuaire orné de deux conques.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zafkhalikhita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śaṅkhalikhita</i> [<a href="#entry-likhita" class="skt-link">likhita</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zafkhalikhita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zafkhalikhita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zafkhalikhitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śaṅkhalikhitā</i> impeccable, parfait; sans défault, irréprochable.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zafkhalikhitav_rtti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śaṅkhalikhitavṛtti</i> [<a href="#entry-v_rtti" class="skt-link">vṛtti</a>] <a href="#entry-zafkhalikhitav_rtti" class="skt-link">f.</a> conduite irréprochable.<br> <a href="#entry-zafkhalikhita" class="skt-link">śaṅkhalikhita</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zafkhamuktaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śaṅkhamuktā</i> [<a href="#entry-muktaa" class="skt-link">muktā</a>] <a href="#entry-zafkhamuktaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> nacre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zafkhanidhi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śaṅkhanidhi</i> [<a href="#entry-nidhi" class="skt-link">nidhi</a>] <a href="#entry-zafkhanidhi" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Śaṅkhanidhi, l'un des 9 trésors de <a href="#entry-Ukubera" class="skt-link">Kubera</a> [<a href="#entry-navanidhi" class="skt-link">navanidhi</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zafkhapaala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śaṅkhapāla</i> [<a href="#entry-paala" class="skt-link">pāla</a>] <a href="#entry-zafkhapaala" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. du <a href="#entry-naaga" class="skt-link">nāga</a> Śaṅkhapāla.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zafkhinii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शङ्खिनी</span> <i>śaṅkhinī</i> [<a href="#entry-zafkha" class="skt-link">śaṅkha</a><a href="#entry-in" class="skt-link">in</a>] <a href="#entry-zafkhinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> nacre <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. [<a href="#entry-kaamazaastra" class="skt-link">kāmaśāstra</a>] l'une des 4 natures de femmes (avec <a href="#entry-citri_nii" class="skt-link">citriṇī</a>, <a href="#entry-padminii" class="skt-link">padminī</a>, et <a href="#entry-hastinii" class="skt-link">hastinī</a>).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zafkin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शङ्किन्</span> <i>śaṅkin</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-zafk" class="skt-link">śaṅk</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zafkin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zafkin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zafkinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śaṅkinī</i> qui suppose, qui soupçonne; méfiant; jaloux; timide — ifc. qui craint, qui croit <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui éveille des inquiétudes.<br> <i>kākaśaṅkin</i> a. m. n. méfiant comme un corbeau.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zafkita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शङ्कित</span> <i>śaṅkita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-zafk" class="skt-link">śaṅk</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zafkita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zafkita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zafkitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śaṅkitā</i> préoccupé, inquiet, alarmé; méfiant, craintif <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> craint; douteux, incertain.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zafkitavya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शङ्कितव्य</span> <i>śaṅkitavya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">3</span>] <a href="#entry-zafk" class="skt-link">śaṅk</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zafkitavya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zafkitavya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zafkitavyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śaṅkitavyā</i> à craindre, dont il faut se méfier.<br> <i>śaṅkitavyam</i> (impers.) c'est à craindre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zafku">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शङ्कु</span> <i>śaṅku</i> [<a href="#entry-zafk" class="skt-link">śaṅk</a><a href="#entry--u" class="skt-link">-u</a>] <a href="#entry-zafku" class="skt-link">m.</a> pieu, poteau; tronc d'arbre <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> cheville, clou; flèche, pointe <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> astr. gnomon d'un cadran solaire <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. l'aiguillon du remords <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> astr. sinus de l'altitude <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> math. un billion (mille milliards) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. de Śaṅku, l'un des neuf joyaux [<a href="#entry-navaratna" class="skt-link">navaratna</a>] de la cour du roi <a href="#entry-Uvikramaaditya" class="skt-link">Vikramāditya</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. du prince <a href="#entry-v_r_s_ni" class="skt-link">vṛṣṇi</a> Śaṅku_1, fils d'<a href="#entry-Uugrasena" class="skt-link">Ugrasena</a>; c'est l'un des 7 célèbres archers <a href="#entry-yaadavaas" class="skt-link">yādavās</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zafkukar_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śaṅkukarṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-kar_na" class="skt-link">karṇa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zafkukar_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zafkukar_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zafkukar_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śaṅkukarṇī</i> qui a les oreilles pointues — <a href="#entry-zafkukar_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> âne.<br> <a href="#entry-zafku" class="skt-link">śaṅku</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zafkulaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शङ्कुला</span> <i>śaṅkulā</i> <a href="#entry-zafkulaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> scalpel <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> ciseaux; pince coupante (not. pour découper la noix d'arec).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zafkya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शङ्क्य</span> <i>śaṅkya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-zafk" class="skt-link">śaṅk</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zafkya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zafkya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zafkyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śaṅkyā</i> à craindre, dont il faut se méfier <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> à prévoir, à anticiper.<br> <i>śaṅkyam</i> (impers.) c'est à craindre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaibya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शैब्य</span> <i>śaibya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-zibi" class="skt-link">śibi</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zaibya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zaibya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zaibyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śaibyā</i> relatif aux <a href="#entry-zibayas" class="skt-link">śibayas</a>; descendant de <a href="#entry-Uzibi" class="skt-link">Śibi</a> — <a href="#entry-zaibya" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [PP.] np. de Śaibya, l'un des 4 chevaux du char de <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a>, de couleur verte — <a href="#entry-zaibyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śaibyā</i> myth. np. de la princesse Śaibyā, nièce de l'imposteur <a href="#entry-Uvaasudeva_1" class="skt-link">Vāsudeva_1</a>; <a href="#entry-Uzvetaketu_1" class="skt-link">Śvetaketu_1</a> et <a href="#entry-Uuddhava" class="skt-link">Uddhava</a> en tombèrent amoureux; ce dernier fit intervenir <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a> en sa faveur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaikya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शैक्य</span> <i>śaikya</i> a. <a href="#entry-zaikya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zaikya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zaikyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śaikyā</i> damasquiné.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaila">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शैल</span> <i>śaila</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-zilaa" class="skt-link">śilā</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zaila" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zaila" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zailaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śailā</i> en pierre, pierreux — <a href="#entry-zaila" class="skt-link">m.</a> colline, montagne.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zailamaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śailamaya</i> [<a href="#entry-maya" class="skt-link">maya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zailamaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zailamaya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zailamayii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śailamayī</i> en pierre.<br> <i>śailamayī</i> <i>pratimā</i> idole en pierre.<br> <a href="#entry-zaila" class="skt-link">śaila</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zailaputrii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śailaputrī</i> [<a href="#entry-putrii" class="skt-link">putrī</a>] <a href="#entry-zailaputrii" class="skt-link">f.</a> myth. np. de Śailaputrī «Fille de la montagne», épith. de <a href="#entry-Upaarvatii" class="skt-link">Pārvatī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zailuu_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शैलूष</span> <i>śailūṣa</i> <a href="#entry-zailuu_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> danseur, mime; acteur, baladin — <a href="#entry-zailuu_sii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śailūṣī</i> actrice, danseuse.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaitya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शैत्य</span> <i>śaitya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-ziita" class="skt-link">śīta</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] <a href="#entry-zaitya" class="skt-link">n.</a> froid, fraîcheur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaiva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शैव</span> <i>śaiva</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-ziva" class="skt-link">śiva</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zaiva" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zaiva" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zaivii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śaivī</i> relatif à <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> — <a href="#entry-zaiva" class="skt-link">m.</a> shivaïte, adorateur de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaivaagama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śaivāgama</i> [<a href="#entry-aagama" class="skt-link">āgama</a>] <a href="#entry-zaivaagama" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. <a href="#entry-tantra" class="skt-link">tantra</a> shivaïte (sous la forme d'un dialogue où <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> répond à des questions de <a href="#entry-Uzakti" class="skt-link">Śakti</a>, opp. <a href="#entry-nigama" class="skt-link">nigama</a>); on en dénombre traditionnellement 28.<br> <a href="#entry-zaiva" class="skt-link">śaiva</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaivacaapa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śaivacāpa</i> [<a href="#entry-caapa" class="skt-link">cāpa</a>] <a href="#entry-zaivacaapa" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de l'arc magique Śaivacāpa que <a href="#entry-Uvizvakarmaa" class="skt-link">Viśvakarmā</a> fabriqua pour <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> afin de détruire <a href="#entry-Utripura" class="skt-link">Tripura</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Uajagava" class="skt-link">Ajagava</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaivapuraa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śaivapurāṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-puraa_na" class="skt-link">purāṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-zaivapuraa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Śaivapurāṇa; cf. <a href="#entry-Uvaayaviiyapuraa_na" class="skt-link">Vāyavīyapurāṇa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaivasiddhaanta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śaivasiddhānta</i> [<a href="#entry-siddhaanta" class="skt-link">siddhānta</a>] <a href="#entry-zaivasiddhaanta" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. littérature shivaïte de l'Inde du Sud.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaizava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शैशव</span> <i>śaiśava</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-zizu" class="skt-link">śiśu</a>] <a href="#entry-zaizava" class="skt-link">n.</a> enfance.<br> <i>śaiśave</i> adv. dans l'enfance.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zak">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">शक्</span> <i>śak</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">5</span>] pr. <i>(śaknoti)</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] pr. <i>(śakyati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>śakta</i><i>)</i> pfp. <i>(</i><i>śakya</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>sam</i><i>)</i> pouvoir, être capable de ⟨inf.⟩ — ps. <i>(śakyate)</i> pouvoir ⟨inf.⟩ — dés. <i>(śikṣati)</i> dés. md. <i>(śikṣate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>śikṣita</i><i>)</i> cf. <a href="#entry-zik_s" class="skt-link">śikṣ</a>.<br> <i>rāṣṭraṃ</i> <i>bhoktuṃ</i> <i>śakyasi</i> <i>pālayan</i> [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] Tu seras capable de jouir du royaume tout en le protégeant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zak_rt">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शकृत्</span> <i>śakṛt</i> <a href="#entry-zak_rt" class="skt-link">n.</a> excréments, ordure.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शक</span> <i>śaka</i> <a href="#entry-zaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. peuple Scythe arrivé de Bactriane au nord-ouest de l'Inde au 1⟨sup⟩er⟨/sup⟩ siècle ant. pour y fonder le royaume <a href="#entry-ku_saa_na" class="skt-link">kuṣāṇa</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. <i>śakās</i> géo. les Śakās ou Indo-Scythes; myth. on dit que leurs rois sont les <a href="#entry-k_satriyaas" class="skt-link">kṣatriyās</a> issus de la sueur de la vache de <a href="#entry-Uvasi_s_tha" class="skt-link">Vasiṣṭha</a>, afin de détruire l'armée de <a href="#entry-Uvizvaamitra" class="skt-link">Viśvāmitra</a>; le roi <a href="#entry-Usagara" class="skt-link">Sagara</a> les combattit en vain <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> cf. <a href="#entry-zakakaala" class="skt-link">śakakāla</a>.<br> <i>śakavarṣeṣu</i> <i>catuḥśateṣu</i> <i>pañcaṣaṣṭiyuteṣu</i> [«en l'ère <a href="#entry-Uzaka" class="skt-link">Śaka</a> quatre-cent soixante-cinq»] En 543.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaka_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शकट</span> <i>śakaṭa</i> <a href="#entry-zaka_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zaka_ta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zaka_tii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śakaṭī</i> char, chariot — <a href="#entry-zaka_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de l'<a href="#entry-asura" class="skt-link">asura</a> Śakaṭa, tué par <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaka_tikaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śakaṭikā</i> [<a href="#entry--ika" class="skt-link">-ika</a>] <a href="#entry-zaka_tikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> petit chariot à traîner (jouet d'enfant) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> chariot de décoration florale.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaka_tin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śakaṭin</i> [<a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zaka_tin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zaka_tin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zaka_tinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śakaṭinī</i> qui possède un char.<br> <a href="#entry-zaka_ta" class="skt-link">śakaṭa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaka_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शक</span> <i>śaka_1</i> iic. de <a href="#entry-zakan" class="skt-link">śakan</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zakaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शकार</span> <i>śakāra</i> [<i>(śa)</i>-<a href="#entry-kaara" class="skt-link">kāra</a>] <a href="#entry-zakaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> le son ou la lettre ‘<i>śa</i>’ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. [<a href="#entry-Um_rcchaka_tikaa" class="skt-link">Mṛcchakaṭikā</a>] np. de Śakāra, frère d'une épouse du roi <a href="#entry-Upaalaka" class="skt-link">Pālaka</a>; il est frivole, arrogant et cruel.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zakaayanya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śakāyanya</i> [<a href="#entry-aayanya" class="skt-link">āyanya</a>] <a href="#entry-zakaayanya" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. np. du sage Śakāyanya, qui enseigna la sagesse au roi <a href="#entry-Ub_rhadratha_1" class="skt-link">Bṛhadratha_1</a> dans la <a href="#entry-Umaitrii" class="skt-link">Maitrī</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-zaka" class="skt-link">śaka</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zakadhuuma">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śakadhūma</i> [<a href="#entry-dhuuma" class="skt-link">dhūma</a>] <a href="#entry-zakadhuuma" class="skt-link">m.</a> fumée de feu de bouse de vache.<br> <a href="#entry-zaka_1" class="skt-link">śaka_1</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zakakaala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śakakāla</i> [<a href="#entry-kaala_1" class="skt-link">kāla_1</a>] <a href="#entry-zakakaala" class="skt-link">m.</a> ère [<a href="#entry-sa_mvat" class="skt-link">saṃvat</a>] Śaka, débutant en l'an 78 avec le roi <a href="#entry-Uzaalivaahana" class="skt-link">Śālivāhana</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zakala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शकल</span> <i>śakala</i> <a href="#entry-zakala" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zakala" class="skt-link">n.</a> morceau, portion, bout; fragment, éclat, tesson; écaille; écorce — <a href="#entry-zakala" class="skt-link">n.</a> moitié; not. demi-lune, hémistiche, etc. <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> cannelle <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> sorte de pigment noir utilisé en teinture
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zakan">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शकन्</span> <i>śakan</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-zak_rt" class="skt-link">śakṛt</a>] <a href="#entry-zakan" class="skt-link">n.</a> excréments, ordure <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gr. σκατος.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zakita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शकित</span> <i>śakita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-zak" class="skt-link">śak</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zakita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zakita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zakitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śakitā</i> capable de ⟨inf.⟩; incapable de ⟨inf.⟩ (avec <i>na</i>, passif).<br> <i>na</i> <i>śakitaṃ</i> <i>chettum</i> on ne pouvait pas le couper.<br> <i>na</i> <i>śakitaṃ</i> <i>tena</i> (impers.) il n'en était pas capable.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaknuvat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शक्नुवत्</span> <i>śaknuvat</i> [ppr. <a href="#entry-zak" class="skt-link">śak</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zaknuvat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zaknuvat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zaknuvatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śaknuvatī</i> capable (de ⟨inf.⟩).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zakra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शक्र</span> <i>śakra</i> [<a href="#entry-zak" class="skt-link">śak</a><a href="#entry--ra" class="skt-link">-ra</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zakra" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zakra" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zakraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śakrā</i> véd. puissant, fort — <a href="#entry-zakra" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. du radieux [<a href="#entry-aaditya" class="skt-link">āditya</a>] Śakra, épith. d'<a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a> «le Puissant».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zakraavataara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śakrāvatāra</i> [<a href="#entry-avataara" class="skt-link">avatāra</a>] <a href="#entry-zakraavataara" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Śakrāvatāra, lieu dit de la «Descente d'<a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a>»; cf. <a href="#entry-zaciitiirtha" class="skt-link">śacītīrtha</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-zakra" class="skt-link">śakra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zakradhanus">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śakradhanus</i> [<a href="#entry-dhanus" class="skt-link">dhanus</a>] <a href="#entry-zakradhanus" class="skt-link">n.</a> myth. l'arc-en-ciel, attribut de puissance d'<a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zakragopa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śakragopa</i> [<a href="#entry-gopa" class="skt-link">gopa</a>] <a href="#entry-zakragopa" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. cochenille; insecte utilisé pour fabriquer une teinture rouge, le cramoisi.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zakragopaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śakragopaka</i> [<a href="#entry-gopaka" class="skt-link">gopaka</a>] <a href="#entry-zakragopaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zakta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शक्त</span> <i>śakta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-zak" class="skt-link">śak</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zakta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zakta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zaktaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śaktā</i> capable de ⟨inf. g. dat. i.⟩; puissant, fort.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zakti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शक्ति</span> <i>śakti</i> [act. <a href="#entry-zak" class="skt-link">śak</a>] <a href="#entry-zakti" class="skt-link">f.</a> pouvoir, puissance, force <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lance, hampe; javelot de fer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. la lance de <a href="#entry-Uskanda" class="skt-link">Skanda</a>, fabriquée par <a href="#entry-Uvizvakarmaa" class="skt-link">Viśvakarmā</a> en rabotant l'éclat du Soleil <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. l'énergie du feu, arme d'<a href="#entry-Uagni" class="skt-link">Agni</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. principe divin féminin, opp. <a href="#entry-vahni" class="skt-link">vahni</a>; puissance d'un dieu personnifiée <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Śakti «la Force» (de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>), Déesse suprême, épith. de <a href="#entry-Udurgaa" class="skt-link">Durgā</a> dans son aspect d'énergie cosmique <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-trika" class="skt-link">trika</a>] l'énergie créatrice suprême, qui a trois aspects: <a href="#entry-paraazakti" class="skt-link">parāśakti</a> l'énergie transcendante, <a href="#entry-paraapara" class="skt-link">parāpara</a> <a href="#entry-zakti" class="skt-link">śakti</a> l'énergie intermédiaire, et <a href="#entry-aparaa" class="skt-link">aparā</a> <a href="#entry-zakti" class="skt-link">śakti</a> l'énergie immanente <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-nyaaya" class="skt-link">nyāya</a>] sens d'un mot — <a href="#entry-zakti" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Śakti_1, fils aîné du <a href="#entry-_r_si" class="skt-link">ṛṣi</a> <a href="#entry-Uvasi_s_tha" class="skt-link">Vasiṣṭha</a> et d'<a href="#entry-Uarundhatii" class="skt-link">Arundhatī</a>; cheminant un jour sur un chemin étroit, il se trouva face au roi <a href="#entry-Ukalmaa_sapaada" class="skt-link">Kalmāṣapāda</a>, et lui demanda de s'écarter, ayant priorité comme brâhmane; le roi le frappa d'un bâton, et il le maudit; son père <a href="#entry-Uvasi_s_tha" class="skt-link">Vasiṣṭha</a> fit posséder le roi par le vampire [<a href="#entry-raak_sasa" class="skt-link">rākṣasa</a>] <a href="#entry-Ukifkara" class="skt-link">Kiṃkara</a>; <a href="#entry-Ukalmaa_sapaada" class="skt-link">Kalmāṣapāda</a> devint lui-même un vampire, et il dévora <a href="#entry-Uzakti_1" class="skt-link">Śakti_1</a> et ses 100 frères; après sa mort, sa femme <a href="#entry-Uad_rzyantii" class="skt-link">Adṛśyantī</a> accoucha de <a href="#entry-Uparaazara" class="skt-link">Parāśara</a>, qui déjà chantait le <a href="#entry-Uveda" class="skt-link">Veda</a> dans son ventre.<br> <i>śaktitrikoṇa</i> n. phil. [tantr.] triangle aqueux, la pointe en bas, symbolisant le principe femelle dans les diagrammes [<a href="#entry-yantra" class="skt-link">yantra</a>] géométriques.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaktibh_rt">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śaktibhṛt</i> [<a href="#entry-bh_rt" class="skt-link">bhṛt</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zaktibh_rt" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zaktibh_rt" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zaktibh_rt" class="skt-link">f.</a> puissant <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> np. de Śaktibhṛt, épith. de <a href="#entry-Uskanda" class="skt-link">Skanda</a> «qui porte le javelot».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaktigraha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śaktigraha</i> [<a href="#entry-graha" class="skt-link">graha</a>] <a href="#entry-zaktigraha" class="skt-link">m.</a> [«porteur de lance»] soldat.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaktimat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śaktimat</i> [<a href="#entry--mat" class="skt-link">-mat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zaktimat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zaktimat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zaktimatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śaktimatī</i> qui peut; puissant, fort <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> compétent; capable de ⟨inf. loc.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> armé d'une lance <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. (dieu) accompagné de sa parèdre, personnalisant sa puissance.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaktimattva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śaktimattva</i> [<a href="#entry--tva" class="skt-link">-tva</a>] <a href="#entry-zaktimattva" class="skt-link">n.</a> puissance.<br> <a href="#entry-zaktimat" class="skt-link">śaktimat</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaktipaata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śaktipāta</i> [<a href="#entry-paata" class="skt-link">pāta</a>] <a href="#entry-zaktipaata" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. [«chute de la Puissance»] initiation spirituelle par un maître [<a href="#entry-guru" class="skt-link">guru</a>]; transmission d'énergie spirituelle d'un maître à un disciple théorisée dans la philosophie <a href="#entry-zaiva" class="skt-link">śaiva</a> d'<a href="#entry-Uabhinavagupta" class="skt-link">Abhinavagupta</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaktipii_tha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śaktipīṭha</i> [<a href="#entry-pii_tha" class="skt-link">pīṭha</a>] <a href="#entry-zaktipii_tha" class="skt-link">n.</a> cf. <a href="#entry-pii_thasthaana" class="skt-link">pīṭhasthāna</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaktitaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śaktitā</i> [<a href="#entry--taa" class="skt-link">-tā</a>] <a href="#entry-zaktitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> ifc. pouvoir, capacité, faculté de.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaktitva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śaktitva</i> [<a href="#entry--tva" class="skt-link">-tva</a>] <a href="#entry-zaktitva" class="skt-link">n.</a> ifc. pouvoir, capacité, faculté de.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaktivaada">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śaktivāda</i> [<a href="#entry-vaada" class="skt-link">vāda</a>] <a href="#entry-zaktivaada" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Śaktivāda «Pouvoir de la parole», traité de sémantique dû à <a href="#entry-Ugadaadhara" class="skt-link">Gadādhara</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-zakti" class="skt-link">śakti</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zakuna">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शकुन</span> <i>śakuna</i> <a href="#entry-zakuna" class="skt-link">m.</a> (grand) oiseau; oiseau de proie, milan, vautour, aigle <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. oiseau de présage — <a href="#entry-zakuna" class="skt-link">n.</a> présage; bon présage; mauvais présage — <a href="#entry-zakunii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śakunī</i> oiseau femelle <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Śakunī, démon femelle qui rend les enfants malades; cf. <a href="#entry-Upuutanaa" class="skt-link">Pūtanā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zakunaj_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śakunajña</i> [<a href="#entry-j_na" class="skt-link">jña</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zakunaj_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zakunaj_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zakunaj_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śakunajñā</i> qui connaît les présages.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zakunaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śakunaka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-zakunaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zakunikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śakunikā</i> sorte de grand oiseau.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zakunazaastra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śakunaśāstra</i> [<a href="#entry-zaastra" class="skt-link">śāstra</a>] <a href="#entry-zakunazaastra" class="skt-link">n.</a> science des présages.<br> <a href="#entry-zakuna" class="skt-link">śakuna</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zakuni">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शकुनि</span> <i>śakuni</i> [<a href="#entry-zakuna" class="skt-link">śakuna</a>] <a href="#entry-zakuni" class="skt-link">m.</a> (grand) oiseau; not. milan <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. du prince <a href="#entry-gaandhaara" class="skt-link">gāndhāra</a> Śakuni «Vautour»; il est <a href="#entry-Usaubala" class="skt-link">Saubala</a>, fils du roi <a href="#entry-Usubala" class="skt-link">Subala</a>, frère de <a href="#entry-Ugaandhaarii" class="skt-link">Gāndhārī</a>, et fourbe conseiller de <a href="#entry-Uduryodhana" class="skt-link">Duryodhana</a>; il gagna pour lui le royaume de <a href="#entry-Uyudhi_s_thira" class="skt-link">Yudhiṣṭhira</a> en trichant aux dés (les dés étaient faits de l'os de la cuisse de son père, et lui obéissaient); il personnifie le <a href="#entry-dvaaparayuga" class="skt-link">dvāparayuga</a>; <a href="#entry-Uuluuka" class="skt-link">Ulūka</a> est son fils; à la fin de la grande bataille, il fut décapité par <a href="#entry-Usahadeva" class="skt-link">Sahadeva</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> astr. deuxième demi-jour lunaire [<a href="#entry-kara_na" class="skt-link">karaṇa</a>] fixe.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zakunta">
