  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शकुन्त</span> <i>śakunta</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-zakuna" class="skt-link">śakuna</a>] <a href="#entry-zakunta" class="skt-link">m.</a> oiseau; not. oiseau de proie.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zakuntalaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शकुन्तला</span> <i>śakuntalā</i> [<a href="#entry-zakunta" class="skt-link">śakunta</a><a href="#entry--la" class="skt-link">-la</a>] <a href="#entry-zakuntalaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> myth. np. de Śakuntalā «Protégée par les oiseaux», fille de <a href="#entry-Uvizvaamitra" class="skt-link">Viśvāmitra</a> et de la nymphe [<a href="#entry-apsaras" class="skt-link">apsaras</a>] <a href="#entry-Umenakaa" class="skt-link">Menakā</a>; abandonnée dans les bois à sa naissance, elle fut «protégée par les oiseaux», puis trouvée par le sage <a href="#entry-Uka_nva" class="skt-link">Kaṇva</a>, qui l'éleva à son ermitage comme sa fille; lit. c'est l'héroïne du drame <a href="#entry-Uabhij_naanazakuntalaa" class="skt-link">Abhijñānaśakuntalā</a> de <a href="#entry-Ukaalidaasa" class="skt-link">Kālidāsa</a>; le roi <a href="#entry-Udu_syanta" class="skt-link">Duṣyanta</a> l'ayant épousée à la mode <a href="#entry-gaandharva" class="skt-link">gāndharva</a> (par rapt), elle donna naissance à <a href="#entry-Ubharata" class="skt-link">Bharata</a>; par la malédiction de <a href="#entry-Udurvaasaa" class="skt-link">Durvāsā</a> le roi l'oublia, et ne la reconnut plus tard que grâce à l'anneau qu'il lui avait donné.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zakya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शक्य</span> <i>śakya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-zak" class="skt-link">śak</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zakya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zakya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zakyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śakyā</i> faisable, praticable, qui peut être mis à exécution <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> capable de ⟨inf.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> explicite <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. (sens) littéral <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-nyaaya" class="skt-link">nyāya</a>] référent, dénotation — <a href="#entry-zakya" class="skt-link">n.</a> (impers.) il est possible de ⟨inf.⟩ — ifc. qui peut être obligé à, qui peut être soumis à <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> capacité de.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zakyataa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śakyatā</i> [<a href="#entry--taa" class="skt-link">-tā</a>] <a href="#entry-zakyataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> phil. [<a href="#entry-nyaaya" class="skt-link">nyāya</a>] qualité de référent; signification.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zakyataavacchedaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śakyatāvacchedaka</i> [<a href="#entry-avacchedaka" class="skt-link">avacchedaka</a>] <a href="#entry-zakyataavacchedaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. [<a href="#entry-nyaaya" class="skt-link">nyāya</a>] délimiteur de référence.<br> <a href="#entry-zakyataa" class="skt-link">śakyatā</a> <br> <a href="#entry-zakya" class="skt-link">śakya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zal">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">शल्</span> <i>śal</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(śalati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>śalita</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>ut</i><i>)</i> sauter (avec <i>uc-</i>).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शल</span> <i>śala</i> <a href="#entry-zala" class="skt-link">m.</a> pointe, broche, lance <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> piquant de porc-épic <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. de Śala «Qui transperce», un des 100 fils de <a href="#entry-Udh_rtaraa_s_tra" class="skt-link">Dhṛtarāṣṭra</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. du roi Śala_1 «Transpercé», fils aîné du roi <a href="#entry-Upariik_sit" class="skt-link">Parīkṣit</a>; il refusa de rendre les chevaux qu'il avait empruntés à l'ermite <a href="#entry-Uvaamadeva_1" class="skt-link">Vāmadeva_1</a>; lors de leur querelle, des géants l'attaquèrent et le transpercèrent d'un trident.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zalaakaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शलाका</span> <i>śalākā</i> [<a href="#entry-zala" class="skt-link">śala</a>] <a href="#entry-zalaakaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> baguette, tige, bout de bois; règle; barre; pion <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> baleine de parasol; barreau de cage <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> aiguille de porc-épic; épine; pousse <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> dé à jouer cylindique de section quadrangulaire, d'ivoire ou d'os <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> aiguille, instrument pointu; bistouri; pinceau — <a href="#entry-zalaaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <i>śalāka</i> id. (rare).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zalaakaapari">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śalākāpari</i> [<a href="#entry-pari" class="skt-link">pari</a>] ind. coup de dé défavorable <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> manqué d'un dé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zalaakaapariik_saa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śalākāparīkṣā</i> [<a href="#entry-iik_saa" class="skt-link">īkṣā</a>] <a href="#entry-zalaakaapariik_saa" class="skt-link">f.</a> méthode traditionnelle d'évaluation d'un étudiant.<br> <a href="#entry-zalaakaapari" class="skt-link">śalākāpari</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zalaakaapuru_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śalākāpuruṣa</i> [<a href="#entry-puru_sa" class="skt-link">puruṣa</a>] <a href="#entry-zalaakaapuru_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> jn. personne éminente; la tradition en compte 63: 24 saints émancipateurs [<a href="#entry-tiirthafkara" class="skt-link">tīrthaṅkara</a>], 27 héros épiques (répartis en 9 groupes de triplets formés d'un saint [<a href="#entry-baladeva" class="skt-link">baladeva</a>], d'un héros [<a href="#entry-vaasudeva" class="skt-link">vāsudeva</a>] et d'un anti-héros [<a href="#entry-prativaasudeva" class="skt-link">prativāsudeva</a>]), et 12 empereurs [<a href="#entry-cakravartin" class="skt-link">cakravartin</a>].<br> <a href="#entry-zalaakaa" class="skt-link">śalākā</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zalaatura">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शलातुर</span> <i>śalātura</i> [<a href="#entry-zala" class="skt-link">śala</a>-<a href="#entry-aatura" class="skt-link">ātura</a>] <a href="#entry-zalaatura" class="skt-link">n.</a> géo. np. du village de Śalātura, lieu d'origine présumé de <a href="#entry-Upaa_nini" class="skt-link">Pāṇini</a>, près de mod. Peshawar.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zalabha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शलभ</span> <i>śalabha</i> <a href="#entry-zalabha" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. zoo. papillon de nuit <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> natu. sauterelle — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. md. <i>(śalabhāyate)</i> aller à une mort certaine (comme un papillon de nuit se jetant dans le feu).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zalala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śalala</i> [<a href="#entry--la" class="skt-link">-la</a>] <a href="#entry-zalala" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. porc-épic — <a href="#entry-zalala" class="skt-link">n.</a> piquant de porc-épic — <a href="#entry-zalalii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śalalī</i> id.<br> <a href="#entry-zala" class="skt-link">śala</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zallaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शल्ला</span> <i>śallā</i> var. <i>śallakī</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-zalya" class="skt-link">śalya</a>] <a href="#entry-zallaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> natu. bot. <i>Boswellia serrata</i>, arbre dont la résine sert à faire l'encens.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zallaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śallaka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-zallaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. porc-épic — <a href="#entry-zallikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śallikā</i> <a href="#entry-zallakii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śallakī</i> cf. <a href="#entry-zallaa" class="skt-link">śallā</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-zallaa" class="skt-link">śallā</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zalya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शल्य</span> <i>śalya</i> [<a href="#entry-zala" class="skt-link">śala</a>] <a href="#entry-zalya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zalya" class="skt-link">n.</a> pointe de flèche ou de lance <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> épine, écharde, piquant — <a href="#entry-zalya" class="skt-link">m.</a> porc-épic <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. de Śalya «Lancier», roi des <a href="#entry-madraas" class="skt-link">madrās</a>, frère de <a href="#entry-Umaadrii" class="skt-link">Mādrī</a>; il incarne l'<a href="#entry-asura" class="skt-link">asura</a> <a href="#entry-Usa_mhraada" class="skt-link">Saṃhrāda</a>; son emblème est le sillon [<a href="#entry-siitaa" class="skt-link">sītā</a>]; il rassembla une grande armée, promit à <a href="#entry-Uduryodhana" class="skt-link">Duryodhana</a> d'être son général en chef, et à la requête de <a href="#entry-Uyudhi_s_thira" class="skt-link">Yudhiṣṭhira</a> accepta de servir de cocher à <a href="#entry-Ukar_na" class="skt-link">Karṇa</a> dans son combat contre <a href="#entry-Uarjuna" class="skt-link">Arjuna</a>; après la mort de <a href="#entry-Ukar_na" class="skt-link">Karṇa</a> il commanda l'armée des <a href="#entry-Ukauravaas" class="skt-link">Kauravās</a>; il tua au javelot <a href="#entry-Uuttara" class="skt-link">Uttara</a>, fut attaqué par son frère <a href="#entry-Uzveta" class="skt-link">Śveta</a>, et en fut sauvé par <a href="#entry-Ubhii_sma" class="skt-link">Bhīṣma</a>; après une longue bataille inconclusive à la massue avec <a href="#entry-Ubhiima" class="skt-link">Bhīma</a>, il fut tué par <a href="#entry-Uyudhi_s_thira" class="skt-link">Yudhiṣṭhira</a> armé du missile céleste <a href="#entry-Ukaalaraatrii" class="skt-link">Kālarātrī</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Uaartaayani" class="skt-link">Ārtāyani</a> — <a href="#entry-zalyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śalyā</i> ifc. id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zalyaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śalyaka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-zalyaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. zoo. <i>Hystrix Indica</i>, porc-épic.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zalyaparvan">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śalyaparvan</i> [<a href="#entry-parvan" class="skt-link">parvan</a>] <a href="#entry-zalyaparvan" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. 9⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ livre du <a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>, «de <a href="#entry-Uzalya" class="skt-link">Śalya</a>».<br> <a href="#entry-zalya" class="skt-link">śalya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zalyatantra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śalyatantra</i> [<a href="#entry-tantra" class="skt-link">tantra</a>] <a href="#entry-zalyatantra" class="skt-link">n.</a> manuel de chirurgie.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zam_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">शम्</span> <i>śam_1</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] pr. <i>(śāmyati)</i> pft. <i>(śaśāma)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>śānta</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>upa</i>, <i>pra</i><i>)</i> travailler, se fatiguer; exercer des actes rituels <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> s'apaiser; être calme, être apaisé, être en paix <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> se reposer, se désister, renoncer; s'éteindre — ca. <i>(śamayati)</i> calmer, apaiser; se concilier (not. par un rite de conjuration) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> rendre immobile; tuer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> all. hemmen; ang. to hem.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zam_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">शम्</span> <i>śam_2</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] pr. <i>(śamayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>śānta</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>ni</i><i>)</i> [inusité sans pf.].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zam_3">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शम्</span> <i>śam_3</i> [act. <a href="#entry-zam_1" class="skt-link">śam_1</a>] part. bénédiction, bonheur; joyeusement, avec bonheur, bien.<br> <i>śaṃyoḥ</i> [<a href="#entry-yos" class="skt-link">yos</a>] véd. paix et prospérité.<br> <i>saṃ</i> <i>ca</i> <i>yoś</i> <i>ca</i> véd. id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शम</span> <i>śama</i> [act. <a href="#entry-zam_1" class="skt-link">śam_1</a>] <a href="#entry-zama" class="skt-link">m.</a> apaisement, quiétude; paix du cœur; tranquillité; pacification <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> indifférence, apathie; abandon, renonciation, extinction <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-vedaanta" class="skt-link">vedānta</a>] la tranquillité, l'une des 6 vertus cardinales [<a href="#entry-_sa_tsampad" class="skt-link">ṣaṭsampad</a>].<br> <i>śamaṃ</i> <i>kṛ</i> se calmer; être tranquille.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zamana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शमन</span> <i>śamana</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-zam_1" class="skt-link">śam_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zamana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zamana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zamanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śamanā</i> qui calme, qui apaise, qui adoucit <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui éteint — <a href="#entry-zamana" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Śamana, épith. de <a href="#entry-Uyama" class="skt-link">Yama</a> «Qui éteint (la vie)» — <a href="#entry-zamana" class="skt-link">n.</a> apaisement; extinction.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zamatha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शमथ</span> <i>śamatha</i> [<a href="#entry-zama" class="skt-link">śama</a><a href="#entry--tha" class="skt-link">-tha</a>] <a href="#entry-zamatha" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. tranquillité de l'esprit, quiétude.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zamba">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शम्ब</span> <i>śamba</i> <a href="#entry-zamba" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. l'arme d'<a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a>; parfois identifié avec le syn. <a href="#entry-vajra" class="skt-link">vajra</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> chaîne de fer autour de la ceinture, portée par certains ascètes <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> sillon de labourage à l'aller.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zambara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शम्बर</span> <i>śambara</i> <a href="#entry-zambara" class="skt-link">m.</a> espèce de cerf ou de daim <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. [RV.] np. du démon [<a href="#entry-daanava" class="skt-link">dānava</a>] Śambara (ou Śaṃvara); il avait 99 forteresses dans lesquelles il cachait le <a href="#entry-soma" class="skt-link">soma</a> et gardait prisonnier <a href="#entry-Udivodaasa" class="skt-link">Divodāsa</a>-<a href="#entry-Uatithigva" class="skt-link">Atithigva</a>; il personnifie la sécheresse; <a href="#entry-Uhira_nyakazipu" class="skt-link">Hiraṇyakaśipu</a> l'employa pour persécuter <a href="#entry-Uprahlaada" class="skt-link">Prahlāda</a>; <a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a> détruisit ses forts et le tua pour libérer le <a href="#entry-soma" class="skt-link">soma</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de l'<a href="#entry-asura" class="skt-link">asura</a> Śambara_1 qui enleva <a href="#entry-Upradyumna" class="skt-link">Pradyumna</a> enfant; ce dernier s'échappa, et devenu adulte revint pour le tuer, et épouser sa femme la magicienne <a href="#entry-Umaayaavatii" class="skt-link">Māyāvatī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zambhu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शम्भु</span> <i>śambhu</i> var. <i>śaṃbhu</i> [<a href="#entry-zam_3" class="skt-link">śam_3</a>-<a href="#entry-bhu" class="skt-link">bhu</a>] <a href="#entry-zambhu" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Śambhu «Bienheureux», nom d'un aspect propice de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] np. du <a href="#entry-daitya" class="skt-link">daitya</a> Śambhu_1, meurtrier de <a href="#entry-Ukuzadhvaja" class="skt-link">Kuśadhvaja</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zambhunaatha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śambhunātha</i> [<a href="#entry-naatha" class="skt-link">nātha</a>] <a href="#entry-zambhunaatha" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. np. de Śambhunātha, épith. de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> «Bienheureux seigneur», il est adoré not. au temple éponyme au <a href="#entry-Unepaala" class="skt-link">Nepāla</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. de Śambhunātha_1, <a href="#entry-guru" class="skt-link">guru</a> d'<a href="#entry-Uabhinavagupta" class="skt-link">Abhinavagupta</a> pour la doctrine <a href="#entry-kaula" class="skt-link">kaula</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-zambhu" class="skt-link">śambhu</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zambu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शम्बु</span> <i>śambu</i> <a href="#entry-zambu" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. coquillage bivalve; huître, moule, coque <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> var. <i>śambuka</i> id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zambuuka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शम्बूक</span> <i>śambūka</i> [<a href="#entry-zambu" class="skt-link">śambu</a>] <a href="#entry-zambuuka" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. coquillage <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] np. du <a href="#entry-zuudra" class="skt-link">śūdra</a> Śambūka, qui pratiquait fautivement l'ascèse [<a href="#entry-tapasya" class="skt-link">tapasya</a>] au <a href="#entry-dvaaparayuga" class="skt-link">dvāparayuga</a> sans initiation; cette infraction provoqua la mort du fils d'un brâhmane qui en fit reproche à <a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a>; la cause du désordre ayant été expliquée par <a href="#entry-Unaarada" class="skt-link">Nārada</a>, <a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a> lui trancha la tête, et le jeune brâhmane revint à la vie.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zami">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शमि</span> <i>śami</i> [act. <a href="#entry-zam_1" class="skt-link">śam_1</a>] <a href="#entry-zami" class="skt-link">n.</a> travail, labeur, exercice — <a href="#entry-zami" class="skt-link">f.</a> cf. <a href="#entry-zamii" class="skt-link">śamī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zamii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शमी</span> <i>śamī</i> [f. <a href="#entry-zami" class="skt-link">śami</a>] <a href="#entry-zamii" class="skt-link">f.</a> natu. bot. <i>Acacia suma</i>, mimosa; véd. son bois sert à allumer le feu sacré par friction, car «<a href="#entry-Uagni" class="skt-link">Agni</a> s'y cache»; il sert aussi pour le pal [<a href="#entry-yuupa" class="skt-link">yūpa</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zamiika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शमीक</span> <i>śamīka</i> <a href="#entry-zamiika" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. de Śamīka, fils de <a href="#entry-Uzuura" class="skt-link">Śūra</a>, héros <a href="#entry-v_r_s_ni" class="skt-link">vṛṣṇi</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zamika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śamika</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-zamika" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. du sage [<a href="#entry-_r_si" class="skt-link">ṛṣi</a>] Śamika «Travailleur», père de <a href="#entry-Umaatali" class="skt-link">Mātali</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-zami" class="skt-link">śami</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zamit_r">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शमितृ</span> <i>śamitṛ</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-zam_1" class="skt-link">śam_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zamit_r" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zamit_r" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zamitrii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śamitrī</i> qui calme son esprit <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> tueur — <a href="#entry-zamit_r" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. officiant préposé à la mise à mort des victimes sacrificielles <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> boucher.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zamita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शमित</span> <i>śamita</i> [<a href="#entry-zam_1" class="skt-link">śam_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zamita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zamita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zamitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śamitā</i> apaisé, calmé; éteint <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> détruit, tué <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> coupé (ongle).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zanais">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शनैस्</span> <i>śanais</i> [<a href="#entry-zam_1" class="skt-link">śam_1</a>] adv. tout doucement, lentement; peu à peu, au fur et à mesure, graduellement.<br> <i>śanaiḥśanais</i> adv. petit à petit, graduellement.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zanaizcara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śanaiścara</i> [<a href="#entry-cara" class="skt-link">cara</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zanaizcara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zanaizcara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zanaizcaraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śanaiścarā</i> qui se meut lentement — <a href="#entry-zanaizcara" class="skt-link">m.</a> astr. np. de Śanaiścarā, épith. de la planète Saturne et du <a href="#entry-graha" class="skt-link">graha</a> <a href="#entry-Uzani" class="skt-link">Śani</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-zanais" class="skt-link">śanais</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zani">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शनि</span> <i>śani</i> [<a href="#entry-zam_1" class="skt-link">śam_1</a>] <a href="#entry-zani" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. du <a href="#entry-graha" class="skt-link">graha</a> Śani «(Astre) Lent», régent de la planète Saturne; il est fils de <a href="#entry-Usuurya" class="skt-link">Sūrya</a> et de <a href="#entry-Uchaayaa" class="skt-link">Chāyā</a>; astr. son influence astrale est crainte, il a le mauvais œil; [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] <a href="#entry-Uraava_na" class="skt-link">Rāvaṇa</a>, craignant son influence, l'enferma dans une citerne, dont le délivra son ami <a href="#entry-Uhanumaan" class="skt-link">Hanumān</a>; son premier regard tomba sur le palais d'or de <a href="#entry-Uraava_na" class="skt-link">Rāvaṇa</a>, qui devint noir; il est représenté comme une masse noire avec des yeux brillants, ou comme un homme noir vêtu de noir; sa monture est un corbeau; cf. <a href="#entry-Ukruurad_r_t" class="skt-link">Krūradṛṭ</a>, <a href="#entry-Ukruuralocana" class="skt-link">Krūralocana</a>, <a href="#entry-Ukro_da" class="skt-link">Kroḍa</a>, <a href="#entry-Uniilaa_njanagiri" class="skt-link">Nīlāñjanagiri</a>, <a href="#entry-Umanda" class="skt-link">Manda</a>, <a href="#entry-Usaura" class="skt-link">Saura</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zanivaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śanivāra</i> [<a href="#entry-vaara" class="skt-link">vāra</a>] <a href="#entry-zanivaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> samedi.<br> <a href="#entry-zani" class="skt-link">śani</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zantu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शन्तु</span> <i>śantu</i> [<a href="#entry-zam_3" class="skt-link">śam_3</a><a href="#entry--tu" class="skt-link">-tu</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zantu" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zantu" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zantu" class="skt-link">f.</a> heureux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zap">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">शप्</span> <i>śap</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(śapati)</i> pft. <i>(śaśāpa)</i> pft. md. <i>(śepe)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>śapta</i>, <i>śapita</i><i>)</i> pfp. <i>(</i><i>śapya</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>abhi</i><i>)</i> maudire, prononcer une malédiction, lancer une imprécation <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> injurier ⟨acc.⟩ — pr. md. <i>(śapate)</i> jurer; prêter serment à ⟨dat.⟩ sur ⟨i.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> injurier, maudire ⟨dat.⟩ — ca. <i>(śāpayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>śāpita</i><i>)</i> faire jurer par ⟨i.⟩.<br> <i>śape</i> je jure.<br> <i>śap</i> <i>yadi</i> <i>...</i> s'engager à ne pas ...<br> <i>anṛtaṃ</i> <i>śap</i> faire un faux serment.<br> <i>satyena</i> <i>vai</i> <i>śape</i> En vérité je le jure.<br> <i>svayamupagatā</i> <i>śrīstyajyamanā</i> <i>śapati</i> Fortune inespérée qu'on néglige devient malédiction.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zapa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शप</span> <i>śapa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-zap" class="skt-link">śap</a>] <a href="#entry-zapa" class="skt-link">m.</a> malédiction, imprécation <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> serment.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zapatha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शपथ</span> <i>śapatha</i> [<a href="#entry-zap" class="skt-link">śap</a>] <a href="#entry-zapatha" class="skt-link">m.</a> malédiction, imprécation, anathème <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> résolution, serment, vœu.<br> <i>śapathaṃ</i> <i>kṛ</i> jurer, faire serment.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zapha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शफ</span> <i>śapha</i> <a href="#entry-zapha" class="skt-link">m.</a> sabot <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> crochet de bois pour retirer un pot du feu.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaphara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शफर</span> <i>śaphara</i> <a href="#entry-zaphara" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. zoo. <i>Cyprinus Sophore</i>, petit poisson brillant; carpe.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaphavat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śaphavat</i> [<a href="#entry--vat" class="skt-link">-vat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zaphavat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zaphavat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zaphavatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śaphavatī</i> (animal) à sabots.<br> <a href="#entry-zapha" class="skt-link">śapha</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zapita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शपित</span> <i>śapita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-zap" class="skt-link">śap</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zapita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zapita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zapitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śapitā</i> maudit.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zapta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शप्त</span> <i>śapta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-zap" class="skt-link">śap</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zapta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zapta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zaptaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śaptā</i> maudit <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> conjuré, adjuré — <a href="#entry-zapta" class="skt-link">n.</a> serment.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaptavat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शप्तवत्</span> <i>śaptavat</i> [ppa. <a href="#entry-zap" class="skt-link">śap</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zaptavat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zaptavat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zaptavatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śaptavatī</i> qui a maudit.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शर</span> <i>śara</i> [act. <a href="#entry-z_rr" class="skt-link">śṝ</a>] <a href="#entry-zara" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. bot. <i>Saccharum sara</i>, roseau (dont on fait des flèches) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> flèche <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> math. symb. le nombre 5 (pour les 5 <a href="#entry-pu_spabaa_na" class="skt-link">puṣpabāṇa</a>) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> peau du lait; cf. <a href="#entry-saras" class="skt-link">saras</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gr. καλαμος; lat. <i>calamus</i>; all. Halm; ang. halm; fr. chalumeau.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zara_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शरण</span> <i>śaraṇa</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-zri" class="skt-link">śri</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zara_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zara_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zara_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śaraṇā</i> qui protège, qui défend — <a href="#entry-zara_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> abri, sanctuaire, refuge <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> asile, protection.<br> <i>śaraṇaṃ</i> <i>gam</i> chercher refuge auprès de ⟨acc. g.⟩.<br> <i>śaraṇaṃ</i> <i>yā</i> id.<br> <i>buddhaṃ</i> <i>śaraṇaṃ</i> <i>gacchāmi</i> Je prends refuge dans le <a href="#entry-Ubuddha" class="skt-link">Buddha</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zara_naagata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śaraṇāgata</i> [<a href="#entry-aagata" class="skt-link">āgata</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zara_naagata" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zara_naagata" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zara_naagataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śaraṇāgatā</i> qui vient chercher refuge ou protection; qui demande asile, réfugié; fugitif.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zara_naagati">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śaraṇāgati</i> [<a href="#entry-aagati" class="skt-link">āgati</a>] <a href="#entry-zara_naagati" class="skt-link">f.</a> demande d'asile ou de protection <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. fait de se placer sous la protection d'une divinité.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zara_naarthin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śaraṇārthin</i> [<a href="#entry-arthin" class="skt-link">arthin</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zara_naarthin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zara_naarthin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zara_naarthinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śaraṇārthinī</i> qui cherche refuge; qui demande protection.<br> <a href="#entry-zara_na" class="skt-link">śaraṇa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zara_nada">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śaraṇada</i> [<a href="#entry-da" class="skt-link">da</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zara_nada" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zara_nada" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zara_nadaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śaraṇadā</i> qui accorde sa protection.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zara_nadeva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śaraṇadeva</i> [<a href="#entry-deva" class="skt-link">deva</a>] <a href="#entry-zara_nadeva" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. de Śaraṇadeva, poète de la cour du roi <a href="#entry-Ulak_sma_nasena" class="skt-link">Lakṣmaṇasena</a> du Bengale, à la fin du 12⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle; on lui doit la <a href="#entry-Udurgha_tav_rtti" class="skt-link">Durghaṭavṛtti</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zara_nalaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śaraṇalaya</i> [<a href="#entry-laya" class="skt-link">laya</a>] <a href="#entry-zara_nalaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> lieu d'asile; refuge, abri.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zara_nya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शरण्य</span> <i>śaraṇya</i> [<a href="#entry-zara_na" class="skt-link">śaraṇa</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zara_nya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zara_nya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zara_nyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śaraṇyā</i> qui offre l'asile, qui accorde protection (à ⟨g. iic.⟩); protecteur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui cherche asile chez ⟨iic.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zara_nyataa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śaraṇyatā</i> [<a href="#entry--taa" class="skt-link">-tā</a>] <a href="#entry-zara_nyataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> fait de donner protection.<br> <a href="#entry-zara_nya" class="skt-link">śaraṇya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaraahata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śarāhata</i> [<a href="#entry-aahata" class="skt-link">āhata</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zaraahata" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zaraahata" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zaraahataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śarāhatā</i> frappé par une flèche.<br> <a href="#entry-zara" class="skt-link">śara</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaraaru">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शरारु</span> <i>śarāru</i> a. <a href="#entry-zaraaru" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zaraaru" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zaraaru" class="skt-link">f.</a> dangereux, toxique, méchant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaraasana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śarāsana</i> [<a href="#entry-aasana" class="skt-link">āsana</a>] <a href="#entry-zaraasana" class="skt-link">n.</a> [«qui lance des flèches»] arc.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaraava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शराव</span> <i>śarāva</i> <a href="#entry-zaraava" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zaraava" class="skt-link">n.</a> assiette, écuelle; plat (en terre) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> couvercle de plat.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zarabandha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śarabandha</i> [<a href="#entry-bandha" class="skt-link">bandha</a>] <a href="#entry-zarabandha" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. poème géométrique [<a href="#entry-citrakaavya" class="skt-link">citrakāvya</a>] en forme de flèche.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zarabha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शरभ</span> <i>śarabha</i> <a href="#entry-zarabha" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. bélier Markhor aux cornes en spirale <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. <a href="#entry-zarabha" class="skt-link">śarabha</a>, animal fabuleux créé par <a href="#entry-Ubrahmaa" class="skt-link">Brahmā</a>; plus fort que le lion et l'éléphant, il a 8 pattes [<a href="#entry-a_s_tapad" class="skt-link">aṣṭapad</a>] et court à une vitesse prodigieuse; il orne notamment les colonnes des temples d'Inde du Sud, où il est appelé <i>yālī</i>; le thème du <a href="#entry-zarabha" class="skt-link">śarabha</a> dominant l'éléphant représente la victoire de l'hindouisme sur le bouddhisme; syn. <a href="#entry-vyaala" class="skt-link">vyāla</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. [SP.] forme de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>-<a href="#entry-Uviirabhadra" class="skt-link">Vīrabhadra</a> qui met fin à l'incarnation de <a href="#entry-Unarasi_mha" class="skt-link">Narasiṃha</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zarabhafga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śarabhaṅga</i> [<a href="#entry-bhafga" class="skt-link">bhaṅga</a>] <a href="#entry-zarabhafga" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] np. de l'ermite Śarabhaṅga «Briseur de flèches»; il accueillit <a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a> dans la forêt <a href="#entry-Uda_n_dakaa" class="skt-link">Daṇḍakā</a> après avoir reçu la visite d'<a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a>; il envoya <a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a>, <a href="#entry-Ulak_sma_na" class="skt-link">Lakṣmaṇa</a> et <a href="#entry-Usiitaa" class="skt-link">Sītā</a> chez l'ascète <a href="#entry-Usutiik_s_na" class="skt-link">Sutīkṣṇa</a>, puis s'immola dans le feu pour rejoindre le ciel de <a href="#entry-Ubrahmaa" class="skt-link">Brahmā</a> sous les traits d'un ange étincelant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zarabhinna">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śarabhinna</i> [<a href="#entry-bhinna" class="skt-link">bhinna</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zarabhinna" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zarabhinna" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zarabhinnaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śarabhinnā</i> transpercé d'une flèche.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zarabhopani_sad">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śarabhopaniṣad</i> [<a href="#entry-upani_sad" class="skt-link">upaniṣad</a>] <a href="#entry-zarabhopani_sad" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de la Śarabhopaniṣad, aussi appelée <a href="#entry-Upaippalaadopani_sad" class="skt-link">Paippalādopaniṣad</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-zarabha" class="skt-link">śarabha</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zarac">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शरच्</span> <i>śarac</i> iic. de <a href="#entry-zarad" class="skt-link">śarad</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaraccandra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śaraccandra</i> [<a href="#entry-candra" class="skt-link">candra</a>] <a href="#entry-zaraccandra" class="skt-link">m.</a> lune d'automne.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaracchikhin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śaracchikhin</i> [<a href="#entry-zikhin" class="skt-link">śikhin</a>] <a href="#entry-zaracchikhin" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. paon (en automne, ayant arrêté de crier).<br> <a href="#entry-zarac" class="skt-link">śarac</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zarad">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शरद्</span> <i>śarad</i> <a href="#entry-zarad" class="skt-link">f.</a> saison [<a href="#entry-_rtu" class="skt-link">ṛtu</a>] d'automne (mois d'<a href="#entry-aazvina" class="skt-link">āśvina</a> et <a href="#entry-kaarttika" class="skt-link">kārttika</a>) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> année <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. <i>śaradas</i> des années (poétique).<br> <i>śaracchatam</i> adv. pour 100 ans.<br> <i>śataṃ</i> <i>jīva</i> <i>śaradaḥ</i> Puissiez vous vivre jusqu'à 100 ans.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaradvat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śaradvat</i> [<a href="#entry--vat" class="skt-link">-vat</a>] <a href="#entry-zaradvat" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. du sage Śaradvān «(né en) Automne», fils de <a href="#entry-Uk_rpa" class="skt-link">Kṛpa</a> <a href="#entry-Ugautama" class="skt-link">Gautama</a>, né avec des flèches [<a href="#entry-zara" class="skt-link">śara</a>] à la main; il était attiré par les armes, qu'il obtint par ses austérités de <a href="#entry-brahmacaarin" class="skt-link">brahmacārin</a>; <a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a> tenta en vain de l'en détourner par la séduction de la nymphe <a href="#entry-Ujaanapadii" class="skt-link">Jānapadī</a>; sa semence tomba dans une touffe de roseau [<a href="#entry-zarastamba" class="skt-link">śarastamba</a>], où naquirent deux jumeaux, garçon et fille; le roi <a href="#entry-Uzaantanu" class="skt-link">Śāṃtanu</a> les recueillit, et les adopta sous les noms de <a href="#entry-Uk_rpa" class="skt-link">Kṛpa</a> et <a href="#entry-Uk_rpii" class="skt-link">Kṛpī</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-zarad" class="skt-link">śarad</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaralaava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śaralāva</i> [<a href="#entry-laava" class="skt-link">lāva</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zaralaava" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zaralaava" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zaralaavii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śaralāvī</i> coupeur de roseaux; débroussailleur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaras">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शरस्</span> <i>śaras</i> <a href="#entry-zaras" class="skt-link">n.</a> crème, peau du lait.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zarastamba">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śarastamba</i> [<a href="#entry-stamba" class="skt-link">stamba</a>] <a href="#entry-zarastamba" class="skt-link">m.</a> touffe de roseaux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zarat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शरत्</span> <i>śarat</i> iic. de <a href="#entry-zarad" class="skt-link">śarad</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaratkaala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śaratkāla</i> [<a href="#entry-kaala_1" class="skt-link">kāla_1</a>] <a href="#entry-zaratkaala" class="skt-link">m.</a> saison d'automne.<br> <i>śaratkālam</i> var. <i>śaratkāle</i> adv. en automne.<br> <a href="#entry-zarat" class="skt-link">śarat</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaravar_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śaravarṣa</i> [<a href="#entry-var_sa" class="skt-link">varṣa</a>] <a href="#entry-zaravar_sa" class="skt-link">n.</a> pluie de flèches.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaravar_sin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śaravarṣin</i> [<a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zaravar_sin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zaravar_sin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zaravar_si_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śaravarṣiṇī</i> qui lance une pluie de flèches.<br> <a href="#entry-zaravar_sa" class="skt-link">śaravarṣa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaravya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शरव्य</span> <i>śaravya</i> [<a href="#entry-zaru" class="skt-link">śaru</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zaravya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zaravya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zaravyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śaravyā</i> capable de blesser — <a href="#entry-zaravyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śaravyā</i> tir de flèche; déluge de flèches — <a href="#entry-zaravya" class="skt-link">n.</a> cible; but.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaravyataa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śaravyatā</i> [<a href="#entry--taa" class="skt-link">-tā</a>] <a href="#entry-zaravyataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> fait de devenir une cible; exposition au danger.<br> <a href="#entry-zaravya" class="skt-link">śaravya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zarayantraka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śarayantraka</i> [<a href="#entry-yantraka" class="skt-link">yantraka</a>] <a href="#entry-zarayantraka" class="skt-link">n.</a> ficelle servant à lier les feuilles de palme d'un manuscript.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zariira">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शरीर</span> <i>śarīra</i> [<a href="#entry-z_rr" class="skt-link">śṝ</a>] <a href="#entry-zariira" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zariira" class="skt-link">n.</a> corps humain, personne; vie <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-vedaanta" class="skt-link">vedānta</a>] le corps physique périssable, l'un des 3 corps formé par les 5 enveloppes [<a href="#entry-koza" class="skt-link">kośa</a>] de l'âme; cf. <a href="#entry-kaara_na" class="skt-link">kāraṇa</a>, <a href="#entry-suuk_sma" class="skt-link">sūkṣma</a>, <a href="#entry-sthuula" class="skt-link">sthūla</a> — <a href="#entry-zariiraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śarīrā</i> ifc. corps de.<br> <i>śarīrībhū</i> prendre corps, s'incarner.<br> <i>śarīram</i> <i>ādyaṃ</i> <i>khalu</i> <i>dharmasādhanam</i> On remplit son devoir, en vérité, en prenant soin de son corps.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zariiraka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śarīraka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-zariiraka" class="skt-link">n.</a> petit corps <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> «ce vil corps».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zariirakastuupa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śarīrakastūpa</i> [<a href="#entry-stuupa" class="skt-link">stūpa</a>] <a href="#entry-zariirakastuupa" class="skt-link">m.</a> bd. reliquaire de restes corporels de <a href="#entry-Ubuddha" class="skt-link">Buddha</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-zariiraka" class="skt-link">śarīraka</a> <br> <a href="#entry-zariira" class="skt-link">śarīra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zariirin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शरीरिन्</span> <i>śarīrin</i> [<a href="#entry-zariira" class="skt-link">śarīra</a><a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zariirin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zariirin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zariiri_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śarīriṇī</i> corporel — ifc. ayant pour corps ⟨iic.⟩ — <a href="#entry-zariirin" class="skt-link">m.</a> créature; homme.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zarkaraa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शर्करा</span> <i>śarkarā</i> <a href="#entry-zarkaraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> pl. <i>śarkarās</i> gravier, sable; calcul, bouchon <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> sucre (candi ou cristallisé) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>saccharum</i>; ang. sugar; fr. sucre; pali <i>sakharā</i>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zarman">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शर्मन्</span> <i>śarman</i> [<a href="#entry-zri" class="skt-link">śri</a><a href="#entry--man" class="skt-link">-man</a>] <a href="#entry-zarman" class="skt-link">n.</a> abri, refuge, protection; sécurité — <a href="#entry-zarman" class="skt-link">m.</a> ifc. soc. joie de, bonheur de <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. titre Śarmā formant des np. de brâhmanes.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zarmi_s_thaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शर्मिष्ठा</span> <i>śarmiṣṭhā</i> [super. <a href="#entry-zarmin" class="skt-link">śarmin</a>] <a href="#entry-zarmi_s_thaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> myth. np. de Śarmiṣṭhā «la plus Fortunée», fille de <a href="#entry-Uv_r_saparvan" class="skt-link">Vṛṣaparvan</a>, épouse de <a href="#entry-Uyayaati" class="skt-link">Yayāti</a> et mère de <a href="#entry-Upuru" class="skt-link">Puru</a>, <a href="#entry-Udruhyu" class="skt-link">Druhyu</a>, et <a href="#entry-Uanu" class="skt-link">Anu</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zarmin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शर्मिन्</span> <i>śarmin</i> [<a href="#entry-zarman" class="skt-link">śarman</a><a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zarmin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zarmin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zarmi_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śarmiṇī</i> heureux, fortuné, chanceux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaru">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शरु</span> <i>śaru</i> [<a href="#entry-zara" class="skt-link">śara</a>] <a href="#entry-zaru" class="skt-link">f.</a> trait, lance, flèche <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. missile divin, arme d'<a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a> et des génies des vents [<a href="#entry-marutas" class="skt-link">marutas</a>] — <a href="#entry-zaru" class="skt-link">f.</a> myth. cette arme personnifiée.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zarva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शर्व</span> <i>śarva</i> [<a href="#entry-zaru" class="skt-link">śaru</a>] <a href="#entry-zarva" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Śarva, épith. de <a href="#entry-Urudra" class="skt-link">Rudra</a>-<a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> «(qui tue) avec sa flèche» <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> véd. l'un des 11 <a href="#entry-rudraas" class="skt-link">rudrās</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zarvara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शर्वर</span> <i>śarvara</i> [<a href="#entry-zarva" class="skt-link">śarva</a><a href="#entry--ra" class="skt-link">-ra</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zarvara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zarvara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zarvarii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śarvarī</i> tacheté; étoilée — <a href="#entry-zarvarii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śarvarī</i> nuit (étoilée) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> crépuscule <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Śarvarī, épouse de <a href="#entry-Udo_sa" class="skt-link">Doṣa</a>, mère de <a href="#entry-Uzizumaara" class="skt-link">Śiśumāra</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. <i>śarvarās</i> myth. épith. des chevaux (ou des antilopes) des vents [<a href="#entry-marutas" class="skt-link">marutas</a>] «à la robe étoilée».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zarvavarman">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śarvavarman</i> [<a href="#entry-varman" class="skt-link">varman</a>] <a href="#entry-zarvavarman" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du grammairien Śarvavarmā; il vivait au 2⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle en Inde du Sud, sous les <a href="#entry-Usaatavaahanaas" class="skt-link">Sātavāhanās</a>; il est l'auteur du <a href="#entry-Ukaatantra" class="skt-link">Kātantra</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-zarva" class="skt-link">śarva</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zarvilaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शर्विलक</span> <i>śarvilaka</i> <a href="#entry-zarvilaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du brâhmane Śarvilaka, voleur cynique dans la pièce <a href="#entry-Um_rcchaka_tikaa" class="skt-link">Mṛcchakaṭikā</a>; il se révolte contre l'injustice du roi, et aide <a href="#entry-Uaaryaka_1" class="skt-link">Āryaka_1</a> à le renverser.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zarya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शर्य</span> <i>śarya</i> [<a href="#entry-zara" class="skt-link">śara</a>] <a href="#entry-zarya" class="skt-link">m.</a> flèche; missile — <a href="#entry-zaryaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śaryā</i> canne; tige, flèche; doigt.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zarya_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śaryaṇa</i> [<a href="#entry--na" class="skt-link">-na</a>] <a href="#entry-zarya_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> pl. bosquet de roseaux <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> véd. np. d'une région du <a href="#entry-Ukuruk_setra" class="skt-link">Kurukṣetra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zarya_naavat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śaryaṇāvat</i> [<a href="#entry--vat" class="skt-link">-vat</a>] <a href="#entry-zarya_naavat" class="skt-link">m.</a> véd. np. du Śaryaṇāvān, région d'altitude du <a href="#entry-Ukuruk_setra" class="skt-link">Kurukṣetra</a> où l'on trouve le <a href="#entry-soma" class="skt-link">soma</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-zarya_na" class="skt-link">śaryaṇa</a> <br> <a href="#entry-zarya" class="skt-link">śarya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaryaati">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शर्याति</span> <i>śaryāti</i> <a href="#entry-zaryaati" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. du roi Śaryāti, fils de <a href="#entry-Umanu" class="skt-link">Manu</a>-<a href="#entry-Uvaivasvata" class="skt-link">Vaivasvata</a>; il vit à la cour de <a href="#entry-Uyama" class="skt-link">Yama</a>; il fonda la dynastie des <a href="#entry-Uzaaryaataas" class="skt-link">Śāryātās</a>; il est père de la belle <a href="#entry-Usukanyaa" class="skt-link">Sukanyā</a>, qu'il dut marier au vieil ascète aveugle <a href="#entry-Ucyavana" class="skt-link">Cyavana</a> qu'elle avait perturbé dans ses pénitences; c'est pour <a href="#entry-Uzaryaati" class="skt-link">Śaryāti</a> que <a href="#entry-Ucyavana" class="skt-link">Cyavana</a> exécuta le <a href="#entry-yaj_na" class="skt-link">yajña</a> au cours duquel les <a href="#entry-Uazvinau" class="skt-link">Aśvinau</a> burent le <a href="#entry-soma" class="skt-link">soma</a>, en désobéissance à <a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a>; <a href="#entry-Uaanarta" class="skt-link">Ānarta</a> est son fils.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">शस्</span> <i>śas</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(śasati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>śasta</i><i>)</i> pfp. <i>(</i><i>śasya</i><i>)</i> couper, égorger, tuer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zasana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शसन</span> <i>śasana</i> [act. <a href="#entry-zas" class="skt-link">śas</a>] <a href="#entry-zasana" class="skt-link">n.</a> abattage, mise à mort.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zasta_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शस्त</span> <i>śasta_1</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-za_ms" class="skt-link">śaṃs</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zasta_1" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zasta_1" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zastaa_1" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śastā_1</i> dit, récité <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> loué, célébré, approuvé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zasta_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शस्त</span> <i>śasta_2</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-zas" class="skt-link">śas</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zasta_2" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zasta_2" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zastaa_2" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śastā_2</i> coupé, égorgé; abattu.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zasta_3">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शस्त</span> <i>śasta_3</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-zaas" class="skt-link">śās</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zasta_3" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zasta_3" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zastaa_3" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śastā_3</i> puni.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zastra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शस्त्र</span> <i>śastra</i> [instr. <a href="#entry-zas" class="skt-link">śas</a>] <a href="#entry-zastra" class="skt-link">n.</a> épée — <a href="#entry-zastra" class="skt-link">n.</a> épée, couteau; dague; arme en gén. <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> invocation, hymne — f. cf. <a href="#entry-zastrii" class="skt-link">śastrī</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>castrāre</i>; fr. castré.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zastrakarma">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śastrakarma</i> [<a href="#entry-karma" class="skt-link">karma</a>] <a href="#entry-zastrakarma" class="skt-link">n.</a> chirurgie.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zastrazaastra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śastraśāstra</i> [<a href="#entry-zaastra" class="skt-link">śāstra</a>] <a href="#entry-zastrazaastra" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Śastraśāstra ou science des armes, un <a href="#entry-upaveda" class="skt-link">upaveda</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-zastra" class="skt-link">śastra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zastrii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शस्त्री</span> <i>śastrī</i> [f. <a href="#entry-zastra" class="skt-link">śastra</a>] <a href="#entry-zastrii" class="skt-link">f.</a> dague, couteau.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zastriizyaama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śastrīśyāma</i> [<a href="#entry-zyaama" class="skt-link">śyāma</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zastriizyaama" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zastriizyaama" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zastriizyaamaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śastrīśyāmā</i> bleu acier [«bleu comme la lame d'un couteau»].<br> <a href="#entry-zastrii" class="skt-link">śastrī</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zastrin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शस्त्रिन्</span> <i>śastrin</i> [<a href="#entry-zastra" class="skt-link">śastra</a><a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zastrin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zastrin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zastri_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śastriṇī</i> qui porte une arme; armé d'une épée.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zasya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शस्य</span> <i>śasya</i> var. <i>sasya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-zas" class="skt-link">śas</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zasya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zasya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zasyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śasyā</i> à couper; à récolter; à tuer — <a href="#entry-zasya" class="skt-link">n.</a> grain, blé; récolte de grain, moisson.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zat_r">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शतृ</span> <i>śatṛ</i> nota. gram. affixe primaire [<a href="#entry-k_rt_2" class="skt-link">kṛt_2</a>] marquant le participe présent actif.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शत</span> <i>śata</i> num. <a href="#entry-zata" class="skt-link">n.</a> sg. cent, une centaine — <a href="#entry-zatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śatī</i> ifc. id. <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gr. εκατον; lat. <i>centum</i>; fr. cent.<br> <i>dve</i> <i>śate</i> var. <i>dviśata</i> deux cents.<br> <i>trīṇi</i> <i>śatāni</i> 300.<br> <i>śatāni</i> <i>trīṇi</i> 103.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zataananda">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śatānanda</i> [<a href="#entry-aananda" class="skt-link">ānanda</a>] <a href="#entry-zataananda" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] np. de l'ascète Śatānanda «joie au centuple», fils aîné de <a href="#entry-Ugautama" class="skt-link">Gautama</a>; il était chapelain [<a href="#entry-purohita" class="skt-link">purohita</a>] du roi <a href="#entry-Ujanakaraaja" class="skt-link">Janakarāja</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zataaniika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śatānīka</i> [<a href="#entry-aniika" class="skt-link">anīka</a>] <a href="#entry-zataaniika" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. de Śatānīka «à l'armée de cent braves», fils de <a href="#entry-Unakula" class="skt-link">Nakula</a> et <a href="#entry-Udraupadii" class="skt-link">Draupadī</a>; durant la grande guerre il fut tué par <a href="#entry-Uazvatthaamaa" class="skt-link">Aśvatthāmā</a>; <a href="#entry-Usahasraaniika" class="skt-link">Sahasrānīka</a> est son fils; cf. <a href="#entry-Ujayatsena" class="skt-link">Jayatsena</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. [BhP.] np. de Śatānīka_1, fils de <a href="#entry-Ujanamejaya" class="skt-link">Janamejaya</a>, père d'<a href="#entry-Uadhisiimak_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Adhisīmakṛṣṇa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zataavadhaanin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śatāvadhānin</i> [<a href="#entry-avadhaanin" class="skt-link">avadhānin</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zataavadhaanin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zataavadhaanin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zataavadhaaninii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śatāvadhāninī</i> [«qui peut faire attention à 100 choses différentes»] champion d'<a href="#entry-a_s_taavadhaana" class="skt-link">aṣṭāvadhāna</a> capable de prodiges de mémorisation.<br> <a href="#entry-zata" class="skt-link">śata</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zatabhi_saj">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śatabhiṣaj</i> [<a href="#entry-bhi_saj" class="skt-link">bhiṣaj</a>] <a href="#entry-zatabhi_saj" class="skt-link">f.</a> astr. np. de Śatabhiṣak «Qui guérit au centuple», 23⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ astérisme lunaire [<a href="#entry-nak_satra" class="skt-link">nakṣatra</a>], corr. à l'étoile λ du Verseau [<a href="#entry-kumbha" class="skt-link">kumbha</a>]; <a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a> (ou <a href="#entry-Uvaru_na" class="skt-link">Varuṇa</a>) y préside; son symbole est un cercle.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zatadhanvan">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śatadhanvan</i> [<a href="#entry-dhanvan" class="skt-link">dhanvan</a>] <a href="#entry-zatadhanvan" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. du roi <a href="#entry-yaadava" class="skt-link">yādava</a> Śatadhanvā «aux cent arcs»; à l'incitation d'<a href="#entry-Uakruura" class="skt-link">Akrūra</a> et <a href="#entry-Uk_rtavarmaa" class="skt-link">Kṛtavarmā</a> il assassina <a href="#entry-Usatraajit" class="skt-link">Satrājit</a> pour s'emparer du joyau magique <a href="#entry-Usyamantaka" class="skt-link">Syamantaka</a>; <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a> et <a href="#entry-Ubalabhadra" class="skt-link">Balabhadra</a> le poursuivirent et le tuèrent.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zatadru">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śatadru</i> [<a href="#entry-dru_2" class="skt-link">dru_2</a>] <a href="#entry-zatadru" class="skt-link">f.</a> géo. np. de la rivière Śatadru ou <a href="#entry-Uzutudrii" class="skt-link">Śutudrī</a> «Qui s'écoule en 100 (branches)»; elle descend de l'<a href="#entry-Uhimaalaya" class="skt-link">Himālaya</a> vers le Pañjāb pour se jeter dans l'Indus (mod. Sutlej); cf. <a href="#entry-Uanavatapta" class="skt-link">Anavatapta</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zatajit">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śatajit</i> [<a href="#entry-jit" class="skt-link">jit</a>] <a href="#entry-zatajit" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. du roi Śatajit «aux cent victoires», fils de <a href="#entry-Usahasrajit" class="skt-link">Sahasrajit</a>; il est père d'<a href="#entry-Uekaviira" class="skt-link">Ekavīra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zatak_rtvas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śatakṛtvas</i> [<a href="#entry-k_rtvas" class="skt-link">kṛtvas</a>] adv. 100 fois.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zataka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शतक</span> <i>śataka</i> [<a href="#entry-zata" class="skt-link">śata</a><a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zataka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zataka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zatikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śatikā</i> en cent parties <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> centième — <a href="#entry-zataka" class="skt-link">n.</a> centaine; ensemble de 100 strophes; centurie <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. recueil de cent poèmes; cf. <a href="#entry-zatakatraya" class="skt-link">śatakatraya</a>.<br> <i>sūryaśataka</i> cent hymnes au Soleil.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zatakar_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śatakarṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-kar_na" class="skt-link">karṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-zatakar_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> np. d'homme.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zatakatraya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śatakatraya</i> [<a href="#entry-traya" class="skt-link">traya</a>] <a href="#entry-zatakatraya" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Śatakatraya «Triple recueil de cent poèmes» de <a href="#entry-Ubhart_rhari_1" class="skt-link">Bhartṛhari_1</a>; il comprend le <a href="#entry-Uniitizataka" class="skt-link">Nītiśataka</a>, le <a href="#entry-Uz_rfgaarazataka" class="skt-link">Śṛṅgāraśataka</a>, et le <a href="#entry-Uvairaagyazataka" class="skt-link">Vairāgyaśataka</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-zataka" class="skt-link">śataka</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zatakratu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śatakratu</i> [<a href="#entry-kratu" class="skt-link">kratu</a>] <a href="#entry-zatakratu" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Śatakratu, épith. d'<a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a> «Qui a pratiqué 100 sacrifices».<br> <i>śatakratovivardhasva</i> Prospère, Ô <a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zatapad">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śatapad</i> [<a href="#entry-pad_2" class="skt-link">pad_2</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zatapad" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zatapad" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zatapadii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śatapadī</i> qui a cent pieds.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zatapatha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śatapatha</i> [<a href="#entry-patha" class="skt-link">patha</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zatapatha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zatapatha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zatapathaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śatapathā</i> [«qui a 100 chemins»] qui a beaucoup d'aspects.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zatapathabraahma_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śatapathabrāhmaṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-braahma_na" class="skt-link">brāhmaṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-zatapathabraahma_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Śatapatha <a href="#entry-braahma_na" class="skt-link">brāhmaṇa</a> «Cent leçons», rattaché au <a href="#entry-Uyajurveda" class="skt-link">Yajurveda</a> blanc; cf. <a href="#entry-Uyaaj_navalkya" class="skt-link">Yājñavalkya</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-zatapatha" class="skt-link">śatapatha</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zataruupaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śatarūpā</i> [<a href="#entry-ruupa" class="skt-link">rūpa</a>] <a href="#entry-zataruupaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> myth. np. de Śatarūpā «Multiforme», émanation cosmologique féminine de <a href="#entry-Ubrahmaa" class="skt-link">Brahmā</a>, à la fois créatrice (par son double masculin <a href="#entry-Uviraa_t" class="skt-link">Virāṭ</a>) et épouse de <a href="#entry-Umanu" class="skt-link">Manu</a>-<a href="#entry-Usvaayambhuva" class="skt-link">Svāyaṃbhuva</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zatasaahasra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śatasāhasra</i> [<a href="#entry-saahasra" class="skt-link">sāhasra</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zatasaahasra" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zatasaahasra" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zatasaahasraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śatasāhasrā</i> <a href="#entry-zatasaahasrii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śatasāhasrī</i> constitué de cent mille — <a href="#entry-zatasaahasra" class="skt-link">n.</a> cent-millième part.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zatasaahasraka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śatasāhasraka</i> [<a href="#entry-saahasraka" class="skt-link">sāhasraka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zatasaahasraka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zatasaahasraka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zatasaahasrikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śatasāhasrikā</i> constitué de cent mille.<br> <i>śatasāhasrikā</i> <i>prajñāpāramitā</i> bd. longue version de la <a href="#entry-Upraj_naapaaramitaa" class="skt-link">Prajñāpāramitā</a>, en 100000 vers [<a href="#entry-suutra" class="skt-link">sūtra</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zatasahasra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śatasahasra</i> [<a href="#entry-sahasra" class="skt-link">sahasra</a>] <a href="#entry-zatasahasra" class="skt-link">n.</a> cent mille.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zatasahasraka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śatasahasraka</i> [<a href="#entry-sahasraka" class="skt-link">sahasraka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zatasahasraka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zatasahasraka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zatasahasrikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śatasahasrikā</i> constitué de cent mille.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zatasahasrazas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śatasahasraśas</i> [<a href="#entry--zas" class="skt-link">-śas</a>] ind. par centaines de milliers.<br> <a href="#entry-zatasahasra" class="skt-link">śatasahasra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zataz_rfga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śataśṛṅga</i> [<a href="#entry-z_rfga" class="skt-link">śṛṅga</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zataz_rfga" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zataz_rfga" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zataz_rfgaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śataśṛṅgā</i> qui a cent cîmes — <a href="#entry-zataz_rfga" class="skt-link">n.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. de Śataśṛṅga, la montagne «aux Cent Cîmes», où se retira le roi <a href="#entry-Upaa_n_du" class="skt-link">Pāṇḍu</a> quand il devint ermite.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zatazas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śataśas</i> [<a href="#entry--zas" class="skt-link">-śas</a>] adv. par centaines; cent fois.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zatika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शतिक</span> <i>śatika</i> [<a href="#entry-zata" class="skt-link">śata</a><a href="#entry--ika" class="skt-link">-ika</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zatika" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zatika" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zatikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śatikā</i> au nombre de cent; contenant cent; au prix de cent; relatif à cent.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zatru">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शत्रु</span> <i>śatru</i> <a href="#entry-zatru" class="skt-link">m.</a> (qui attaque, qui renverse) ennemi, rival, agresseur, envahisseur; not. roi voisin hostile — ifc. hostile envers, qui vainc <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> (a. <a href="#entry-bahuvriihi" class="skt-link">bahuvrīhi</a>) vaincu par <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gr. κοτος; ang. hate.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zatrughna">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śatrughna</i> [<a href="#entry-ghna" class="skt-link">ghna</a>] <a href="#entry-zatrughna" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Śatrughna «Qui vainc l'ennemi», fils de <a href="#entry-Udazaratha" class="skt-link">Daśaratha</a> et de <a href="#entry-Usumitraa" class="skt-link">Sumitrā</a>, frère jumeau de <a href="#entry-Ulak_sma_na" class="skt-link">Lakṣmaṇa</a>, compagnon de son demi-frère <a href="#entry-Ubharata_1" class="skt-link">Bharata_1</a>; il tua les <a href="#entry-asuraas" class="skt-link">asurās</a> <a href="#entry-Umadhu_2" class="skt-link">Madhu_2</a> et <a href="#entry-Ulava_na" class="skt-link">Lavaṇa</a>; on lui attribue la fondation de la ville de <a href="#entry-Umathuraa" class="skt-link">Mathurā</a>, qu'il donna à son fils <a href="#entry-Usubaahu_1" class="skt-link">Subāhu_1</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zatrum">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शत्रुम्</span> <i>śatrum</i> [acc. <a href="#entry-zatru" class="skt-link">śatru</a>] iic.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zatrunibarha_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śatrunibarhaṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-nibarha_na" class="skt-link">nibarhaṇa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zatrunibarha_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zatrunibarha_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zatrunibarha_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śatrunibarhaṇā</i> qui extermine ses ennemis.<br> <a href="#entry-zatru" class="skt-link">śatru</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zatrutaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śatrutā</i> [<a href="#entry--taa" class="skt-link">-tā</a>] <a href="#entry-zatrutaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> hostilité, inimitié.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zatru_njaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śatruṃjaya</i> var. <i>śatruñjaya</i> [<a href="#entry-jaya" class="skt-link">jaya</a>] <a href="#entry-zatru_mjaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> Śatruñjaya «Victoire sur l'ennemi», np. d'homme, de roi, d'éléphant <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> géo. épith. du Mont <a href="#entry-Uvimalaadri" class="skt-link">Vimalādri</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-zatrum" class="skt-link">śatrum</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zatya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शत्य</span> <i>śatya</i> [<a href="#entry-zata" class="skt-link">śata</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zatya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zatya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zatyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śatyā</i> constitué d'une centaine <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui peut être acquis pour 100 (roupies).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zau_n_da">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शौण्ड</span> <i>śauṇḍa</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-zu_n_daa" class="skt-link">śuṇḍā</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zau_n_da" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zau_n_da" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zau_n_daa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śauṇḍā</i> ivre, intoxiqué — ifc. dépendant de; intoxiqué à — <a href="#entry-zau_n_da" class="skt-link">m.</a> ivrogne <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> paon — <a href="#entry-zau_n_daa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śauṇḍā</i> alcool, liqueur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zau_n_dika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शौण्डिक</span> <i>śauṇḍika</i> [<a href="#entry-zau_n_da" class="skt-link">śauṇḍa</a><a href="#entry--ika" class="skt-link">-ika</a>] <a href="#entry-zau_n_dika" class="skt-link">m.</a> fabriquant ou vendeur d'alcool — <a href="#entry-zau_n_dikii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śauṇḍikī</i> tenancière de débit d'alcool.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zauca">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शौच</span> <i>śauca</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-zuc_1" class="skt-link">śuc_1</a>] <a href="#entry-zauca" class="skt-link">n.</a> pureté, propreté; honnêteté; purification <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. la pureté, une des disciplines [<a href="#entry-niyama" class="skt-link">niyama</a>] du <a href="#entry-yoga" class="skt-link">yoga</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaudra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शौद्र</span> <i>śaudra</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-zuudra" class="skt-link">śūdra</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zaudra" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zaudra" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zaudraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śaudrā</i> relatif à la classe <a href="#entry-zuudra" class="skt-link">śūdra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zauka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शौक</span> <i>śauka</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-zuka" class="skt-link">śuka</a>] <a href="#entry-zauka" class="skt-link">n.</a> envol de perroquets.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zauna">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शौन</span> <i>śauna</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-zuuna" class="skt-link">śūna</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zauna" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zauna" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zaunii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śaunī</i> relatif au chien.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zauna_hzepa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शौनःशेप</span> <i>śaunaḥśepa</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-zuna_hzepa" class="skt-link">śunaḥśepa</a>] <a href="#entry-zauna_hzepa" class="skt-link">n.</a> myth. [AB.] histoire de <a href="#entry-Uzuna_hzepa" class="skt-link">Śunaḥśepa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaunaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शौनक</span> <i>śaunaka</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-zunaka" class="skt-link">śunaka</a>] <a href="#entry-zaunaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] patr. du chef de clan [<a href="#entry-kulapati" class="skt-link">kulapati</a>] Śaunaka «issu de <a href="#entry-Uzunaka" class="skt-link">Śunaka</a>», descendant de <a href="#entry-Ubh_rgu" class="skt-link">Bhṛgu</a>; on lui attribue le système des 4 classes [<a href="#entry-var_na" class="skt-link">varṇa</a>]; il dirigeait la session sacrificielle lors de laquelle <a href="#entry-Uugrazravaa" class="skt-link">Ugraśravā</a> <a href="#entry-Usauti_2" class="skt-link">Sauti_2</a> conta le <a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>; on dit que <a href="#entry-Ug_rtsamada" class="skt-link">Gṛtsamada</a> et <a href="#entry-Uparamaara" class="skt-link">Paramāra</a> sont ses fils <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. [MO.] np. d'un riche maître de maison à la recherche de savoir; il questionna <a href="#entry-Uafgiraa" class="skt-link">Aṅgirā</a> sur la connaissance suprême <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. de Śaunaka_1, grammairien du 4⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle ant.; il établit une recension éponyme de l'<a href="#entry-Uatharvaveda" class="skt-link">Atharvaveda</a> avec son <a href="#entry-padapaa_tha" class="skt-link">padapāṭha</a> [<a href="#entry-Ucaturaadhyaayikaa" class="skt-link">Caturādhyāyikā</a>], et le <a href="#entry-praatizaakhya" class="skt-link">prātiśākhya</a> du <a href="#entry-U_rgveda" class="skt-link">Ṛgveda</a>; la tradition l'identifie au précédent <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. sa recension de l'<a href="#entry-Uatharvaveda" class="skt-link">Atharvaveda</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. [DKC.] np. de Śaunaka_2, qui épousa <a href="#entry-Uaaryadaasii" class="skt-link">Āryadāsī</a>; <a href="#entry-Ukaamapaala" class="skt-link">Kāmapāla</a> en est une réincarnation.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaunakiiya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śaunakīya</i> [<a href="#entry--iiya" class="skt-link">-īya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zaunakiiya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zaunakiiya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zaunakiiyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śaunakīyā</i> composé par <a href="#entry-Uzaunaka_1" class="skt-link">Śaunaka_1</a> — <a href="#entry-zaunakiiya" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. recension de l'<a href="#entry-Uatharvaveda" class="skt-link">Atharvaveda</a> par <a href="#entry-Uzaunaka_1" class="skt-link">Śaunaka_1</a>; syn. <a href="#entry-zaunakiiyasa_mhitaa" class="skt-link">śaunakīyasaṃhitā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaunakiiyasa_mhitaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
