    <i>śaunakīyasaṃhitā</i> [<a href="#entry-sa_mhitaa" class="skt-link">saṃhitā</a>] <a href="#entry-zaunakiiyasa_mhitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de la Śaunakīyasaṃhitā, recension de l'<a href="#entry-Uatharvaveda" class="skt-link">Atharvaveda</a> due à <a href="#entry-Uzaunaka_1" class="skt-link">Śaunaka_1</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Ucaturaadhyaayikaa" class="skt-link">Caturādhyāyikā</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-zaunakiiya" class="skt-link">śaunakīya</a> <br> <a href="#entry-zaunaka" class="skt-link">śaunaka</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaura">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शौर</span> <i>śaura</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-zuura" class="skt-link">śūra</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zaura" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zaura" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zaurii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śaurī</i> héroïque.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaurasena">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शौरसेन</span> <i>śaurasena</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-zuurasena" class="skt-link">śūrasena</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zaurasena" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zaurasena" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zaurasenii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śaurasenī</i> géo. relatif au peuple des Śūrasena — <a href="#entry-zaurasenii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śaurasenī</i> leur langue, <a href="#entry-praak_rta" class="skt-link">prākṛta</a> de la région de <a href="#entry-Umathuraa" class="skt-link">Mathurā</a>; elle est utilisée dans le théâtre sanskrit pour les femmes de haut rang; elle est l'ancêtre du hindi.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zauri">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शौरि</span> <i>śauri</i> [<a href="#entry-zaura" class="skt-link">śaura</a>] <a href="#entry-zauri" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Śauri, patr. de <a href="#entry-Uvasudeva" class="skt-link">Vasudeva</a> «fils de <a href="#entry-Uzuura" class="skt-link">Śūra</a>» <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. épith. de <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a> ou de <a href="#entry-Ubaladeva" class="skt-link">Baladeva</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaurya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शौर्य</span> <i>śaurya</i> [<a href="#entry-zaura" class="skt-link">śaura</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] <a href="#entry-zaurya" class="skt-link">n.</a> héroïsme; valeur; prouesse.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शव</span> <i>śava</i> <a href="#entry-zava" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zava" class="skt-link">n.</a> cadavre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zavaaruu_dha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śavārūḍha</i> [<a href="#entry-aaruu_dha" class="skt-link">ārūḍha</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zavaaruu_dha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zavaaruu_dha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zavaaruu_dhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śavārūḍhā</i> se dit d'une divinité ayant pour monture un cadavre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zavaasana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śavāsana</i> [<a href="#entry-aasana" class="skt-link">āsana</a>] <a href="#entry-zavaasana" class="skt-link">n.</a> phil. [<a href="#entry-yoga" class="skt-link">yoga</a>] posture de relaxation, allongé sur le sol, paumes de main vers le haut, dos adhérant au sol <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. [<a href="#entry-tantra" class="skt-link">tantra</a>] posture d'accouplement «comme un cadavre», où le partenaire féminin est seul actif.<br> <a href="#entry-zava" class="skt-link">śava</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zavarga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शवर्ग</span> <i>śavarga</i> [<i>(śa)</i>-<a href="#entry-varga" class="skt-link">varga</a>] <a href="#entry-zavarga" class="skt-link">m.</a> phon. la classe des sibilantes.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zavya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शव्य</span> <i>śavya</i> [<a href="#entry-zava" class="skt-link">śava</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] <a href="#entry-zavya" class="skt-link">n.</a> soc. crémation; funérailles.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शय</span> <i>śaya</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-zii_1" class="skt-link">śī_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zaya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zayaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śayā</i> ifc. gisant, couché, étendu sur; dormant sur — <a href="#entry-zaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> sommeil <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit, couche — <a href="#entry-zayaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śayā</i> endroit de repos.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zayana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शयन</span> <i>śayana</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-zii_1" class="skt-link">śī_1</a>] <a href="#entry-zayana" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit, couche <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> sommeil, repos.<br> <i>śayanīkṛ</i> se faire une couche de.<br> <i>śayanāya</i> <i>icchati</i> il veut s'allonger pour dormir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zayanag_rha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śayanagṛha</i> [<a href="#entry-g_rha" class="skt-link">gṛha</a>] <a href="#entry-zayanag_rha" class="skt-link">n.</a> chambre à coucher.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zayanaracana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śayanaracana</i> [<a href="#entry-racana" class="skt-link">racana</a>] <a href="#entry-zayanaracana" class="skt-link">n.</a> l'arrangement de la couche, l'un des 64 arts [<a href="#entry-kalaa" class="skt-link">kalā</a>].<br> <a href="#entry-zayana" class="skt-link">śayana</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zayaniiya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शयनीय</span> <i>śayanīya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] <a href="#entry-zii_1" class="skt-link">śī_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zayaniiya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zayaniiya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zayaniiyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śayanīyā</i> servant à dormir ou à se reposer — <a href="#entry-zayaniiya" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit, couche.<br> <i>śayanīyam</i> adv. (impers.) il faut dormir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zayita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शयित</span> <i>śayita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-zii_1" class="skt-link">śī_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zayita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zayita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zayitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śayitā</i> gisant, étendu, couché; qui repose, qui dort; endormi.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zayitavya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शयितव्य</span> <i>śayitavya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">3</span>] <a href="#entry-zii_1" class="skt-link">śī_1</a>] <a href="#entry-zayitavya" class="skt-link">n.</a> (impers.) il faut se reposer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zayyaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शय्या</span> <i>śayyā</i> [act. <a href="#entry-zii_1" class="skt-link">śī_1</a>] <a href="#entry-zayyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit, couche <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> repos, sommeil.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaz">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">शश्</span> <i>śaś</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(śaśati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>śaśita</i><i>)</i> bondir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zaza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शश</span> <i>śaśa</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-zaz" class="skt-link">śaś</a>] <a href="#entry-zaza" class="skt-link">m.</a> lièvre, lapin <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> tache de la Lune en forme de lièvre <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> type d'homme; cf. <a href="#entry-pa_ncamanu_syavibhaaga" class="skt-link">pañcamanuṣyavibhāga</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zazaada">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śaśāda</i> [<a href="#entry-ada" class="skt-link">ada</a>] <a href="#entry-zazaada" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Śaśāda, épith. de <a href="#entry-Uvikuk_si" class="skt-link">Vikukṣi</a> «Mangeur du lièvre (destiné au sacrifice)».<br> <a href="#entry-zaza" class="skt-link">śaśa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zazaafka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śaśāṅka</i> [<a href="#entry-afka" class="skt-link">aṅka</a>] <a href="#entry-zazaafka" class="skt-link">m.</a> la Lune [«qui porte l'image d'un lièvre»] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. du roi Śaśāṅka du <a href="#entry-Ugau_da" class="skt-link">Gauḍa</a> (fin du 6⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle); allié secret du roi <a href="#entry-Udevagupta" class="skt-link">Devagupta</a> du <a href="#entry-Umaalava" class="skt-link">Mālava</a>, il assassina traîtreusement le roi <a href="#entry-Uraajya" class="skt-link">Rājya</a> <a href="#entry-Uvardhana_1" class="skt-link">Vardhana_1</a> en 606.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zazabindu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śaśabindu</i> [<a href="#entry-bindu" class="skt-link">bindu</a>] <a href="#entry-zazabindu" class="skt-link">m.</a> lune [«qui a une tache en forme de lièvre»] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. du roi Śaśabindu, fils de <a href="#entry-Ucitraratha" class="skt-link">Citraratha</a>, père de <a href="#entry-Ubindumatii" class="skt-link">Bindumatī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zazadhara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śaśadhara</i> [<a href="#entry-dhara" class="skt-link">dhara</a>] <a href="#entry-zazadhara" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. de Śaśadhara (1275–1325), philosophe de la connaissance; il investigua la notion de concomitance [<a href="#entry-vyaapti" class="skt-link">vyāpti</a>]; son ouvrage <a href="#entry-Unyaayasiddhaantadiipa" class="skt-link">Nyāyasiddhāntadīpa</a> influença <a href="#entry-Ugafgeza" class="skt-link">Gaṅgeśa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zazaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śaśaka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-zazaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> (petit) lièvre, lapin.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zazi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शशि</span> <i>śaśi</i> iic. de <a href="#entry-zazin" class="skt-link">śaśin</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zazimauli">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śaśimauli</i> [<a href="#entry-mauli" class="skt-link">mauli</a>] <a href="#entry-zazimauli" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. épith. de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> «Portant la lune en diadème».<br> <a href="#entry-zazi" class="skt-link">śaśi</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zazin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शशिन्</span> <i>śaśin</i> [<a href="#entry-zaza" class="skt-link">śaśa</a><a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] <a href="#entry-zazin" class="skt-link">m.</a> lune [«qui porte un lièvre»].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zazvat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शश्वत्</span> <i>śaśvat</i> [<a href="#entry-zaz" class="skt-link">śaś</a><a href="#entry--vat" class="skt-link">-vat</a>] adv. toujours; perpétuellement; continuellement, constamment <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> tout de suite, là dessus.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zcandra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">श्चन्द्र</span> <i>ścandra</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-candra" class="skt-link">candra</a>] ifc. a. <a href="#entry-zcandra" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zcandra" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zcandraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ścandrā</i> qui brille.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zcota">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">श्चोत</span> <i>ścota</i> [act. <a href="#entry-zcut_1" class="skt-link">ścut_1</a>] <a href="#entry-zcota" class="skt-link">m.</a> fait de suinter; fuite; arrosage.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zcotana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">श्चोतन</span> <i>ścotana</i> [act. <a href="#entry-zcut_1" class="skt-link">ścut_1</a>] <a href="#entry-zcotana" class="skt-link">n.</a> fait de suinter, de couler.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zcut_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">श्चुत्</span> <i>ścut_1</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(ścotati)</i> aor. [<span class="skt-grammar">5</span>] <i>(aścotīt)</i> aor. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] <i>(aścutat)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>śscutita</i><i>)</i> suinter; couler; distiller <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> verser; asperger; arroser — ca. <i>(ścotayati)</i> faire couler.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zcut_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">श्चुत्</span> <i>ścut_2</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-zcut_1" class="skt-link">ścut_1</a>] ifc. a. <a href="#entry-zcut_2" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zcut_2" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zcut_2" class="skt-link">f.</a> qui suinte, qui exsude, qui distille; qui arrose.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zcutita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">श्चुतित</span> <i>ścutita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-zcut_1" class="skt-link">ścut_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zcutita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zcutita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zscutitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śscutitā</i> suinté, exsudé; versé; arrosé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ze">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शे</span> <i>śe</i> cf. <a href="#entry-zii_1" class="skt-link">śī_1</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ze_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शेष</span> <i>śeṣa</i> [obj. <a href="#entry-zi_s" class="skt-link">śiṣ</a>] <a href="#entry-ze_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ze_sa" class="skt-link">n.</a> résidu, reste <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. not. reste des oblations aux dieux du <a href="#entry-paakayaj_na" class="skt-link">pākayajña</a>, mangé par le maître de maison <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> fin, conclusion; souvenir <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Śeṣa «Vestige», fils de <a href="#entry-Ukazyapa" class="skt-link">Kaśyapa</a> et de <a href="#entry-Ukadruu" class="skt-link">Kadrū</a>, roi des dragons [<a href="#entry-naaga" class="skt-link">nāga</a>] symbolisant le résidu de l'Univers entre deux <a href="#entry-kalpa" class="skt-link">kalpa</a> et le germe de la création; pendant le <a href="#entry-kalpa" class="skt-link">kalpa</a> il soutient le monde; au <a href="#entry-pralaya" class="skt-link">pralaya</a>, il vomit le feu qui détruit tout; pendant le sommeil cosmique, <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a>-<a href="#entry-Unaaraaya_na" class="skt-link">Nārāyaṇa</a> repose sur lui; il s'incarna sur terre en <a href="#entry-Ulak_sma_na" class="skt-link">Lakṣmaṇa</a> et <a href="#entry-Ubalaraama" class="skt-link">Balarāma</a>; on dit aussi qu'il s'incarna comme <a href="#entry-Ucaraka" class="skt-link">Caraka</a> pour guérir les hommes, et comme <a href="#entry-Upata_njali" class="skt-link">Patañjali</a> pour apporter le sanskrit et le yoga; cf. <a href="#entry-Uananta" class="skt-link">Ananta</a>, <a href="#entry-Uanantaziir_saa" class="skt-link">Anantaśīrṣā</a>, <a href="#entry-Uaadize_sa" class="skt-link">Ādiśeṣa</a>, <a href="#entry-Upha_niindra" class="skt-link">Phaṇīndra</a>, <a href="#entry-Uvaasuki" class="skt-link">Vāsuki</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> onyx; soc. cette pierre est considérée comme maléfique <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. [<a href="#entry-Ukaadambarii" class="skt-link">Kādambarī</a>] np. de Śeṣa_1 «Résiduel», collier de perles à l'éclat sans égal, dernier trésor issu du barratage primordial; il fut donné par <a href="#entry-Uvaru_na" class="skt-link">Varuṇa</a> à <a href="#entry-Ucitraratha" class="skt-link">Citraratha</a>; <a href="#entry-Ukaadambarii" class="skt-link">Kādambarī</a> l'offrit à <a href="#entry-Ucandraapii_da_1" class="skt-link">Candrāpīḍa_1</a>.<br> <i>śeṣe</i> adv. gram. dans les autres cas.<br> <i>na</i> <i>śeṣaṃ</i> <i>āpnuvanti</i> il n'en reste plus.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ze_saadri">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śeṣādri</i> [<a href="#entry-adri" class="skt-link">adri</a>] <a href="#entry-ze_saadri" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du grammairien Śeṣādri.<br> <a href="#entry-ze_sa" class="skt-link">śeṣa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ze_sabhaava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śeṣabhāva</i> [<a href="#entry-bhaava" class="skt-link">bhāva</a>] <a href="#entry-ze_sabhaava" class="skt-link">m.</a> état résiduel ou auxiliaire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ze_sak_r_s_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śeṣakṛṣṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-k_r_s_na" class="skt-link">kṛṣṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-ze_sak_r_s_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. de Śeṣakṛṣṇa, premier <a href="#entry-guru" class="skt-link">guru</a> de <a href="#entry-Ubha_t_toji" class="skt-link">Bhaṭṭoji</a> <a href="#entry-Udiik_sita" class="skt-link">Dīkṣita</a>; on lui doit la <a href="#entry-Udharmaanubandhizlokacaturdazii" class="skt-link">Dharmānubandhiślokacaturdaśī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ze_sazaayin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śeṣaśāyin</i> [<a href="#entry-zaayin" class="skt-link">śāyin</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ze_sazaayin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ze_sazaayin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ze_sazaayinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śeṣaśāyinī</i> myth. syn. de <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a>-<a href="#entry-Uanantazaayin" class="skt-link">Anantaśāyin</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zekhara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शेखर</span> <i>śekhara</i> [<a href="#entry-zikhara" class="skt-link">śikhara</a>] <a href="#entry-zekhara" class="skt-link">m.</a> sommet du crâne, diadème, couronne <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pic, sommet; pinacle <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. de Śekhara <a href="#entry-Ukaamandaki" class="skt-link">Kāmandaki</a>; il vivait sous <a href="#entry-Ucandragupta_2" class="skt-link">Candragupta_2</a> à la fin du 4⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle — ifc. le meilleur, le plus beau des.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zekharaapii_dayojana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śekharāpīḍayojana</i> [<a href="#entry-aapii_da" class="skt-link">āpīḍa</a>-<a href="#entry-yojana" class="skt-link">yojana</a>] <a href="#entry-zekharaapii_dayojana" class="skt-link">n.</a> l'arrangement de diadèmes et de chapelets, l'un des 64 arts [<a href="#entry-kalaa" class="skt-link">kalā</a>].<br> <a href="#entry-zekhara" class="skt-link">śekhara</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zepa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शेप</span> <i>śepa</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-sapa" class="skt-link">sapa</a>] <a href="#entry-zepa" class="skt-link">m.</a> pénis; syn. <a href="#entry-zepha" class="skt-link">śepha</a> — <a href="#entry-zepas" class="skt-link">n.</a> <i>śepas</i> id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zepaana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शेपान</span> <i>śepāna</i> [ppft. md. <a href="#entry-zap" class="skt-link">śap</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zepaana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zepaana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zepaanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śepānā</i> ayant juré.<br> <i>śepāno</i> <i>'smi</i> j'ai juré.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zepha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शेफ</span> <i>śepha</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-zepa" class="skt-link">śepa</a>] <a href="#entry-zepha" class="skt-link">m.</a> verge, pénis; scrotum — <a href="#entry-zephas" class="skt-link">n.</a> <i>śephas</i> id. <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> du. <i>śephau</i> testicules.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zephaalikaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शेफालिका</span> <i>śephālikā</i> <a href="#entry-zephaalikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> natu. bot. <i>Nyctanthes arbortristis</i>, jasmin nocturne; syn. <a href="#entry-paarijaata" class="skt-link">pārijāta</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zephahar_sa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śephaharṣaṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-har_sa_na" class="skt-link">harṣaṇa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zephahar_sa_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zephahar_sa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zephahar_sa_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śephaharṣaṇī</i> [«qui dresse la verge»] aphrodisiaque, provoquant l'érection.<br> <a href="#entry-zepha" class="skt-link">śepha</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zeva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शेव</span> <i>śeva</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-ziva" class="skt-link">śiva</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zeva" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zeva" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zevaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śevā</i> véd. amical, cher; précieux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zi_mzapaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शिंशपा</span> <i>śiṃśapā</i> <a href="#entry-zi_mzapaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> natu. bot. <i>Dalbergia sissoo</i>, grand arbre Sissou, aux fleurs jaune pâle; il est employé en médecine; son bois, dense et rose veiné, est employé en menuiserie de qualité; lit. le cadavre/vampire du <a href="#entry-Uvetaalapa_ncavi_mzatikaa" class="skt-link">Vetālapañcaviṃśatikā</a> était sur un Sissou; <a href="#entry-Uhanumaan" class="skt-link">Hanumān</a> observa <a href="#entry-Usitaa" class="skt-link">Sitā</a> prisonnière à <a href="#entry-Ulafkaa" class="skt-link">Laṅkā</a> du haut d'un Sissou.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zi_mzumaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शिंशुमार</span> <i>śiṃśumāra</i> [<a href="#entry-zizumaara" class="skt-link">śiśumāra</a>] <a href="#entry-zi_mzumaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. zoo. <i>Platanista Gangetica</i>, dauphin du Gange.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zi_s">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">शिष्</span> <i>śiṣ</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">7</span>] pr. <i>(śinaṣṭi)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>śiṣṭa</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>ava</i>, <i>ut</i>, <i>pari</i>, <i>vi</i><i>)</i> laisser — ps. <i>(śiṣyate)</i> rester — ca. <i>(śeṣayati)</i> faire rester; épargner.<br> <i>na</i> <i>śiṣyate</i> manquer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zi_s_ta_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शिष्ट</span> <i>śiṣṭa_1</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-zi_s" class="skt-link">śiṣ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zi_s_ta_1" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zi_s_ta_1" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zi_s_taa_1" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śiṣṭā_1</i> laissé, resté — <a href="#entry-zi_s_ta_1" class="skt-link">n.</a> résidu, reste.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zi_s_ta_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शिष्ट</span> <i>śiṣṭa_2</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-zaas" class="skt-link">śās</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zi_s_ta_2" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zi_s_ta_2" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zi_s_taa_2" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śiṣṭā_2</i> châtié, puni <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> ordonné, commandé; enseigné, prescrit <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> éduqué, instruit; poli, bien élevé; cultivé, éminent.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zi_s_taacaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śiṣṭācāra</i> [<a href="#entry-aacaara" class="skt-link">ācāra</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zi_s_taacaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zi_s_taacaara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zi_s_taacaaraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śiṣṭācārā</i> poli, bien élevé — <a href="#entry-zi_s_taacaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> bonnes manières; (formule de) politesse.<br> <a href="#entry-zi_s_ta_2" class="skt-link">śiṣṭa_2</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zi_s_taprayoga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śiṣṭaprayoga</i> [<a href="#entry-prayoga" class="skt-link">prayoga</a>] <a href="#entry-zi_s_taprayoga" class="skt-link">m.</a> pratique correcte (not. de la grammaire [<a href="#entry-vyaakara_na" class="skt-link">vyākaraṇa</a>]) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. bon usage, forme linguistique attestée.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zi_s_ti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शिष्टि</span> <i>śiṣṭi</i> [act. <a href="#entry-zaas" class="skt-link">śās</a>] <a href="#entry-zi_s_ti" class="skt-link">f.</a> correction, punition <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> langage châtié, parole correcte, enseignement autorisé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zi_sya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शिष्य</span> <i>śiṣya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-zaas" class="skt-link">śās</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zi_sya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zi_sya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zi_syaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śiṣyā</i> qui doit être éduqué — <a href="#entry-zi_sya" class="skt-link">m.</a> élève, apprenti, disciple.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zibi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शिबि</span> <i>śibi</i> <a href="#entry-zibi" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. du sage royal [<a href="#entry-raajar_si" class="skt-link">rājarṣi</a>] Śibi ou Śivi, descendant d'<a href="#entry-Uuziinara" class="skt-link">Uśīnara</a> et <a href="#entry-Umaadhavii" class="skt-link">Mādhavī</a>, roi des <a href="#entry-Uuziinaraas" class="skt-link">Uśīnarās</a>; il est le symbole de l'altruisme désintéressé et du dévouement surhumain; pour sauver <a href="#entry-Uagni" class="skt-link">Agni</a> transformé en colombe des coups d'<a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a> transformé en faucon, il offrit en poids égal des morceaux de son propre corps; un jour un brâhmane vint à son palais pour mendier de la nourriture; à sa demande, <a href="#entry-Uzibi" class="skt-link">Śibi</a> tua son fils unique <a href="#entry-Ub_rhadgarbha" class="skt-link">Bṛhadgarbha</a> pour lui préparer le repas; bien que le brâhmane ait incendié son palais, il fit bonne figure et le servit; lorsque le brâhmane demanda qu'il partage ce repas, alors qu'il était végétarien, il acquiesca; <a href="#entry-Ubrahmaa" class="skt-link">Brahmā</a> qui était venu le tester se révéla alors, et lui rendit son fils et son palais <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. <i>śibayas</i> myth. ses descendants; cf. <a href="#entry-zaibya" class="skt-link">śaibya</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de l'<a href="#entry-asura" class="skt-link">asura</a> Śibi_1, fils de <a href="#entry-Usa_mhraada" class="skt-link">Saṃhrāda</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zibicarita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śibicarita</i> [<a href="#entry-carita" class="skt-link">carita</a>] <a href="#entry-zibicarita" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. histoire de Śibi, épisode du livre 3 du <a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> var. <i>śibicaritra</i> id.<br> <a href="#entry-zibi" class="skt-link">śibi</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zibikaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शिबिका</span> <i>śibikā</i> <a href="#entry-zibikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> palanquin; litière <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> estrade.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zibira">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शिबिर</span> <i>śibira</i> <a href="#entry-zibira" class="skt-link">n.</a> résidence royale; camp royal; tente royale <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> tente; camp retranché <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> mod. [«camp d'étude»] atelier, séminaire.<br> <i>saṃbhāṣaṇaśibira</i> mod. camp d'étude du sanskrit parlé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zigru">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शिग्रु</span> <i>śigru</i> <a href="#entry-zigru" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. bot. <i>Moringa oleifera</i>, arbre Moringa; ses feuilles, riches en protéines, sont utilisées en médecine [<a href="#entry-aayurveda" class="skt-link">āyurveda</a>] — <a href="#entry-zigru" class="skt-link">n.</a> ses graines.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zii_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">शी</span> <i>śī_1</i> var. <i>śe</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] pr. md. <i>(śete)</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. md. <i>(śayate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>śayita</i><i>)</i> pfp. <i>(</i><i>śayanīya</i>, <i>śayitavya</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>ati</i>, <i>adhi</i>, <i>anu</i>, <i>ā</i>, <i>upa</i>, <i>vi</i>, <i>sam</i><i>)</i> être étendu, être couché, être gisant; reposer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> se reposer, dormir; se coucher, s'endormir — ca. <i>(śāyayati)</i> faire dormir, envoyer dormir — dés. <i>(śiśayiṣate)</i> avoir sommeil <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>quiesco</i>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zii_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शी</span> <i>śī_2</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-zii_1" class="skt-link">śī_1</a>] ifc. a. <a href="#entry-zii_2" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zii_2" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zii_2" class="skt-link">f.</a> qui repose sur — <a href="#entry-zii_2" class="skt-link">f.</a> repos de; tranquillité de.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ziidhu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शीधु</span> <i>śīdhu</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-siidhu" class="skt-link">sīdhu</a>] <a href="#entry-ziidhu" class="skt-link">m.</a> rhum.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ziighra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शीघ्र</span> <i>śīghra</i> a. <a href="#entry-ziighra" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ziighra" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ziighraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śīghrā</i> rapide; opp. <a href="#entry-manda" class="skt-link">manda</a> — <a href="#entry-ziighra" class="skt-link">n.</a> astr. conjonction — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. md. <i>(śīghrāyate)</i> accélérer.<br> <i>śīghram</i> adv. rapidement, vite, en vitesse.<br> <i>śīghreṇa</i> adv. id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ziighraga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śīghraga</i> [<a href="#entry-ga_1" class="skt-link">ga_1</a>] <a href="#entry-ziighraga" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] np. du roi d'<a href="#entry-Uayodhyaa" class="skt-link">Ayodhyā</a> Śīghraga «Rapide», fils d'<a href="#entry-Uagnivar_na" class="skt-link">Agnivarṇa</a>, père de <a href="#entry-Umaru_1" class="skt-link">Maru_1</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ziighratara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śīghratara</i> [<a href="#entry--tara" class="skt-link">-tara</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ziighratara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ziighratara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ziighrataraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śīghratarā</i> compar. plus rapide.<br> <a href="#entry-ziighra" class="skt-link">śīghra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ziikara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शीकर</span> <i>śīkara</i> <a href="#entry-ziikara" class="skt-link">m.</a> pl. <i>śīkarās</i> pluie fine, bruine.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ziil">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">शील्</span> <i>śīl</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(śīlati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>śīlita</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>pari</i><i>)</i> servir, révérer; pratiquer, agir — ca. <i>(śīlayati)</i> pratiquer intensément, s'engager dans; exercer, cultiver.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ziila">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शील</span> <i>śīla</i> [act. <a href="#entry-ziil" class="skt-link">śīl</a>] <a href="#entry-ziila" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ziila" class="skt-link">n.</a> habitude, caractère, disposition, nature <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pratique morale; bonne conduite, moralité, piété, vertu <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> bd. la vertu, une des 6 perfections [<a href="#entry-paaramitaa" class="skt-link">pāramitā</a>] d'un accompli [<a href="#entry-bodhisattva" class="skt-link">bodhisattva</a>] — <a href="#entry-ziilaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śīlā</i> ifc. habitué à, qui pratique, adonné à.<br> <i>māṃsaśīla</i> qui mange usuellement de la viande; carnivore.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ziilaaditya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śīlāditya</i> [<a href="#entry-aaditya" class="skt-link">āditya</a>] <a href="#entry-ziilaaditya" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du roi du <a href="#entry-Umaalava" class="skt-link">Mālava</a> Śīlāditya «Soleil de vertu», fils de <a href="#entry-Uyazodharmaa" class="skt-link">Yaśodharmā</a> (6⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle); bouddhiste vertueux, il émigra au <a href="#entry-Ukazmiira" class="skt-link">Kaśmīra</a>, où il redevint roi en 540.<br> <a href="#entry-ziila" class="skt-link">śīla</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ziilagu_nopeta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śīlaguṇopeta</i> [<a href="#entry-gu_na" class="skt-link">guṇa</a>-<a href="#entry-upeta" class="skt-link">upeta</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ziilagu_nopeta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ziilagu_nopeta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ziilagu_nopetaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śīlaguṇopetā</i> bd. [«qui possède la qualité de vertu»] vertueux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ziilana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शीलन</span> <i>śīlana</i> [act. <a href="#entry-ziil" class="skt-link">śīl</a>] <a href="#entry-ziilana" class="skt-link">n.</a> pratique assidue, étude incessante (not du savoir [<a href="#entry-zaastra" class="skt-link">śāstra</a>]); dévotion.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ziilavat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śīlavat</i> [<a href="#entry--vat" class="skt-link">-vat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ziilavat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ziilavat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ziilavatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śīlavatī</i> de bonne moralité, vertueux — <a href="#entry-ziilavatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śīlavatī</i> myth. np. de Śīlavatī «la Pieuse», épouse d'<a href="#entry-Uugrazravaa" class="skt-link">Ugraśravā</a>; pour sauver celui-ci de la malédiction de <a href="#entry-Umaa_n_davya" class="skt-link">Māṇḍavya</a>, elle empêcha le Soleil de se lever; elle fut apaisée par <a href="#entry-Uanasuuyaa" class="skt-link">Anasūyā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ziir_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शीर्ण</span> <i>śīrṇa</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-z_rr" class="skt-link">śṝ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ziir_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ziir_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ziir_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śīrṇā</i> brisé, déchiré, broyé; fané, détruit; tombé (dent) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pourri, gâté.<br> <i>śīrṇīkṛ</i> endommager, blesser.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ziir_natva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śīrṇatva</i> [<a href="#entry--tva" class="skt-link">-tva</a>] <a href="#entry-ziir_natva" class="skt-link">n.</a> pourriture.<br> <a href="#entry-ziir_na" class="skt-link">śīrṇa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ziir_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शीर्ष</span> <i>śīrṣa</i> [<a href="#entry-ziras" class="skt-link">śiras</a>] <a href="#entry-ziir_sa" class="skt-link">n.</a> tête, crâne; partie supérieure, front; partie antérieure, début <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> var. <i>śīrṣan</i> véd. id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ziir_sa_nya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śīrṣaṇya</i> [<a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ziir_sa_nya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ziir_sa_nya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ziir_sa_nyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śīrṣaṇyā</i> phon. [«issu du crâne»] cérébral (phonème); syn. <a href="#entry-muurdhanya" class="skt-link">mūrdhanya</a>.<br> <i>śīrṣaṇyāḥ</i> crinière.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ziir_saavaze_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śīrṣāvaśeṣa</i> [<a href="#entry-avaze_sa" class="skt-link">avaśeṣa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ziir_saavaze_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ziir_saavaze_sa" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ziir_saavaze_saa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śīrṣāvaśeṣā</i> dont il ne reste que la tête.<br> <a href="#entry-ziir_sa" class="skt-link">śīrṣa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ziir_saka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शीर्षक</span> <i>śīrṣaka</i> [<a href="#entry-ziir_sa" class="skt-link">śīrṣa</a><a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ziir_saka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ziir_saka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ziir_sakaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śīrṣakā</i> supérieur, en tête <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. qui connaît le vers <a href="#entry-Uziraa" class="skt-link">Śirā</a> — <a href="#entry-ziir_saka" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Śīrṣaka, épith. de <a href="#entry-Uraahu" class="skt-link">Rāhu</a> «réduit à une tête» — <a href="#entry-ziir_saka" class="skt-link">n.</a> tête, crâne <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> sommet; casque, chapeau; guirlande portée sur la tête <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. jugement, verdict, sentence.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ziit">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शीत्</span> <i>śīt</i> cf. <a href="#entry-siit" class="skt-link">sīt</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ziita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शीत</span> <i>śīta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-zyaa" class="skt-link">śyā</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ziita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ziita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ziitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śītā</i> froid, frais — <a href="#entry-ziita" class="skt-link">n.</a> le froid, gel, hiver.<br> <i>śītāḥ</i> eau froide (ellipse de <a href="#entry-aapas" class="skt-link">āpas</a>).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ziitaa_mzu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śītāṃśu</i> [<a href="#entry-a_mzu" class="skt-link">aṃśu</a>] <a href="#entry-ziitaa_mzu" class="skt-link">m.</a> lune (aux rayons froids); opp. <a href="#entry-gharmaa_mzu" class="skt-link">gharmāṃśu</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-ziita" class="skt-link">śīta</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ziitaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śītaka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ziitaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ziitaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ziitikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śītikā</i> paresseux; opp. <a href="#entry-u_s_naka" class="skt-link">uṣṇaka</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ziitala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शीतल</span> <i>śītala</i> [<a href="#entry-ziita" class="skt-link">śīta</a><a href="#entry--la" class="skt-link">-la</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ziitala" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ziitala" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ziitalaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śītalā</i> froid, frais, rafraîchissant <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> glacé; glaçant <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> sans passion, calme — <a href="#entry-ziitala" class="skt-link">m.</a> Lune <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> camphre — f. cf. <a href="#entry-ziitalaa" class="skt-link">śītalā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ziitalaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शीतला</span> <i>śītalā</i> [f. <a href="#entry-ziitala" class="skt-link">śītala</a>] <a href="#entry-ziitalaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> variole <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de la déesse Śītalā «la Froide», personnifiant la variole; on prie pour sa protection; sa monture est un âne.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ziitalaasaptamii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śītalāsaptamī</i> [<a href="#entry-saptamii" class="skt-link">saptamī</a>] <a href="#entry-ziitalaasaptamii" class="skt-link">f.</a> soc. festival du 7⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ jour de la quinzaine claire [<a href="#entry-zuklapak_sa" class="skt-link">śuklapakṣa</a>] du mois de <a href="#entry-maagha" class="skt-link">māgha</a>, consacré à la déesse <a href="#entry-Uziitalaa" class="skt-link">Śītalā</a>; on doit n'y consommer que des aliments froids.<br> <a href="#entry-ziitalaa" class="skt-link">śītalā</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ziitalanaatha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śītalanātha</i> [<a href="#entry-naatha" class="skt-link">nātha</a>] <a href="#entry-ziitalanaatha" class="skt-link">m.</a> jn. np. de Śītalanātha «Maître du froid», 10⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ <a href="#entry-tiirthafkara" class="skt-link">tīrthaṅkara</a> mythique; son corps est jaune d'or, son symbole est le <a href="#entry-svastika" class="skt-link">svastika</a> ou une feuille de pipal.<br> <a href="#entry-ziitala" class="skt-link">śītala</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ziitapitta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śītapitta</i> [<a href="#entry-pitta" class="skt-link">pitta</a>] <a href="#entry-ziitapitta" class="skt-link">n.</a> méd. maladie de peau; not. urticaire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ziitapuutanaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śītapūtanā</i> [<a href="#entry-puutanaa" class="skt-link">pūtanā</a>] <a href="#entry-ziitapuutanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> myth. np. de Śītapūtanā «Puante glaciale», horrible <a href="#entry-pizaacii" class="skt-link">piśācī</a> qui dérobe les fœtus.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zik_s">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शिक्ष्</span> <i>śikṣ</i> [dés. <a href="#entry-zak" class="skt-link">śak</a>] v. pr. md. <i>(śikṣate)</i> v. pr. <i>(śikṣati)</i> fut. <i>(</i><i>śayiṣyati</i><i>)</i> pft. <i>(śiśikṣa)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>śikṣita</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>anu</i>, <i>upa</i><i>)</i> apprendre, étudier — ca. <i>(śikṣayati)</i> instruire, apprendre qqc. à qqn. ⟨2 acc.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zik_sa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शिक्षण</span> <i>śikṣaṇa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-zik_s" class="skt-link">śikṣ</a>] <a href="#entry-zik_sa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> étude; acquisition de connaissances <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> instruction, enseignement (en ⟨loc. iic.⟩).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zik_sa_niiya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शिक्षणीय</span> <i>śikṣaṇīya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] <a href="#entry-zik_s" class="skt-link">śikṣ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zik_sa_niiya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zik_sa_niiya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zik_sa_niiyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śikṣaṇīyā</i> qui doit être enseigné.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zik_saa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शिक्षा</span> <i>śikṣā</i> [act. <a href="#entry-zik_s" class="skt-link">śikṣ</a>] <a href="#entry-zik_saa" class="skt-link">f.</a> désir d'accomplir <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> apprentissage, étude; connaissance, art; science en ⟨loc. iic.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. la récitation ou phonétique, un des 6 traités annexes du <a href="#entry-Uveda" class="skt-link">Veda</a> [<a href="#entry-vedaafga" class="skt-link">vedāṅga</a>]; cf. <a href="#entry-Uaapizalizik_saa" class="skt-link">Āpiśaliśikṣā</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. cf. <a href="#entry-paa_niniiyazik_saa" class="skt-link">pāṇinīyaśikṣā</a> — <a href="#entry-zik_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <i>śikṣa</i> myth. np. de Śikṣa «Savant», un roi <a href="#entry-gandharva" class="skt-link">gandharva</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zik_saa_s_taka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śikṣāṣṭaka</i> [<a href="#entry-a_s_taka" class="skt-link">aṣṭaka</a>] <a href="#entry-zik_saa_s_taka" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Śikṣāṣṭaka, poème de 8 vers de quatre strophes dû à <a href="#entry-Ucaitanya" class="skt-link">Caitanya</a>, et résumant son enseignement.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zik_saaguru">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śikṣāguru</i> [<a href="#entry-guru" class="skt-link">guru</a>] <a href="#entry-zik_saaguru" class="skt-link">m.</a> maître qui enseigne (opp. <a href="#entry-diik_saaguru" class="skt-link">dīkṣāguru</a>, qui initie).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zik_saasamuccaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śikṣāsamuccaya</i> [<a href="#entry-samuccaya" class="skt-link">samuccaya</a>] <a href="#entry-zik_saasamuccaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Śikṣāsamuccaya «Compendium didactique», œuvre philosophique bd. de <a href="#entry-Uzaantideva" class="skt-link">Śāntideva</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-zik_saa" class="skt-link">śikṣā</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zik_saavat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śikṣāvat</i> [<a href="#entry--vat" class="skt-link">-vat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zik_saavat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zik_saavat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zik_saavatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śikṣāvatī</i> instruit; savant <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> instructif.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zik_saavrata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śikṣāvrata</i> [<a href="#entry-vrata" class="skt-link">vrata</a>] <a href="#entry-zik_saavrata" class="skt-link">n.</a> jn. vœu disciplinaire, exercice spirituel d'un renonçant <a href="#entry-jaina" class="skt-link">jaina</a>; on en compte quatre: la pratique de la méditation [<a href="#entry-saamaayika" class="skt-link">sāmāyika</a>], l'évitement de certains lieux [<a href="#entry-dezaavakaazika" class="skt-link">deśāvakāśika</a>], l'expérience de vie ascétique d'une journée [<a href="#entry-po_sadhopavaasa" class="skt-link">poṣadhopavāsa</a>] et le partage avec un hôte [<a href="#entry-atithisa_mvibhaaga" class="skt-link">atithisaṃvibhāga</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zik_saka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शिक्षक</span> <i>śikṣaka</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-zik_s" class="skt-link">śikṣ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zik_saka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zik_saka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zik_sikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śikṣikā</i> qui instruit — <a href="#entry-zik_saka" class="skt-link">m.</a> instructeur; enseignant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zik_sita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शिक्षित</span> <i>śikṣita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-zik_s" class="skt-link">śikṣ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zik_sita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zik_sita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zik_sitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śikṣitā</i> appris, étudié <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui a appris ⟨acc.⟩; instruit, exercé en ⟨acc. loc. iic.⟩ — <a href="#entry-zik_sita" class="skt-link">n.</a> enseignement, instruction.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zikha_n_da">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शिखण्ड</span> <i>śikhaṇḍa</i> [<a href="#entry-zikhaa" class="skt-link">śikhā</a>] <a href="#entry-zikha_n_da" class="skt-link">m.</a> touffe de poils; mèche de cheveux au sommet de la tête <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> crête; plumet, aigrette <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> queue de paon.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zikha_n_daka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śikhaṇḍaka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-zikha_n_daka" class="skt-link">m.</a> id. <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> mèche de cheveux; groupe de 3 ou 5 mèches réservées dans la coiffure des guerriers.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zikha_n_din">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śikhaṇḍin</i> [<a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] <a href="#entry-zikha_n_din" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. de Śikhaṇḍī «Portant la mèche», héros des <a href="#entry-Upaa_ncaalaas" class="skt-link">Pāñcālās</a>, en lequel se réincarna <a href="#entry-Uambaa" class="skt-link">Ambā</a> comme transsexuel [<a href="#entry-kliiba" class="skt-link">klība</a>]; il affronta <a href="#entry-Ubhii_sma" class="skt-link">Bhīṣma</a> au combat; celui-ci refusant de combattre une femme, il servit de bouclier à <a href="#entry-Uarjuna" class="skt-link">Arjuna</a> qui porta le coup fatal à son aïeul; ainsi il vengea l'humiliation d'<a href="#entry-Uambaa" class="skt-link">Ambā</a>; il fut attaqué dans son sommeil et tué par <a href="#entry-Uazvatthaamaa" class="skt-link">Aśvatthāmā</a> — <a href="#entry-zikha_n_dinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śikhaṇḍinī</i> myth. np. de Śikhaṇḍinī, fille du roi <a href="#entry-Udrupada" class="skt-link">Drupada</a> qui devint le transsexuel <a href="#entry-Uzikha_n_dii" class="skt-link">Śikhaṇḍī</a> par échange de son sexe avec le <a href="#entry-yak_sa" class="skt-link">yakṣa</a> <a href="#entry-Usthuu_nakar_na" class="skt-link">Sthūṇakarṇa</a>; épith. injurieuse employée par <a href="#entry-Uuluuka" class="skt-link">Ulūka</a> pour désigner <a href="#entry-Uzikha_n_dii" class="skt-link">Śikhaṇḍī</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-zikha_n_da" class="skt-link">śikhaṇḍa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zikhaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शिखा</span> <i>śikhā</i> <a href="#entry-zikhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> pointe <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> aigrette, toupet, crête; touffe de cheveux <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. mèche réservée lors de la tonsure d'un <a href="#entry-braahma_na" class="skt-link">brāhmaṇa</a>; elle est portée not. par les disciples de <a href="#entry-Ucaitanya" class="skt-link">Caitanya</a>; syn. <a href="#entry-cuu_daa" class="skt-link">cūḍā</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [tantr.] la <a href="#entry-zikhaa" class="skt-link">śikhā</a> représente l'espace intermédiaire entre le <a href="#entry-brahmarandhra" class="skt-link">brahmarandhra</a> et le <a href="#entry-dvaadazaanta" class="skt-link">dvādaśānta</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> mamelon; pointe du pied <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> flamme, rayon <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. intérêt [<a href="#entry-v_rddhaa" class="skt-link">vṛddhā</a>] [«poussant comme une mèche de cheveux»], payable quotidiennement à un taux usuraire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zikhaama_ni">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śikhāmaṇi</i> [<a href="#entry-ma_ni" class="skt-link">maṇi</a>] <a href="#entry-zikhaama_ni" class="skt-link">m.</a> joyau porté sur la tête — ifc. splendeur de (titre not. d'ouvrages).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zikhaamu_n_da">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śikhāmuṇḍa</i> [<a href="#entry-mu_n_da" class="skt-link">muṇḍa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zikhaamu_n_da" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zikhaamu_n_da" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zikhaamu_n_daa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śikhāmuṇḍā</i> qui a la tête rasée, à l'exception d'une mèche de cheveux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zikhaamuula">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śikhāmūla</i> [<a href="#entry-muula" class="skt-link">mūla</a>] <a href="#entry-zikhaamuula" class="skt-link">n.</a> [«racine avec une touffe»] carotte, navet.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zikhaavat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śikhāvat</i> [<a href="#entry--vat" class="skt-link">-vat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zikhaavat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zikhaavat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zikhaavatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śikhāvatī</i> pointu; brûlant, flamboyant — <a href="#entry-zikhaavat" class="skt-link">m.</a> feu, lampe <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> astr. comète — adv. pareil à une crête.<br> <a href="#entry-zikhaa" class="skt-link">śikhā</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zikhara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शिखर</span> <i>śikhara</i> [<a href="#entry-zikhaa" class="skt-link">śikhā</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zikhara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zikhara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zikharaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śikharā</i> pointu, dentelé — <a href="#entry-zikhara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zikhara" class="skt-link">n.</a> sommet, pic; pointe; faîte, pinnacle <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> arch. tour principale au-dessus du sanctuaire d'un temple <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> rubis.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zikharin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śikharin</i> [<a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zikharin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zikharin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zikhari_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śikhariṇī</i> pointu; couronné; muni d'une crête — <a href="#entry-zikharin" class="skt-link">m.</a> montagne — <a href="#entry-zikhari_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śikhariṇī</i> lit. nom d'un mètre de <a href="#entry-kaavya" class="skt-link">kāvya</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-zikhara" class="skt-link">śikhara</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zikhin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शिखिन्</span> <i>śikhin</i> [<a href="#entry-zikhaa" class="skt-link">śikhā</a><a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zikhin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zikhin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zikhinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śikhinī</i> qui a un toupet ou une aigrette <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> brûlant — <a href="#entry-zikhin" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. zoo. paon <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> feu.<br> <i>yatnād</i> <i>api</i> <i>kaḥ</i> <i>paśyec</i> <i>chikhinām</i> <i>āhāraniḥsaraṇamārgam</i> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> <i>yadi</i> <i>jaladadhvanimuditās</i> <i>ta</i> <i>eva</i> <i>mūḍhā</i> <i>na</i> <i>nṛtyeyuḥ</i> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> [PT.] Quelque effort qu'on fasse, on ne verra jamais le cul d'un paon, à moins que cet idiot ne fasse la roue, mis en joie par le grondement de l'orage.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zila">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शिल</span> <i>śila</i> <a href="#entry-zila" class="skt-link">m.</a> glânage (d'épis).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zilaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शिला</span> <i>śilā</i> <a href="#entry-zilaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> pierre, rocher <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pierre inférieure d'une meule; syn. <a href="#entry-d_r_sad" class="skt-link">dṛṣad</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zilaacaarika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śilācārika</i> [<a href="#entry-caarika" class="skt-link">cārika</a>] <a href="#entry-zilaacaarika" class="skt-link">m.</a> épigraphe.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zilaahaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śilāhāra</i> [<a href="#entry-aahaara" class="skt-link">āhāra</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zilaahaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zilaahaara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zilaahaaraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śilāhārā</i> glâneur; qui se nourrit du glânage — <a href="#entry-zilaahaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. de la dynastie Śilāhāra du nord de l'ouest du Deccan; elle se réclamait de descendre du <a href="#entry-vidyaadhara" class="skt-link">vidyādhara</a> <a href="#entry-Ujiimuutavaahana" class="skt-link">Jīmūtavāhana</a>; au 13⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle, elle s'établit dans le <a href="#entry-Ukofka_na" class="skt-link">Koṅkaṇa</a> comme puissance maritime réglant le commerce à l'Ouest avec les arabes, au détriment de leur voisins [<a href="#entry-samanta" class="skt-link">samanta</a>] <a href="#entry-Uraa_s_trakuu_ta" class="skt-link">Rāṣṭrakūṭa</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Uaparaarka" class="skt-link">Aparārka</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zilaahaarin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śilāhārin</i> [<a href="#entry-aahaarin" class="skt-link">āhārin</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zilaahaarin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zilaahaarin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zilaahaari_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śilāhāriṇī</i> glaneur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zilaak_sara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śilākṣara</i> [<a href="#entry-k_sara" class="skt-link">kṣara</a>] <a href="#entry-zilaak_sara" class="skt-link">n.</a> lithographie.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zilaaku_t_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śilākuṭṭa</i> [<a href="#entry-ku_t_ta" class="skt-link">kuṭṭa</a>] <a href="#entry-zilaaku_t_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> broyage au mortier.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zilaanyaasa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śilānyāsa</i> [<a href="#entry-nyaasa" class="skt-link">nyāsa</a>] <a href="#entry-zilaanyaasa" class="skt-link">m.</a> arch. pose de la première pierre d'un édifice.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zilaapa_t_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śilāpaṭṭa</i> [<a href="#entry-pa_t_ta" class="skt-link">paṭṭa</a>] <a href="#entry-zilaapa_t_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> pierre plate, dalle; meule.<br> <a href="#entry-zilaa" class="skt-link">śilā</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zilo_ncha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śiloñcha</i> [<a href="#entry-u_ncha" class="skt-link">uñcha</a>] <a href="#entry-zilo_ncha" class="skt-link">m.</a> du. <i>siloñchau</i> glânage d'épis et de grains <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> subsistance par des moyens de fortune.<br> <a href="#entry-zila" class="skt-link">śila</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zilpa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शिल्प</span> <i>śilpa</i> <a href="#entry-zilpa" class="skt-link">n.</a> décoration; œuvre d'art <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> art plastique, métier; la tradition en énumère 64.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zilpazaastra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śilpaśāstra</i> [<a href="#entry-zaastra" class="skt-link">śāstra</a>] <a href="#entry-zilpazaastra" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. de l'<a href="#entry-upaveda" class="skt-link">upaveda</a> Śilpaśāstra «Science des arts», traité définissant les règles de représentation artistique.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zilpin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śilpin</i> [<a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zilpin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zilpin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zilpinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śilpinī</i> artisan, artiste, ouvrier; not. sculpteur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zilpiraaja">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śilpirāja</i> [<a href="#entry-raaja" class="skt-link">rāja</a>] <a href="#entry-zilpiraaja" class="skt-link">m.</a> roi parmi les artistes.<br> <a href="#entry-zilpin" class="skt-link">śilpin</a> <br> <a href="#entry-zilpa" class="skt-link">śilpa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ziluu_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शिलूष</span> <i>śilūṣa</i> <a href="#entry-ziluu_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. bot. <i>Ægle marmelos</i>; syn. <a href="#entry-bilva" class="skt-link">bilva</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zimba">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शिम्ब</span> <i>śimba</i> <a href="#entry-zimba" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zimbaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śimbā</i> cosse, gousse.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zimbi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शिम्बि</span> <i>śimbi</i> [<a href="#entry-zimba" class="skt-link">śimba</a>] <a href="#entry-zimbi" class="skt-link">f.</a> cosse, gousse <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> <i>śimbī</i> id. <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> natu. bot. <i>Phaseolus Trilobus</i>, légumineuse employée pour le fourrage et en médecine pour guérir l'hépatite.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ziras">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शिरस्</span> <i>śiras</i> <a href="#entry-ziras" class="skt-link">n.</a> tête; crâne <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> partie supérieure, sommet; avant-garde; début (d'un vers) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. np. du vers Śirā «<i>āpo</i> <i>jyotir</i> <i>āpo</i> <i>'mṛtam</i>» [Baudhāyana] — ifc. chef, premier des <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gr. χαρα; lat. <i>cerebrum</i>; fr. cerveau, cérébral.<br> <i>uṣā</i> <i>vā</i> <i>aśvasya</i> <i>medhyasya</i> <i>śiraḥ</i> [BU.] La tête du cheval du sacrifice, en vérité, est l'Aube.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zirii_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शिरीष</span> <i>śirīṣa</i> <a href="#entry-zirii_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. bot. <i>Acacia sirissa</i> ou bot. <i>Albizia lebbeck</i>, arbre au bois noir et aux fleurs blanches odorantes; il attire les perroquets — <a href="#entry-zirii_sa" class="skt-link">n.</a> sa fleur; les femmes en font des pendants d'oreille; la douceur de ses pétales évoque en poésie la tendresse.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zirii_sava_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śirīṣavaṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-vana" class="skt-link">vana</a>] <a href="#entry-zirii_sava_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> bois d'acacias.<br> <a href="#entry-zirii_sa" class="skt-link">śirīṣa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ziroma_ni">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śiromaṇi</i> [<a href="#entry-ma_ni" class="skt-link">maṇi</a>] <a href="#entry-ziroma_ni" class="skt-link">m.</a> joyau de coiffure ou de turban; diadême <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> titre d'ouvrage, somme littéraire ou scientifique; principal ouvrage sur un sujet <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> np. de Śiromaṇi, titre d'érudit [<a href="#entry-pa_n_dita" class="skt-link">paṇḍita</a>]; cf. <a href="#entry-Uraghunaatha_1" class="skt-link">Raghunātha_1</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zirorekhaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śirorekhā</i> [<a href="#entry-rekhaa" class="skt-link">rekhā</a>] <a href="#entry-zirorekhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> ligne supérieure de l'écriture <a href="#entry-devanaagarii" class="skt-link">devanāgarī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zirovrata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śirovrata</i> [<a href="#entry-vrata" class="skt-link">vrata</a>] <a href="#entry-zirovrata" class="skt-link">n.</a> soc. [MO.] vœu de renoncement, marqué par le rasage de la tête.<br> <a href="#entry-ziras" class="skt-link">śiras</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शित</span> <i>śita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-zaa" class="skt-link">śā</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śitā</i> aiguisé, tranchant <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> mince, maigre, émacié; faible.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zithila">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शिथिल</span> <i>śithila</i> a. <a href="#entry-zithila" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zithila" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zithilaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śithilā</i> lâche, relâché, desserré, délié, libre <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> flasque, mou — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. <i>(śithilayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>śithilita</i><i>)</i> rendre lâche, relâcher, desserrer; défaire (ses vêtements) — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. md. <i>(śithilayate)</i> négliger — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. md. <i>(śithilāyate)</i> se relâcher; s'amollir, s'affaiblir.<br> <i>śithilīkṛ</i> se relâcher; se relaxer.<br> <i>śithilībhū</i> se relâcher, se défaire; être à l'aise <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> abandonner, se désister de ⟨abl.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zithilii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शिथिली</span> <i>śithilī</i> iic. de <a href="#entry-zithila" class="skt-link">śithila</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zithiliikara_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śithilīkaraṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-kara_na" class="skt-link">karaṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-zithiliikara_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> phil. [<a href="#entry-yoga" class="skt-link">yoga</a>] relaxation.<br> <a href="#entry-zithilii" class="skt-link">śithilī</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zithilita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शिथिलित</span> <i>śithilita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-zithila" class="skt-link">śithila</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zithilita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zithilita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zithilitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śithilitā</i> relâché, défait, lâche, flasque.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ziva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शिव</span> <i>śiva</i> a. <a href="#entry-ziva" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ziva" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zivaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śivā</i> bienfaisant; favorable, propice, salutaire, de bon augure — <a href="#entry-ziva" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. épith. euphémique du dieu véd. <a href="#entry-Urudra" class="skt-link">Rudra</a>; plus tard élevé en dieu suprême Śiva, forme transcendante du Divin; sa première épouse, <a href="#entry-Usatii" class="skt-link">Satī</a> fille de <a href="#entry-Udak_sa" class="skt-link">Dakṣa</a>, s'immola par le feu par humiliation qu'il ne soit pas invité au sacrifice que celui-ci fit à <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a>; <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>, fou de douleur, transporta son cadavre dans l'Univers, en dispersant ses membres en autant de sanctuaires [<a href="#entry-pii_thasthaana" class="skt-link">pīṭhasthāna</a>]; plus tard il épousa sa réincarnation la Fille de la Montagne [<a href="#entry-Upaarvatii" class="skt-link">Pārvatī</a>], qui incarne son Inaccessible [<a href="#entry-Udurgaa" class="skt-link">Durgā</a>] Puissance [<a href="#entry-Uzakti" class="skt-link">Śakti</a>]; leurs fils sont <a href="#entry-Uga_neza" class="skt-link">Gaṇeśa</a> et <a href="#entry-Uskanda" class="skt-link">Skanda</a>; <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> est le patron des ascètes, il porte les cheveux tressés en nattes formant chignon [<a href="#entry-Uja_taadhaara" class="skt-link">Jaṭādhāra</a>] et médite au sommet du mont <a href="#entry-Ukailaasa" class="skt-link">Kailāsa</a>; il est orné de serpents [<a href="#entry-naagabhuu_sa_na" class="skt-link">nāgabhūṣaṇa</a>]; omniscient, il possède le 3⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ œil [<a href="#entry-trinetra" class="skt-link">trinetra</a>] au milieu du front, duquel sort le feu qui détruit <a href="#entry-Ukaama" class="skt-link">Kāma</a>; sa monture est le taureau <a href="#entry-Unandii" class="skt-link">Nandī</a>, son symbole le <a href="#entry-lifga" class="skt-link">liṅga</a>; il porte l'arc <a href="#entry-Uajagava" class="skt-link">Ajagava</a>, et est l'archer <a href="#entry-Upinaakin" class="skt-link">Pinākin</a> qui détruisit la Cité des trois démons <a href="#entry-Utripura" class="skt-link">Tripura</a>; il tua le démon-éléphant [<a href="#entry-gajaasura" class="skt-link">gajāsura</a>]; sous sa forme terrible <a href="#entry-Ubhairava" class="skt-link">Bhairava</a> il parcourt les champs crématoires avec son sceptre d'os et de crâne [<a href="#entry-kha_tvaafga" class="skt-link">khaṭvāṅga</a>]; il se manifesta comme colonne de Feu [<a href="#entry-Uaru_naacalezvara" class="skt-link">Aruṇācaleśvara</a>] pour montrer sa suprématie à <a href="#entry-Ubrahmaa" class="skt-link">Brahmā</a> et à <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Uardana" class="skt-link">Ardana</a>, <a href="#entry-Uardhanaariizvara" class="skt-link">Ardhanārīśvara</a>, <a href="#entry-Uiiza" class="skt-link">Īśa</a>, <a href="#entry-Uiizaana" class="skt-link">Īśāna</a>, <a href="#entry-Uumaapati" class="skt-link">Umāpati</a>, <a href="#entry-Ukapardii" class="skt-link">Kapardī</a>, <a href="#entry-Ukaalaari" class="skt-link">Kālāri</a>, <a href="#entry-Ukedaara" class="skt-link">Kedāra</a>, <a href="#entry-Ugafgaadhara" class="skt-link">Gaṅgādhara</a>, <a href="#entry-Ucandramuku_ta" class="skt-link">Candramukuṭa</a>, <a href="#entry-Ucandrazekhara" class="skt-link">Candraśekhara</a>, <a href="#entry-Utripuraghna" class="skt-link">Tripuraghna</a>, <a href="#entry-Udhanvin" class="skt-link">Dhanvin</a>, <a href="#entry-Udhuurja_ti" class="skt-link">Dhūrjaṭi</a>, <a href="#entry-Una_taraaja" class="skt-link">Naṭarāja</a>, <a href="#entry-Unira_njana" class="skt-link">Nirañjana</a>, <a href="#entry-Upazupati" class="skt-link">Paśupati</a>, <a href="#entry-Upuramathana" class="skt-link">Puramathana</a>, <a href="#entry-Uba_tu" class="skt-link">Baṭu</a>, <a href="#entry-Ubhagaalin" class="skt-link">Bhagālin</a>, <a href="#entry-Ubharga" class="skt-link">Bharga</a>, <a href="#entry-Ubhava" class="skt-link">Bhava</a>, <a href="#entry-Ubhik_saa_tana" class="skt-link">Bhikṣāṭana</a>, <a href="#entry-Umanthaana" class="skt-link">Manthāna</a>, <a href="#entry-Umahaakaala" class="skt-link">Mahākāla</a>, <a href="#entry-Um_rtyu_njaya" class="skt-link">Mṛtyuṃjaya</a>, <a href="#entry-Ulakuliiza" class="skt-link">Lakulīśa</a>, <a href="#entry-Uviirabhadra" class="skt-link">Vīrabhadra</a>, <a href="#entry-Uzafkara" class="skt-link">Śaṅkara</a>, <a href="#entry-Uzabara" class="skt-link">Śabara</a>, <a href="#entry-Uzambhu" class="skt-link">Śambhu</a>, <a href="#entry-Uzarva" class="skt-link">Śarva</a>, <a href="#entry-Uhara" class="skt-link">Hara</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. le culte de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> élève la puissance destructrice de <a href="#entry-Urudra" class="skt-link">Rudra</a> en principe divin transcendant suprême [<a href="#entry-Umahezvara" class="skt-link">Maheśvara</a>]; ses temples portent son signe [<a href="#entry-lifga" class="skt-link">liṅga</a>] dans le sanctuaire du Seigneur [<a href="#entry-Uiizvara" class="skt-link">Īśvara</a>], gardé par <a href="#entry-Unanda" class="skt-link">Nanda</a> et <a href="#entry-Usunanda" class="skt-link">Sunanda</a>; l'arbre <a href="#entry-bilva" class="skt-link">bilva</a> lui est sacré, ses feuilles trilobées [<a href="#entry-tryambaka" class="skt-link">tryambaka</a>] lui sont données en offrande <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-tantra" class="skt-link">tantra</a>] la doctrine shivaïte est développée dans les dialogues divins (<a href="#entry-aagama" class="skt-link">āgama</a> et <a href="#entry-nigama" class="skt-link">nigama</a>); le shivaïsme du Cachemire a développé la doctrine <a href="#entry-trika" class="skt-link">trika</a> — <a href="#entry-ziva" class="skt-link">n.</a> bien-être, prospérité, bonheur.<br> <i>śivāya</i> var. <i>śivena</i> heureusement; par chance.<br> <i>śivāya</i> <i>gamyatām</i> bon voyage!<br> <i>śivās</i> <i>te</i> <i>panthānaḥ</i> <i>santu</i> bon voyage!
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zivaadityamizra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śivādityamiśra</i> [<a href="#entry-aaditya" class="skt-link">āditya</a>-<a href="#entry-mizra" class="skt-link">miśra</a>] <a href="#entry-zivaadityamizra" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du logicien Śivādityamiśra (12⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle), auteur de la Saptapadārthī.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zivaananda">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śivānanda</i> [<a href="#entry-aananda" class="skt-link">ānanda</a>] <a href="#entry-zivaananda" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. de Śivānanda «Joie du Seigneur», nom de divers personnages; not. mod. <a href="#entry-Usvaamii" class="skt-link">Svāmī</a> Śivānanda_1 (1887–1963), médecin, sage et écrivain, fondateur de la «Divine Life Society».<br> <a href="#entry-ziva" class="skt-link">śiva</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zivad_r_s_ti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śivadṛṣṭi</i> [<a href="#entry-d_r_s_ti" class="skt-link">dṛṣṭi</a>] <a href="#entry-zivad_r_s_ti" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de la Śivadṛṣṭi «Vision de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>», ouvrage de base de la doctrine <a href="#entry-zaivasiddhaanta" class="skt-link">śaivasiddhānta</a> de la Reconnaissance [<a href="#entry-pratyabhij_naazaastra" class="skt-link">pratyabhijñāśāstra</a>]; son auteur est <a href="#entry-Usomaananda" class="skt-link">Somānanda</a> (fin du 9⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zivadhanus">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śivadhanus</i> [<a href="#entry-dhanus" class="skt-link">dhanus</a>] <a href="#entry-zivadhanus" class="skt-link">n.</a> myth. arc de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Ubhiimasena" class="skt-link">Bhīmasena</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zivadharma">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śivadharma</i> [<a href="#entry-dharma" class="skt-link">dharma</a>] <a href="#entry-zivadharma" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Śivadharma, texte rituel shivaïte du 5⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle; il fait partie de la <a href="#entry-Unandikezvarasa_mhitaa" class="skt-link">Nandikeśvarasaṃhitā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zivadvi_s_taa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śivadviṣṭā</i> [<a href="#entry-dvi_s_taa" class="skt-link">dviṣṭā</a>] <a href="#entry-zivadvi_s_taa" class="skt-link">f.</a> natu. fleur de l'arbre pandane, bot. <i>Pandanus odoratissimus</i>; elle est odieuse à <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-ketakii" class="skt-link">ketakī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zivagiitaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śivagītā</i> [<a href="#entry-giitaa" class="skt-link">gītā</a>] <a href="#entry-zivagiitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> myth. [PP.] sermon prêché par <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> à <a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zivagiri">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śivagiri</i> [<a href="#entry-giri" class="skt-link">giri</a>] <a href="#entry-zivagiri" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. np. de l'ermitage [<a href="#entry-ma_tha" class="skt-link">maṭha</a>] du Śivagiri «Mont <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>», colline près de Varkala sur la côte du <a href="#entry-Ukerala" class="skt-link">Kerala</a>; mod. <a href="#entry-zrii" class="skt-link">śrī</a> Nārāyaṇa Guru (1855–1928) y prêcha l'abolition des castes.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zivajii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शिवजी</span> <i>śivajī</i> <a href="#entry-zivajii" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du roi Marathe <a href="#entry-Uchattrapati" class="skt-link">Chattrapati</a> Śivajī (1627–1680); il est fils du mercenaire Śahjī, de famille Bhonsle, général du sultanat d'Ahmednagar, puis du sultanat de Bijapur à Pune; il combattit les empereurs moghols et créa un puissant royaume hindou au Deccan au 17⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zivaj_naana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śivajñāna</i> [<a href="#entry-j_naana" class="skt-link">jñāna</a>] <a href="#entry-zivaj_naana" class="skt-link">n.</a> phil. gnose révélée par <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> dans un <a href="#entry-aagama" class="skt-link">āgama</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zivaj_naanabodha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śivajñānabodha</i> [<a href="#entry-bodha" class="skt-link">bodha</a>] <a href="#entry-zivaj_naanabodha" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Śivajñānabodha, ouvrage synthétique du mouvement <a href="#entry-zaivasiddhaanta" class="skt-link">śaivasiddhānta</a> du Sud de l'Inde.<br> <a href="#entry-zivaj_naana" class="skt-link">śivajñāna</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zivakaamasundarii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śivakāmasundarī</i> [<a href="#entry-kaama" class="skt-link">kāma</a>-<a href="#entry-sundarii" class="skt-link">sundarī</a>] <a href="#entry-zivakaamasundarii" class="skt-link">f.</a> myth. np. de Śivakāmasundarī «Belle du désir de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>», épith. de <a href="#entry-Upaarvatii" class="skt-link">Pārvatī</a> compagne de <a href="#entry-Una_tezvara" class="skt-link">Naṭeśvara</a>; son sanctuaire principal est à <a href="#entry-Ucidambaram" class="skt-link">Cidambaram</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zivakara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
