    <i>śivakara</i> [<a href="#entry-kara_1" class="skt-link">kara_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zivakara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zivakara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zivakarii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śivakarī</i> qui cause le bonheur, la prospérité; bénéfique, propice.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zivalifga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śivaliṅga</i> [<a href="#entry-lifga" class="skt-link">liṅga</a>] <a href="#entry-zivalifga" class="skt-link">n.</a> cf. <a href="#entry-lifga" class="skt-link">liṅga</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. geste [<a href="#entry-abhinaya" class="skt-link">abhinaya</a>] du pouce dressé symbolisant le <i>śivaliṅga</i>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zivamahiman">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śivamahiman</i> [<a href="#entry-mahiman" class="skt-link">mahiman</a>] <a href="#entry-zivamahiman" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. majesté de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>.<br> <i>śivamahimnaḥ</i> <i>stava</i> m. lit. hymnes de louange de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zivapu_spaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śivapuṣpaka</i> [<a href="#entry-pu_spaka" class="skt-link">puṣpaka</a>] <a href="#entry-zivapu_spaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. bot. <i>Calotropis gigantea</i>; cf. <a href="#entry-jambhana_1" class="skt-link">jambhana_1</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zivapura">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śivapura</i> [<a href="#entry-pura" class="skt-link">pura</a>] <a href="#entry-zivapura" class="skt-link">n.</a> géo. np. de Śivapura, épith. de <a href="#entry-Uvaaraa_nasii" class="skt-link">Vārāṇasī</a> «Cité de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>» — <a href="#entry-zivapurii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śivapurī</i> id. <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> géo. np. de Śivapurī, tiers du site d'<a href="#entry-Uofkaarezvara" class="skt-link">Oṃkāreśvara</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zivapuraa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śivapurāṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-puraa_na" class="skt-link">purāṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-zivapuraa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Śivapurāṇa; cf. <a href="#entry-Uvaayaviiyapuraa_na" class="skt-link">Vāyavīyapurāṇa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zivaraatri">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śivarātri</i> [<a href="#entry-raatri" class="skt-link">rātri</a>] <a href="#entry-zivaraatri" class="skt-link">f.</a> soc. festival de la nuit de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>, le 14⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ jour [<a href="#entry-caturdazii" class="skt-link">caturdaśī</a>] de la quinzaine sombre [<a href="#entry-k_r_s_napak_sa" class="skt-link">kṛṣṇapakṣa</a>] du mois de <a href="#entry-maagha" class="skt-link">māgha</a>; c'est un jour de jeûne [<a href="#entry-vrata" class="skt-link">vrata</a>]; on y célèbre le mariage de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> et <a href="#entry-Upaarvatii" class="skt-link">Pārvatī</a> et la danse cosmique [<a href="#entry-taa_n_dava" class="skt-link">tāṇḍava</a>] de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>; dans les sanctuaires le <a href="#entry-lifga" class="skt-link">liṅga</a> est ondoyé de <a href="#entry-pa_ncagavya" class="skt-link">pañcagavya</a>; les fidèles passent la nuit au temple à chanter des hymnes, et les épouses y prient pour la longévité de leurs époux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zivasmaamin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śivasmāmin</i> [<a href="#entry-svaamin" class="skt-link">svāmin</a>] <a href="#entry-zivasmaamin" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. de Śivasvāmī, poète bd. du <a href="#entry-Ukazmiira" class="skt-link">Kaśmīra</a>, au 9⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle; il était poète de cour du roi Avantivarmā (855–883); on lui doit le <a href="#entry-mahaakaavya" class="skt-link">mahākāvya</a> <a href="#entry-Ukapphi_naabhyudaya" class="skt-link">Kapphiṇābhyudaya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zivasoma">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śivasoma</i> [<a href="#entry-soma" class="skt-link">soma</a>] <a href="#entry-zivasoma" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. de Śivasoma, disciple de <a href="#entry-Uzafkara_1" class="skt-link">Śaṃkara_1</a>, <a href="#entry-guru" class="skt-link">guru</a> du roi <a href="#entry-Uindravarmaa_1" class="skt-link">Indravarmā_1</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zivastotra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śivastotra</i> [<a href="#entry-stotra" class="skt-link">stotra</a>] <a href="#entry-zivastotra" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. de l'hymne dévotionnel Śivastotra, attribué à <a href="#entry-Uraava_na" class="skt-link">Rāvaṇa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zivasuutra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śivasūtra</i> [<a href="#entry-suutra" class="skt-link">sūtra</a>] <a href="#entry-zivasuutra" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Śivasūtra, recueil shivaïte [<a href="#entry-aagama" class="skt-link">āgama</a>] consistant en 77 stances [<a href="#entry-suutra" class="skt-link">sūtra</a>]; il est à la base du système <a href="#entry-trika" class="skt-link">trika</a> du <a href="#entry-Ukazmiira" class="skt-link">Kaśmīra</a>; on dit qu'il fut révélé en rêve à <a href="#entry-Uvasugupta" class="skt-link">Vasugupta</a>; <a href="#entry-Uk_semaraaja" class="skt-link">Kṣemarāja</a> en fit le commentaire <a href="#entry-Uvimarzinii" class="skt-link">Vimarśinī</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. <i>śivasūtrāṇi</i> gram. 14 formules décrivant les familles de phonèmes utiles à l'<a href="#entry-Ua_s_taadhyaayii" class="skt-link">Aṣṭādhyāyī</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-pratyaahaarasuutra" class="skt-link">pratyāhārasūtra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zivatattva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śivatattva</i> [<a href="#entry-tattva" class="skt-link">tattva</a>] <a href="#entry-zivatattva" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Śivatattva, ouvrage de <a href="#entry-zaivasiddhaanta" class="skt-link">śaivasiddhānta</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zivatva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śivatva</i> [<a href="#entry--tva" class="skt-link">-tva</a>] <a href="#entry-zivatva" class="skt-link">n.</a> essence de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zivazara_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śivaśaraṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-zara_na" class="skt-link">śaraṇa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zivazara_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zivazara_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zivazara_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śivaśaraṇā</i> «protecteur de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>», qualifie les prosélytes shivaïtes tels que <a href="#entry-Ubasava" class="skt-link">Basava</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zivi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शिवि</span> <i>śivi</i> cf. <a href="#entry-zibi" class="skt-link">śibi</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ziz">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शिश्</span> <i>śiś</i> cf. <a href="#entry-zaa" class="skt-link">śā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zizira">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शिशिर</span> <i>śiśira</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-zyaa" class="skt-link">śyā</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zizira" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zizira" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ziziraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śiśirā</i> frais, froid — <a href="#entry-zizira" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zizira" class="skt-link">n.</a> froid, fraîcheur, gel <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> saison froide (mois de <a href="#entry-maagha" class="skt-link">māgha</a> et <a href="#entry-phaalguna" class="skt-link">phālguna</a>).<br> <i>śiśirīkṛ</i> refroidir, rafraîchir.<br> <i>śiśirībhū</i> se refroidir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ziziravasantau">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śiśiravasantau</i> [<a href="#entry-vasanta" class="skt-link">vasanta</a>] <a href="#entry-ziziravasantau" class="skt-link">m.</a> du. le froid et le chaud; l'hiver et le printemps.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zizirezvara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śiśireśvara</i> [<a href="#entry-iizvara" class="skt-link">īśvara</a>] <a href="#entry-zizirezvara" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Śiśireśvara, épith. de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> «Maître du froid»; il a un temple à <a href="#entry-Ubhuvanezvarii_1" class="skt-link">Bhuvaneśvarī_1</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-zizira" class="skt-link">śiśira</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zizirita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शिशिरित</span> <i>śiśirita</i> [<a href="#entry-zizira" class="skt-link">śiśira</a><a href="#entry--ita" class="skt-link">-ita</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zizirita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zizirita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ziziritaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śiśiritā</i> refroidi <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> tremblé; crénelé (se dit not. du contour d'un <a href="#entry-yantra" class="skt-link">yantra</a> linéaire formant des recoins à angle droit).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zizna">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शिश्न</span> <i>śiśna</i> <a href="#entry-zizna" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zizna" class="skt-link">n.</a> queue; pénis.<br> <i>śiśnaṃ</i> <i>bhara</i> adonné à la luxure.<br> <i>śiśnodaraparāyaṇa</i> hédoniste, libertin [«qui ne pense qu'à son pénis et son estomac»].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ziznadeva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śiśnadeva</i> [<a href="#entry-deva" class="skt-link">deva</a>] <a href="#entry-ziznadeva" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. satyre, démon personnifiant le pénis — a. <a href="#entry-ziznadeva" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ziznadeva" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ziznadevaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śiśnadevā</i> véd. qualifie les barbares «adorateurs du pénis» <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> libertin, débauché, dépravé.<br> <a href="#entry-zizna" class="skt-link">śiśna</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zizu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शिशु</span> <i>śiśu</i> <a href="#entry-zizu" class="skt-link">m.</a> enfant, jeune garçon; syn. <a href="#entry-kumaara" class="skt-link">kumāra</a>, <a href="#entry-baala" class="skt-link">bāla</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> petit d'un animal — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. md. <i>(śiśūyate)</i> se comporter comme un enfant.<br> <i>śiśūyate</i> <i>vṛddhaḥ</i> le vieillard se comporte comme un enfant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zizumaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śiśumāra</i> [<a href="#entry-maara" class="skt-link">māra</a>] <a href="#entry-zizumaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zizumaarii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śiśumārī</i> natu. zoo. <i>Platanista Gangetica</i>, dauphin du Gange <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> natu. crocodile [«tueur d'enfants»] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. [VP.] np. de Śiśumāra, créature céleste, mi-homme mi-crocodile; la moitié humaine évoque <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a> et la divinité latente en l'homme, le crocodile dévore le temps, alors que l'univers tourne autour de l'étoile polaire <a href="#entry-Udhruva" class="skt-link">Dhruva</a> qui porte sa queue <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> astr. constellation de la Petite Ourse, portant <a href="#entry-Uzizumaara" class="skt-link">Śiśumāra</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-zizu" class="skt-link">śiśu</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zizumaat_r">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śiśumātṛ</i> [<a href="#entry-maat_r" class="skt-link">mātṛ</a>] <a href="#entry-zizumaat_r" class="skt-link">f.</a> pl. <i>śiśumātaras</i> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] les 7 «mères (dangereuses) aux enfants» associées à <a href="#entry-Uskanda" class="skt-link">Skanda</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Uaaryaa" class="skt-link">Āryā</a>, <a href="#entry-maat_rga_na" class="skt-link">mātṛgaṇa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zizunaaga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śiśunāga</i> [<a href="#entry-naaga" class="skt-link">nāga</a>] <a href="#entry-zizunaaga" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. de Śiśunāga, prince du clan <a href="#entry-Ulicchavi" class="skt-link">Licchavi</a>, ministre [<a href="#entry-amaatya" class="skt-link">amātya</a>] du <a href="#entry-Umagadha" class="skt-link">Magadha</a>, vice-roi de <a href="#entry-Uvaaraa_nasii" class="skt-link">Vārāṇasī</a> (5⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle ant.); il mit fin à la dynastie <a href="#entry-Uharyafka" class="skt-link">Haryaṅka</a> et devint empereur du <a href="#entry-Umagadha" class="skt-link">Magadha</a>; il établit sa capitale à <a href="#entry-Uvaizaalii_1" class="skt-link">Vaiśālī_1</a>; il conquit <a href="#entry-Uavanti" class="skt-link">Avanti</a> et mit fin au pouvoir des rois <a href="#entry-Upradyota" class="skt-link">Pradyota</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zizupaala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śiśupāla</i> [<a href="#entry-paala" class="skt-link">pāla</a>] <a href="#entry-zizupaala" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. de Śiśupāla «Protecteur des enfants», prince des <a href="#entry-Ucedayas" class="skt-link">Cedayas</a>, fils de <a href="#entry-Udamagho_sa" class="skt-link">Damaghoṣa</a> et <a href="#entry-Uzrutadevaa" class="skt-link">Śrutadevā</a> (et donc cousin de <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a>); lorsqu'il naquit, il avait 3 yeux et 4 bras, et il brayait comme un âne; on lui prédit qu'il perdrait son 3⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ œil et ses 2 bras supplémentaires à la vue de l'homme destiné à le tuer; un jour où <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a> visitait le pays <a href="#entry-Ucedi" class="skt-link">Cedi</a>, il prit l'enfant dans ses bras et le miracle se produisit; sa mère lui fit alors promettre que si son fils lui faisait offense, il lui pardonnerait; <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a> jura qu'il ne lui nuirait pas à moins qu'il ne l'insultât cent fois; il grandit et devint un roi puissant; <a href="#entry-Urukmi_nii" class="skt-link">Rukmiṇī</a> lui fut promise, mais elle était amoureuse de <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a> qui l'enleva le jour prévu pour son mariage; lors du sacrifice solennel [<a href="#entry-raajasuuya" class="skt-link">rājasūya</a>] de <a href="#entry-Uyudhi_s_thira" class="skt-link">Yudhiṣṭhira</a>, <a href="#entry-Usahadeva" class="skt-link">Sahadeva</a> proposa d'honorer <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a> comme président, mais <a href="#entry-Uzizupaala" class="skt-link">Śiśupāla</a> se mit à l'insulter; à la centième insulte <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a> le décapita avec son disque [<a href="#entry-cakra" class="skt-link">cakra</a>] <a href="#entry-Usudarzana" class="skt-link">Sudarśana</a>; <a href="#entry-Uzizupaala" class="skt-link">Śiśupāla</a>, dernier avatar de la troisième renaissance des <a href="#entry-dvaarapaalau" class="skt-link">dvārapālau</a> maudits, put alors se libérer en se fondant en <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a> «comme un météore qui frappe la Terre».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zizupaalagaarha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śiśupālagārha</i> [<a href="#entry-gaarha" class="skt-link">gārha</a>] <a href="#entry-zizupaalagaarha" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. np. de Śiśupālagārha, capitale médiévale du <a href="#entry-Ukalifga" class="skt-link">Kaliṅga</a>, à l'emplacement de l'antique <a href="#entry-Uto_salii" class="skt-link">Toṣalī</a>; mod. les ruines de ses fortifications sont à 5 km au sud de <a href="#entry-Ubhubanezvar" class="skt-link">Bhubaneśvar</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zizupaalavadha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śiśupālavadha</i> [<a href="#entry-vadha" class="skt-link">vadha</a>] <a href="#entry-zizupaalavadha" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Śiśupālavadha «Meurtre de <a href="#entry-Uzizupaala" class="skt-link">Śiśupāla</a>», poème de <a href="#entry-Umaagha" class="skt-link">Māgha</a>; à la requête des dieux, <a href="#entry-Unaarada" class="skt-link">Nārada</a> demande à <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a> de tuer son propre cousin <a href="#entry-Uzizupaala" class="skt-link">Śiśupāla</a>, réincarnation de <a href="#entry-Uhira_nyakazipu" class="skt-link">Hiraṇyakaśipu</a> et <a href="#entry-Uraava_na" class="skt-link">Rāvaṇa</a>; lors du <a href="#entry-raajasuuya" class="skt-link">rājasūya</a> de <a href="#entry-Uyudhi_s_thira" class="skt-link">Yudhiṣṭhira</a> <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a> s'en acquitta sans renier sa parole; le poème est l'objet de commentaires de <a href="#entry-Umallinaatha_1" class="skt-link">Mallinātha_1</a> et <a href="#entry-Uvallabhadeva" class="skt-link">Vallabhadeva</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-zizupaala" class="skt-link">śiśupāla</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zizutva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śiśutva</i> [<a href="#entry--tva" class="skt-link">-tva</a>] <a href="#entry-zizutva" class="skt-link">n.</a> enfance.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zlaagh">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">श्लाघ्</span> <i>ślāgh</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. md. <i>(ślāghate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>ślāghita</i><i>)</i> louer, célébrer ⟨dat.⟩.<br> <i>nṛpāya</i> <i>ślāghate</i> <i>bandī</i> le barde loue le roi.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zlak_s_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">श्लक्ष्ण</span> <i>ślakṣṇa</i> a. <a href="#entry-zlak_s_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zlak_s_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zlak_s_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ślakṣṇā</i> glissant, lisse, poli <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> tendre, gentil, délicat; petit, mince <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> honnête, sincère.<br> <i>ślakṣṇam</i> doucement, délicatement, gentiment.<br> <i>ślakṣṇīkṛ</i> polir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zle_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">श्लेष</span> <i>śleṣa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-zli_s" class="skt-link">śliṣ</a>] <a href="#entry-zle_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> union, jonction, embrassement <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. rhét. double sens, équivoque verbale, jeu de mots; paronomase; interprétation multiple d'un texte; on en distingue 3 sortes: <a href="#entry-sabhafgazle_sa" class="skt-link">sabhaṅgaśleṣa</a>, <a href="#entry-abhafgazle_sa" class="skt-link">abhaṅgaśleṣa</a> et <a href="#entry-ubhayazle_sa" class="skt-link">ubhayaśleṣa</a>.<br> <i>śabdaiḥ</i> <i>svabhāvādekārthaiḥ</i> <i>śleṣe'nekārthavācanam</i> En style <a href="#entry-zle_sa" class="skt-link">śleṣa</a>, on exprime des sens multiples par des mots ayant naturellement un sens unique.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zle_sakaavya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śleṣakāvya</i> [<a href="#entry-kaavya" class="skt-link">kāvya</a>] <a href="#entry-zle_sakaavya" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. poésie à double sens; cf. <a href="#entry-vilomakaavya" class="skt-link">vilomakāvya</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-zle_sa" class="skt-link">śleṣa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zle_sman">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">श्लेष्मन्</span> <i>śleṣman</i> [<a href="#entry-zle_sa" class="skt-link">śleṣa</a><a href="#entry--man" class="skt-link">-man</a>] <a href="#entry-zle_sman" class="skt-link">m.</a> méd. mucus; le phlegme, une des 3 humeurs [<a href="#entry-trido_sa" class="skt-link">tridoṣa</a>]; syn. <a href="#entry-kapha" class="skt-link">kapha</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zli_s">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">श्लिष्</span> <i>śliṣ</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] pr. <i>(śliṣyati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>śliṣṭa</i><i>)</i> abs. <i>(</i><i>śliṣṭvā</i>, <i>-śliṣya</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>ā</i>, <i>pra</i>, <i>vi</i>, <i>sam</i><i>)</i> s'attacher, adhérer, être fixé à ⟨loc. acc.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> entourer, embrasser — ca. <i>(śleṣayati)</i> unir, joindre.<br> <i>yathā</i> <i>puṣkarapalāśa</i> <i>āpo</i> <i>na</i> <i>śliṣyanta</i> <i>evamevaṃvidi</i> <i>pāpaṃ</i> <i>karma</i> <i>na</i> <i>śliṣyate</i> [ChU.] De même que l'eau n'adhère pas aux feuilles du lotus, tout aussi bien le péché n'adhère pas à qui sait cela.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zli_s_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">श्लिष्ट</span> <i>śliṣṭa</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-zli_s" class="skt-link">śliṣ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zli_s_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zli_s_ta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zli_s_taa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śliṣṭā</i> entouré, étreint, embrassé <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> attaché, adhérent; qui étreint, qui embrasse.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zli_s_tapriya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śliṣṭapriya</i> [<a href="#entry-priya" class="skt-link">priya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zli_s_tapriya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zli_s_tapriya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zli_s_tapriyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śliṣṭapriyā</i> qui embrasse sa bien aimée.<br> <a href="#entry-zli_s_ta" class="skt-link">śliṣṭa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zli_saa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">श्लिषा</span> <i>śliṣā</i> [act. <a href="#entry-zli_s" class="skt-link">śliṣ</a>] <a href="#entry-zli_saa" class="skt-link">f.</a> adhérence.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zloka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">श्लोक</span> <i>śloka</i> [<a href="#entry-zru" class="skt-link">śru</a>] <a href="#entry-zloka" class="skt-link">m.</a> strophe, stance héroïque (not. distique de deux hémistiches, formant 4 <a href="#entry-paadaas" class="skt-link">pādās</a> de 8 pieds); son invention est attribuée à <a href="#entry-Uvaalmiiki" class="skt-link">Vālmīki</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zlokadvaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ślokadvaya</i> [<a href="#entry-dvaya" class="skt-link">dvaya</a>] <a href="#entry-zlokadvaya" class="skt-link">n.</a> couple de strophes.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zlokatva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ślokatva</i> [<a href="#entry--tva" class="skt-link">-tva</a>] <a href="#entry-zlokatva" class="skt-link">n.</a> poésie.<br> <i>śokaḥ</i> <i>ślokatvam</i> <i>āgataḥ</i> [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] La peine (de <a href="#entry-Uvaalmiiki" class="skt-link">Vālmīki</a> à la vue du désespoir de l'oiseau <a href="#entry-krau_nca" class="skt-link">krauñca</a> perdant son compagnon tué par un chasseur) devint poésie.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zlokavaarttika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ślokavārttika</i> [<a href="#entry-vaarttika" class="skt-link">vārttika</a>] <a href="#entry-zlokavaarttika" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Ślokavārttika, œuvre de <a href="#entry-miimaa_msaa" class="skt-link">mīmāṃsā</a> de <a href="#entry-Ukumaarila" class="skt-link">Kumārila</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-zloka" class="skt-link">śloka</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zlu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">श्लु</span> <i>ślu</i> nota. gram. suffixe conjugationnel [<a href="#entry-vikara_na_2" class="skt-link">vikaraṇa_2</a>] de la 3⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ classe de racines avec élision de l'affixe ‘<i>a</i>’.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zmazaana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">श्मशान</span> <i>śmaśāna</i> [<a href="#entry-azma" class="skt-link">aśma</a>-<a href="#entry-zayana" class="skt-link">śayana</a>] <a href="#entry-zmazaana" class="skt-link">n.</a> cimetière, champ de crémation.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zmazru">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">श्मश्रु</span> <i>śmaśru</i> var. <i>śmaśāru</i> <a href="#entry-zmazru" class="skt-link">n.</a> barbe; moustache <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. <i>śmaśrūni</i> id.<br> <i>śmaśru</i> <i>dhara</i> barbu.<br> <i>śmaśru</i> <i>dhṛ</i> porter la barbe.<br> <i>hariśmaśāru</i> à la barbe blonde.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zmazrula">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śmaśrula</i> [<a href="#entry--la" class="skt-link">-la</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zmazrula" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zmazrula" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zmazrulaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śmaśrulā</i> barbu.<br> <a href="#entry-zmazru" class="skt-link">śmaśru</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zo_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शोण</span> <i>śoṇa</i> a. <a href="#entry-zo_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zo_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zo_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śoṇā</i> <a href="#entry-zo_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śoṇī</i> rouge, couleur de sang — <a href="#entry-zo_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> rougeur — <a href="#entry-zo_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> sang; minium — <a href="#entry-zo_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śoṇā</i> géo. np. de la rivière Śoṇā, qui prend naissance près du pic <a href="#entry-Uamaraka_n_taka" class="skt-link">Amarakaṇṭaka</a> des monts <a href="#entry-Uvindhya" class="skt-link">Vindhya</a> et se jette dans le Gange en amont de <a href="#entry-Upaa_taliputra" class="skt-link">Pāṭaliputra</a>.<br> <i>śoṇīkṛ</i> teindre en rouge.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zo_nita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शोणित</span> <i>śoṇita</i> [<a href="#entry-zo_na" class="skt-link">śoṇa</a><a href="#entry--ita" class="skt-link">-ita</a>] <a href="#entry-zo_nita" class="skt-link">n.</a> sang.<br> <i>bahūśoṇitaliptaśarīraḥ</i> qui a le corps couvert de sang.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zo_nitapa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śoṇitapa</i> [<a href="#entry-pa_1" class="skt-link">pa_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zo_nitapa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zo_nitapa" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zo_nitapaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śoṇitapā</i> qui boit du sang.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zo_nitapura">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śoṇitapura</i> [<a href="#entry-pura" class="skt-link">pura</a>] <a href="#entry-zo_nitapura" class="skt-link">n.</a> myth. no. de Śoṇitapura «Cité du sang», capitale de l'<a href="#entry-asura" class="skt-link">asura</a> <a href="#entry-Ubaa_na" class="skt-link">Bāṇa</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-zo_nita" class="skt-link">śoṇita</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zo_s">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शोष्</span> <i>śoṣ</i> [ca. <a href="#entry-zu_s" class="skt-link">śuṣ</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(śoṣayati)</i> sécher, dessécher <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> abattre, détruire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zo_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शोष</span> <i>śoṣa</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-zu_s" class="skt-link">śuṣ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zo_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zo_sa" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zo_saa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śoṣā</i> qui se dessèche — <a href="#entry-zo_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> dessèchement; dessication <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> méd. tuberculose.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zo_sa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शोषण</span> <i>śoṣaṇa</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-zu_s" class="skt-link">śuṣ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zo_sa_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zo_sa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zo_sa_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śoṣaṇā</i> qui sèche, qui dessèche; qui absorbe, qui assèche <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. épith. de la brûlure d'amour [<a href="#entry-dahana" class="skt-link">dahana</a>], flèche de <a href="#entry-Ukaama" class="skt-link">Kāma</a> — ifc. qui enlève, qui détruit — <a href="#entry-zo_sa_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Śoṣaṇa, aspect d'<a href="#entry-Uagni" class="skt-link">Agni</a> — <a href="#entry-zo_sa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> dessication, assèchement <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gingembre sec.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zobh">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शोभ्</span> <i>śobh</i> [ca. <a href="#entry-zubh_1" class="skt-link">śubh_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(śobhayati)</i> orner, décorer; faire briller.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zobhaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शोभा</span> <i>śobhā</i> [act. <a href="#entry-zubh_1" class="skt-link">śubh_1</a>] <a href="#entry-zobhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> beauté, grâce; splendeur; aspect — ifc. excellence de.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zobhana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शोभन</span> <i>śobhana</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-zubh_1" class="skt-link">śubh_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zobhana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zobhana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zobhanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śobhanā</i> brillant; beau, joli; excellent, glorieux, magnifique — ifc. supérieur à, meilleur que — <a href="#entry-zobhana" class="skt-link">n.</a> bien, vertu, prospérité <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> astr. np. de Śobhana «Plénitude», personnifiant l'an 37 d'un cycle de vie [<a href="#entry-sa_mvatsara" class="skt-link">saṃvatsara</a>] — <a href="#entry-zobhanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śobhanā</i> jolie femme.<br> <i>śobhane</i> voc. adr. ma belle.<br> <i>śobhanā</i> <i>kṛtiḥ</i> [<a href="#entry-Upata_njali_1" class="skt-link">Patañjali_1</a>] une œuvre excellente (l'<a href="#entry-Ua_s_taadhyaayii" class="skt-link">Aṣṭādhyāyī</a>).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zobhin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शोभिन्</span> <i>śobhin</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-zubh_1" class="skt-link">śubh_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zobhin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zobhin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zobhinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śobhinī</i> qui brille; resplendissant <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> beau, joli — ifc. paré de, qui resplendit par, qui se distingue par; ornement de.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zobhita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शोभित</span> <i>śobhita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-zubh_1" class="skt-link">śubh_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zobhita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zobhita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zobhitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śobhitā</i> splendide — ifc. orné de, décoré de.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zoc">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शोच्</span> <i>śoc</i> [ca. <a href="#entry-zuc_1" class="skt-link">śuc_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(śocayati)</i> pfp. <i>(</i><i>śocya</i><i>)</i> inf. <i>(śocitum)</i> affliger <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> s'affliger, se lamenter — ps. <i>(śocyate)</i> être affligé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zocya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शोच्य</span> <i>śocya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-zuc_1" class="skt-link">śuc_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zocya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zocya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zocyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śocyā</i> dont il faut s'affliger; lamentable, déplorable, misérable — <a href="#entry-zocya" class="skt-link">n.</a> (impers.) il faut déplorer.<br> <i>gataṃ</i> <i>na</i> <i>śocyam</i> Il ne faut pas regretter le passé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zodh">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शोध्</span> <i>śodh</i> [ca. <a href="#entry-zudh" class="skt-link">śudh</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(śodhayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>sodhita</i><i>)</i> purifier.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zodha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शोध</span> <i>śodha</i> [act. <a href="#entry-zudh" class="skt-link">śudh</a>] <a href="#entry-zodha" class="skt-link">m.</a> purification; nettoyage <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> correction.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zodhaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शोधक</span> <i>śodhaka</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-zudh" class="skt-link">śudh</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zodhaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zodhaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zodhikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śodhikā</i> qui purifie, purificatoire — <a href="#entry-zodhaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> purificateur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> math. quantité à soustraire d'un nombre pour que le reste soit un carré.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zodhana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शोधन</span> <i>śodhana</i> [act. <a href="#entry-zudh" class="skt-link">śudh</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zodhana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zodhana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zodhanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śodhanā</i> qui nettoie, qui purifie — <a href="#entry-zodhana" class="skt-link">n.</a> moyen de purification <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> justification; preuve <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> math. soustraction — <a href="#entry-zodhanii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śodhanī</i> balai; brosse.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zodhanaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śodhanaka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-zodhanaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. appariteur de tribunal, préposé à sa propreté et à son bon fonctionnement.<br> <a href="#entry-zodhana" class="skt-link">śodhana</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zodhaniiya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शोधनीय</span> <i>śodhanīya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] <a href="#entry-zudh" class="skt-link">śudh</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zodhaniiya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zodhaniiya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zodhaniiyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śodhanīyā</i> à purifier; à justifier <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> à payer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zodhin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शोधिन्</span> <i>śodhin</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-zudh" class="skt-link">śudh</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zodhin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zodhin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zodhinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śodhinī</i> qui nettoie, qui purifie.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zodhita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शोधित</span> <i>śodhita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-zodh" class="skt-link">śodh</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zodhita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zodhita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zodhitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śodhitā</i> purifié.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zoka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शोक</span> <i>śoka</i> [act. <a href="#entry-zuc_1" class="skt-link">śuc_1</a>] <a href="#entry-zoka" class="skt-link">m.</a> peine, chagrin, douleur (à cause de ⟨g. iic.⟩) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Śoka, le Chagrin personnifié, fils de la Mort.<br> <i>śokaṃ</i> <i>dā</i> causer du chagrin.<br> <i>alaṃ</i> <i>śokena</i> ne soyez pas triste.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zokaapanuda">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śokāpanuda</i> [<a href="#entry-apanuda" class="skt-link">apanuda</a>] <a href="#entry-zokaapanuda" class="skt-link">m.</a> [«qui enlève la peine»] fils.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zokaarta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śokārta</i> [<a href="#entry-aarta" class="skt-link">ārta</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zokaarta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zokaarta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zokaartaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śokārtā</i> affligé par la peine.<br> <i>śokārtasya</i> <i>pravṛtto</i> <i>me</i> <i>śloko</i> <i>bhavatu</i> <i>nānyathā</i> [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] Affligé par la peine, ainsi le <a href="#entry-zloka" class="skt-link">śloka</a> et nul autre naquit en moi.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zokaarti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śokārti</i> [<a href="#entry-aarti" class="skt-link">ārti</a>] <a href="#entry-zokaarti" class="skt-link">f.</a> affliction; chagrin.<br> <a href="#entry-zoka" class="skt-link">śoka</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zokavat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śokavat</i> [<a href="#entry--vat" class="skt-link">-vat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zokavat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zokavat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zokavatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śokavatī</i> triste, qui a de la peine ou du chagrin.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zopha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शोफ</span> <i>śopha</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-zuu" class="skt-link">śū</a>] <a href="#entry-zopha" class="skt-link">m.</a> tumescence; gonflement <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> tumeur — <a href="#entry-zophaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śophā</i> ifc. gonflement de.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zraaddha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">श्राद्ध</span> <i>śrāddha</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-zraddhaa_2" class="skt-link">śraddhā_2</a>] <a href="#entry-zraaddha" class="skt-link">n.</a> foi, confiance, croyance; fidélité, loyauté <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. véd. rite funéraire, offrande aux mânes [<a href="#entry-pitaras" class="skt-link">pitaras</a>]; c'est un <a href="#entry-paakayaj_na" class="skt-link">pākayajña</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Ugayaa" class="skt-link">Gayā</a>, <a href="#entry-aabhyudayika" class="skt-link">ābhyudayika</a>, <a href="#entry-naandiimukhaas" class="skt-link">nāndīmukhās</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zraaddhabhuj">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śrāddhabhuj</i> [<a href="#entry-bhuj_3" class="skt-link">bhuj_3</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zraaddhabhuj" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zraaddhabhuj" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zraaddhabhuj" class="skt-link">f.</a> qui mange les offrandes aux mânes.<br> <a href="#entry-zraaddha" class="skt-link">śrāddha</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zraaddhin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">श्राद्धिन्</span> <i>śrāddhin</i> [<a href="#entry-zraaddha" class="skt-link">śrāddha</a><a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] qui effectue le rite funéraire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zraam">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">श्राम्</span> <i>śrām</i> [ca. <a href="#entry-zram" class="skt-link">śram</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(śrāmayati)</i> fatiguer; subjuguer, dominer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zraama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">श्राम</span> <i>śrāma</i> cf. <a href="#entry-zrama" class="skt-link">śrama</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zraanta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">श्रान्त</span> <i>śrānta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-zram" class="skt-link">śram</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zraanta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zraanta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zraantaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śrāntā</i> fatigué, épuisé <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> en peine, déprimé; affamé — <a href="#entry-zraanta" class="skt-link">n.</a> fatigue, épuisement <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> mortification, pratique d'austérités.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zraav">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">श्राव्</span> <i>śrāv</i> [ca. <a href="#entry-zru" class="skt-link">śru</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(śrāvayati)</i> raconter, réciter, proclamer à ⟨acc.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zraava_na_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">श्रावण</span> <i>śrāvaṇa_1</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-zrava_na_1" class="skt-link">śravaṇa_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zraava_na_1" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zraava_na_1" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zraava_naa_1" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śrāvaṇā_1</i> prescrit dans le <a href="#entry-Uveda" class="skt-link">Veda</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zraava_na_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">श्रावण</span> <i>śrāvaṇa_2</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-zrava_na_2" class="skt-link">śravaṇa_2</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zraava_na_2" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zraava_na_2" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zraava_naa_2" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śrāvaṇā_2</i> astr. relatif au <a href="#entry-nak_satra" class="skt-link">nakṣatra</a> <a href="#entry-Uzrava_naa" class="skt-link">Śravaṇā</a> — <a href="#entry-zraava_na_2" class="skt-link">m.</a> mois de <a href="#entry-Uzrava_naa" class="skt-link">Śravaṇā</a> (juillet-août) — <a href="#entry-zraava_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śrāvaṇī</i> jour de pleine lune [<a href="#entry-puur_nimaa" class="skt-link">pūrṇimā</a>] de ce mois; soc. on y pratique un <a href="#entry-paakayaj_na" class="skt-link">pākayajña</a>; on y célèbre le sanskrit [<a href="#entry-vizvasa_msk_rtadina" class="skt-link">viśvasaṃskṛtadina</a>]; cette fête qui suit <a href="#entry-tantuparvan" class="skt-link">tantuparvan</a> est l'occasion de renouveler l'<a href="#entry-upaviita" class="skt-link">upavīta</a>, et les sœurs mettent un <a href="#entry-rak_saabandhana" class="skt-link">rakṣābandhana</a> au poignet de leurs frères; mod. hi. <i>rakhīpūrṇimā</i>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zraavaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">श्रावक</span> <i>śrāvaka</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-zru" class="skt-link">śru</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zraavaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zraavaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zraavikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śrāvikā</i> audible — <a href="#entry-zraavaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> auditeur; élève <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> bd. not. disciple du <a href="#entry-Ubuddha" class="skt-link">Buddha</a> ayant écouté la doctrine de la bouche du Maître <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> jn. disciple <a href="#entry-jaina" class="skt-link">jaina</a>; not. renonçant (syn. <a href="#entry-zrava_na_1" class="skt-link">śravaṇa_1</a>).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zraavakaacaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śrāvakācāra</i> [<a href="#entry-aacaara" class="skt-link">ācāra</a>] <a href="#entry-zraavakaacaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> jn. comportement exigé d'un disciple <a href="#entry-jaina" class="skt-link">jaina</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-zraavaka" class="skt-link">śrāvaka</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zraavam">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">श्रावम्</span> <i>śrāvam</i> [abs. <a href="#entry-zru" class="skt-link">śru</a>] ind. ayant écouté.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zraavasta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">श्रावस्त</span> <i>śrāvasta</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-zravas" class="skt-link">śravas</a><a href="#entry--ta" class="skt-link">-ta</a>] <a href="#entry-zraavasta" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du roi Śrāvasta «le Glorieux» — <a href="#entry-zraavastii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śrāvastī</i> géo. np. de la ville de Śrāvastī «la Glorieuse», capitale du roi <a href="#entry-Uzraavasta" class="skt-link">Śrāvasta</a>, située dans le <a href="#entry-Ukosala" class="skt-link">Kosala</a> (<a href="#entry-paali" class="skt-link">pāli</a> Savatthi, mod. Sāhet-Māhet près de Faizabad au nord du <a href="#entry-Ubihaara" class="skt-link">Bihāra</a>); bd. <a href="#entry-Ubuddha" class="skt-link">Buddha</a> y fit un miracle en présence du roi <a href="#entry-Uprasenajit_2" class="skt-link">Prasenajit_2</a>: il se promena dans les airs et multiplia son image à l'infini.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zraddhaa_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">श्रद्धा</span> <i>śraddhā_1</i> [<a href="#entry-zrat" class="skt-link">śrat</a>-<a href="#entry-dhaa_1" class="skt-link">dhā_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">3</span>] pr. <i>(śraddadhāti)</i> pr. md. <i>(śraddhatte)</i> avoir la foi, avoir confiance, croire; être fidèle; croire qqn. ⟨dat. g.⟩; croire à qqc. ⟨g. loc.⟩; approuver, consentir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zraddhaa_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">श्रद्धा</span> <i>śraddhā_2</i> [act. <a href="#entry-zraddhaa_1" class="skt-link">śraddhā_1</a>] <a href="#entry-zraddhaa_2" class="skt-link">f.</a> foi, confiance, croyance, dévotion; fidélité, loyauté <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-vedaanta" class="skt-link">vedānta</a>] la foi, l'une des 6 vertus cardinales [<a href="#entry-_sa_tsampad" class="skt-link">ṣaṭsampad</a>] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Śraddhā «Dévotion», épouse d'<a href="#entry-Uafgiraa" class="skt-link">Aṅgirā</a>; elle est mère d'<a href="#entry-Uutathya" class="skt-link">Utathya</a>, <a href="#entry-Ub_rhaspati" class="skt-link">Bṛhaspati</a>, <a href="#entry-Uvayasya" class="skt-link">Vayasya</a>, <a href="#entry-Uzaanti_1" class="skt-link">Śānti_1</a>, <a href="#entry-Ughora" class="skt-link">Ghora</a>, <a href="#entry-Uviruupa" class="skt-link">Virūpa</a>, <a href="#entry-Usa_mvarta" class="skt-link">Saṃvarta</a> et <a href="#entry-Usudhanvaa" class="skt-link">Sudhanvā</a>; elle est aussi mère de <a href="#entry-Ukaama" class="skt-link">Kāma</a> par <a href="#entry-Udharma" class="skt-link">Dharma</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zraddheya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">श्रद्धेय</span> <i>śraddheya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-zraddhaa_1" class="skt-link">śraddhā_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zraddheya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zraddheya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zraddheyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śraddheyā</i> crédible, digne de confiance.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zram">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">श्रम्</span> <i>śram</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] pr. <i>(śrāmyati)</i> pft. <i>(śaśrāma)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>śrānta</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>pari</i>, <i>vi</i><i>)</i> être las, être fatigué; se fatiguer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pratiquer l'ascétisme; être épuisé par les austérités — ca. <i>(śrāmayati)</i> fatiguer; subjuguer, dominer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zrama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">श्रम</span> <i>śrama</i> [act. <a href="#entry-zram" class="skt-link">śram</a>] <a href="#entry-zrama" class="skt-link">m.</a> fatigue, épuisement, lassitude <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> labeur, exercice; peine, douleurs, ennuis <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> var. <i>śrāma</i> id. — <a href="#entry-zramaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śramā</i> ifc. exercice de, fatigue de.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zrama_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">श्रमण</span> <i>śramaṇa</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-zram" class="skt-link">śram</a>] <a href="#entry-zrama_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> ascète, religieux mendiant; not. bd. moine — <a href="#entry-zrama_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śramaṇī</i> bd. religieuse <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> tokharien shaman.<br> <i>śramaṇa</i> <i>gautama</i> m. bd. le <a href="#entry-Ubuddha" class="skt-link">Buddha</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zrambh">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">श्रम्भ्</span> <i>śrambh</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. md. <i>(śrambhate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>śrabdha</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>vi</i><i>)</i> [inusité sans pf.].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zrat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">श्रत्</span> <i>śrat</i> iic. véd. conforme à ses aspirations <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> foi <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>credo</i>; fr. croire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zrauta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">श्रौत</span> <i>śrauta</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-zruti" class="skt-link">śruti</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zrauta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zrauta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zrautaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śrautā</i> phil. conforme à la connaissance révélée; correct vis-à-vis de la doctrine; se dit not. des rites solennels védiques.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zrautasuutra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śrautasūtra</i> [<a href="#entry-suutra" class="skt-link">sūtra</a>] <a href="#entry-zrautasuutra" class="skt-link">n.</a> phil. texte d'aphorismes résumant les textes <a href="#entry-braahma_na" class="skt-link">brāhmaṇa</a> pour les rites solennels; cf. <a href="#entry-Uaazvalaayana" class="skt-link">Āśvalāyana</a>, <a href="#entry-Ubaudhaayana" class="skt-link">Baudhāyana</a>, <a href="#entry-Uvaadhuula" class="skt-link">Vādhūla</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-zrauta" class="skt-link">śrauta</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zrava_na_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">श्रवण</span> <i>śravaṇa_1</i> [act. <a href="#entry-zru" class="skt-link">śru</a>] <a href="#entry-zrava_na_1" class="skt-link">n.</a> audition, fait d'entendre; audition attentive de la parole du maître <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> chose entendue, enseignement, tradition orale <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-vedaanta" class="skt-link">vedānta</a>] détermination de la vraie doctrine de l'unité de l'Être — <a href="#entry-zrava_na_1" class="skt-link">m.</a> oreille <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> math. hypoténuse; diagonale d'un quadrilatère <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> astr. astérisme [<a href="#entry-nak_satra" class="skt-link">nakṣatra</a>] en forme de triangle; not. <a href="#entry-zrava_na_2" class="skt-link">śravaṇa_2</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> [<i>śramaṇa</i>] ascète, religieux mendiant; not. renonçant bd. ou jn.<br> <i>avaiṣṇava</i> <i>mukhodgīrṇam</i> <i>pūtam</i> <i>harikathāmṛtam</i> <i>śravaṇam</i> <i>naiva</i> <i>kartavyam</i> <i>sarpocchiṣṭam</i> <i>yathā</i> <i>payaḥ</i> [PP.] On ne doit écouter aucune louange du Seigneur de la bouche fétide d'un non-<a href="#entry-vai_s_nava" class="skt-link">vaiṣṇava</a>; c'est du poison, comme le lait souillé par le serpent.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zrava_na_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">श्रवण</span> <i>śravaṇa_2</i> [<a href="#entry-sru" class="skt-link">sru</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zrava_na_2" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zrava_na_2" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zrava_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śravaṇā</i> boiteux — <a href="#entry-zrava_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śravaṇā</i> myth. np. de Śravaṇā «(la vache) Boiteuse», 21⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ astérisme lunaire [<a href="#entry-nak_satra" class="skt-link">nakṣatra</a>], corr. à l'étoile Altaïr α de l'Aigle; <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a> y préside; son symbole est trois empreintes de pas, symbolisées par αβγ de l'Aigle.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zrava_nakumaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śravaṇakumāra</i> [<a href="#entry-kumaara" class="skt-link">kumāra</a>] <a href="#entry-zrava_nakumaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] np. du jeune garçon Śravaṇakumāra, seul secours de ses parents aveugles, que le roi <a href="#entry-Udazaratha" class="skt-link">Daśaratha</a> tua accidentellement à la chasse en décochant une flèche au son de la cruche qu'il remplissait à la <a href="#entry-Usarayuu" class="skt-link">Sarayū</a>; en punition, <a href="#entry-Udazaratha" class="skt-link">Daśaratha</a> fut maudit du chagrin de perdre son fils [<a href="#entry-putrazoka" class="skt-link">putraśoka</a>], et mourut alors que son fils <a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a> était parti en exil.<br> <a href="#entry-zrava_na_1" class="skt-link">śravaṇa_1</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zravas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">श्रवस्</span> <i>śravas</i> [<a href="#entry-zru" class="skt-link">śru</a>] <a href="#entry-zravas" class="skt-link">n.</a> véd. bruit, vacarme, cris de louanges <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> éloge, renommée, gloire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zravi_s_tha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">श्रविष्ठ</span> <i>śraviṣṭha</i> a. <a href="#entry-zravi_s_tha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zravi_s_tha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zravi_s_thaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śraviṣṭhā</i> très glorieux — <a href="#entry-zravi_s_thaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śraviṣṭhā</i> astr. np. de Śraviṣṭhā «la Très glorieuse», 22⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ astérisme lunaire [<a href="#entry-nak_satra" class="skt-link">nakṣatra</a>], corr. à l'étoile β du Dauphin; les trésors [<a href="#entry-vasavas" class="skt-link">vasavas</a>] y président; son symbole est un tambourin; plus tard appelée <a href="#entry-Udhani_s_thaa" class="skt-link">Dhaniṣṭhā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zravya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">श्रव्य</span> <i>śravya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-zru" class="skt-link">śru</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zravya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zravya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zravyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śravyā</i> digne d'être entendu; qui doit être écouté — <a href="#entry-zravya" class="skt-link">n.</a> obligation d'écouter <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. récit (opp. <a href="#entry-d_rzya" class="skt-link">dṛśya</a>).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zre_ni">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">श्रेणि</span> <i>śreṇi</i> <a href="#entry-zre_ni" class="skt-link">f.</a> série, groupe; troupe, multitude <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> essaim <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> guilde, compagnie, corporation <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> var. <i>śreṇī</i> id.<br> <i>śreṇi</i> <i>bhū</i> [<a href="#entry-bhuu_1" class="skt-link">bhū_1</a>] faire la queue, se mettre en rang.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zre_s_tha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">श्रेष्ठ</span> <i>śreṣṭha</i> [super. <a href="#entry-zrii" class="skt-link">śrī</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zre_s_tha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zre_s_tha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zre_s_thaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śreṣṭhā</i> meilleur, excellent en qqc. ⟨loc.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> le premier, le meilleur, le plus beau parmi ⟨g. iic.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> l'aîné — <a href="#entry-zre_s_tha" class="skt-link">m.</a> roi; brâhmane <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. épith. de <a href="#entry-Ukubera" class="skt-link">Kubera</a> ou de <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a> — <a href="#entry-zre_s_tha" class="skt-link">n.</a> lait de vache — <a href="#entry-zre_s_thaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śreṣṭhā</i> excellente femme.<br> <i>dhanvināṃ</i> <i>śreṣṭha</i> le meilleur des archers.<br> <i>muniśreṣṭha</i> meilleur entre les sages.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zre_s_thatama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śreṣṭhatama</i> a. <a href="#entry-zre_s_thatama" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zre_s_thatama" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zre_s_thatamaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śreṣṭhatamā</i> super. le meilleur, le suprême.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zre_s_thatara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śreṣṭhatara</i> a. <a href="#entry-zre_s_thatara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zre_s_thatara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zre_s_thataraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śreṣṭhatarā</i> compar. meilleur.<br> <i>na</i> <i>hi</i> <i>mānuṣācchreṣṭhataraṃ</i> <i>hi</i> <i>kiñcit</i> [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] Il n'y a rien de supérieur à l'homme.<br> <a href="#entry-zre_s_tha" class="skt-link">śreṣṭha</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zre_s_thin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">श्रेष्ठिन्</span> <i>śreṣṭhin</i> [<a href="#entry-zre_s_tha" class="skt-link">śreṣṭha</a><a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] <a href="#entry-zre_s_thin" class="skt-link">m.</a> le meilleur (artisan), chef d'une corporation [<a href="#entry-zre_ni" class="skt-link">śreṇi</a>] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> marchand, artisan <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> bd. trésorier; banquier, prêteur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zre_s_thya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">श्रेष्ठ्य</span> <i>śreṣṭhya</i> [<a href="#entry-zre_s_tha" class="skt-link">śreṣṭha</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] <a href="#entry-zre_s_thya" class="skt-link">n.</a> suprématie, prééminence, précellence (parmi ⟨g. iic.⟩).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zreman">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">श्रेमन्</span> <i>śreman</i> [<a href="#entry-zrii" class="skt-link">śrī</a><a href="#entry--man" class="skt-link">-man</a>] <a href="#entry-zreman" class="skt-link">m.</a> distinction, supériorité; splendeur.<br> <i>sūryasya</i> <i>paśya</i> <i>śremāṇaṃ</i> <i>yo</i> <i>na</i> <i>tandrayate</i> <i>caran</i> [AB.] Admire la splendeur du Soleil qui ne se fatigue jamais de tourner.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zreyaa_msa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">श्रेयांस</span> <i>śreyāṃsa</i> [<a href="#entry-zreyas" class="skt-link">śreyas</a>] <a href="#entry-zreyaa_msa" class="skt-link">m.</a> jn. np. de Śreyāṃsa; cf. <a href="#entry-Uzreyaa_msanaatha" class="skt-link">Śreyāṃsanātha</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zreyaa_msanaatha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śreyāṃsanātha</i> [<a href="#entry-naatha" class="skt-link">nātha</a>] <a href="#entry-zreyaa_msanaatha" class="skt-link">m.</a> jn. np. de Śreyāṃsanātha «Meilleur des maîtres», 11⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ <a href="#entry-tiirthafkara" class="skt-link">tīrthaṅkara</a> mythique; son corps est jaune d'or, son symbole est un aigle, un daim ou un rhinocéros.<br> <a href="#entry-zreyaa_msa" class="skt-link">śreyāṃsa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zreyas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">श्रेयस्</span> <i>śreyas</i> [compar. <a href="#entry-zrii" class="skt-link">śrī</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zreyas" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zreyas" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zreyasii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śreyasī</i> plus beau, meilleur, préférable; excellent; propice, favorable <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> mieux — <a href="#entry-zreyas" class="skt-link">n.</a> meilleure condition, bien-être, bonheur; salut <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [KU.] la voie préférable, qui mène au salut; opp. <a href="#entry-preyas" class="skt-link">preyas</a> la voie des plaisirs, qui mène à la perdition.<br> <i>śreyas</i> <i>...</i> <i>na</i> <i>...</i> mieux vaut ... que ...<br> <i>śreyaśca</i> <i>preyaśca</i> le meilleur et l'agréable.<br> <i>satyād</i> <i>apyanṛtaṃ</i> <i>śreyas</i> [Ve.] Le mensonge est meilleur encore que la vérité.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zreyaskara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śreyaskara</i> [<a href="#entry-kara_1" class="skt-link">kara_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zreyaskara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zreyaskara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zreyaskarii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śreyaskarī</i> qui améliore; qui rend excellent.<br> <a href="#entry-zreyas" class="skt-link">śreyas</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zri">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">श्रि</span> <i>śri</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(śrayati)</i> pr. md. <i>(śrayate)</i> aor. [<span class="skt-grammar">3</span>] <i>(aśiśriyat)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>śrita</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>ā</i>, <i>ut</i>, <i>pari</i>, <i>sam</i><i>)</i> s'appuyer sur, se trouver près de, se réfugier auprès de ⟨acc.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> assumer une condition, occuper une place <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gr. κλινω; lat. <i>inclino</i>; all. lehnen; ang. to lean; fr. incliner.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zrii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">श्री</span> <i>śrī</i> <a href="#entry-zrii" class="skt-link">f.</a> chance; prospérité, fortune, bonheur; gloire <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> beauté <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Śrī «Fortune», épith. de <a href="#entry-Ulak_smii" class="skt-link">Lakṣmī</a>, déesse de la prospérité, épouse-<a href="#entry-zakti" class="skt-link">śakti</a> de <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a>; elle est dite issue du barattage de la mer de lait primordiale [<a href="#entry-k_siirodamathana" class="skt-link">kṣīrodamathana</a>], ou bien fille de <a href="#entry-Ubh_rgu" class="skt-link">Bhṛgu</a> et <a href="#entry-Ukyaati" class="skt-link">Kyāti</a>; elle est la patronne du commerce — iic. [<a href="#entry-zriimat" class="skt-link">śrīmat</a>] (devant noms propres ou d'ouvrages) excellent, splendide; honorable, éminent; fameux, célèbre, illustre; saint, vénéré, sacré.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zrii_m">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">श्रीं</span> <i>śrīṃ</i> part. phil. [<a href="#entry-tantra" class="skt-link">tantra</a>] son du <a href="#entry-kamalaabiija" class="skt-link">kamalābīja</a> ou <a href="#entry-zriibiija" class="skt-link">śrībīja</a>, <a href="#entry-biijamantra" class="skt-link">bījamantra</a> d'évocation de la Déesse.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zriibhaa_sya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śrībhāṣya</i> [<a href="#entry-bhaa_sya" class="skt-link">bhāṣya</a>] <a href="#entry-zriibhaa_sya" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Śrībhāṣya de Rāmānujācārya, commentaire du <a href="#entry-Ubrahmasuutra" class="skt-link">Brahmasūtra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zriibhaagavata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śrībhāgavata</i> [<a href="#entry-bhaagavata" class="skt-link">bhāgavata</a>] <a href="#entry-zriibhaagavata" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Śrībhāgavata <a href="#entry-mahaapuraa_na" class="skt-link">mahāpurāṇa</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Ubhaagavatapuraa_na" class="skt-link">Bhāgavatapurāṇa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zriibiija">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śrībīja</i> [<a href="#entry-biija" class="skt-link">bīja</a>] <a href="#entry-zriibiija" class="skt-link">n.</a> phil. [<a href="#entry-tantra" class="skt-link">tantra</a>] <a href="#entry-biijamantra" class="skt-link">bījamantra</a> ‘<a href="#entry-zrii_m" class="skt-link">śrīṃ</a>’, d'évocation de <a href="#entry-Uzrii" class="skt-link">Śrī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zriicakra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śrīcakra</i> [<a href="#entry-cakra" class="skt-link">cakra</a>] <a href="#entry-zriicakra" class="skt-link">n.</a> cf. <a href="#entry-zriiyantra" class="skt-link">śrīyantra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zriicakrapuujaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śrīcakrapūjā</i> [<a href="#entry-puujaa" class="skt-link">pūjā</a>] <a href="#entry-zriicakrapuujaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> soc. rite tantrique d'union avec <a href="#entry-Ulalitaa" class="skt-link">Lalitā</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-zriicakra" class="skt-link">śrīcakra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zriidhara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śrīdhara</i> [<a href="#entry-dhara" class="skt-link">dhara</a>] <a href="#entry-zriidhara" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Śrīdhara «Divin support», épith. de <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a>-<a href="#entry-Ukuurma" class="skt-link">Kūrma</a>, supportant la baratte primordiale; cf. <a href="#entry-k_siirodamathana" class="skt-link">kṣīrodamathana</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. de Śrīdhara_1 <a href="#entry-Ubha_t_ta" class="skt-link">Bhaṭṭa</a>, philosophe bengali du 10⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle; on lui doit la <a href="#entry-Unyaayakandalii" class="skt-link">Nyāyakandalī</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. de Śrīdhara_2 <a href="#entry-Usvaamii" class="skt-link">Svāmī</a> (14⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle); on lui doit des commentaires du <a href="#entry-Ubhaagavatapuraa_na" class="skt-link">Bhāgavatapurāṇa</a> et du <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nupuraa_na" class="skt-link">Viṣṇupurāṇa</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. cf. <a href="#entry-Uzriidharaacaarya" class="skt-link">Śrīdharācārya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zriidharaacaarya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śrīdharācārya</i> [<a href="#entry-aacaarya" class="skt-link">ācārya</a>] <a href="#entry-zriidharaacaarya" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du mathématicien Śrīdharācārya (~870–~930); on lui doit le traité d'arithmétique <a href="#entry-Upatiga_nita" class="skt-link">Patigaṇita</a>; il donna la règle de résolution des équations quadratiques.<br> <a href="#entry-zriidhara" class="skt-link">śrīdhara</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zriidharadaasa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śrīdharadāsa</i> [<a href="#entry-daasa" class="skt-link">dāsa</a>] <a href="#entry-zriidharadaasa" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. de Śrīdharadāsa, compilateur du recueil de proverbes <a href="#entry-Usaduktikar_naam_rta" class="skt-link">Saduktikarṇāmṛta</a> (1205).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zriihar_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śrīharṣa</i> [<a href="#entry-har_sa" class="skt-link">harṣa</a>] <a href="#entry-zriihar_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du poète Śrīharṣa (12⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle); il est l'auteur du <a href="#entry-mahaakaavya" class="skt-link">mahākāvya</a> <a href="#entry-Unai_sadhacarita" class="skt-link">Naiṣadhacarita</a> et du traité de philosophie <a href="#entry-advaita" class="skt-link">advaita</a> Khaṇḍanakhaṇḍakhādya <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> nom honorifique du roi <a href="#entry-Uhar_sa" class="skt-link">Harṣa</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-zrii" class="skt-link">śrī</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zriik_r_s_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śrīkṛṣṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-k_r_s_na" class="skt-link">kṛṣṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-zriik_r_s_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. le Divin <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zriik_r_s_nabha_t_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śrīkṛṣṇabhaṭṭa</i> [<a href="#entry-bha_t_ta" class="skt-link">bhaṭṭa</a>] <a href="#entry-zriik_r_s_nabha_t_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. de Śrīkṛṣṇabhaṭṭa, auteur du 18⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle; on lui doit not. la <a href="#entry-Uv_rttidiipikaa" class="skt-link">Vṛttidīpikā</a> et la <a href="#entry-Uv_rttamuktaavalii" class="skt-link">Vṛttamuktāvalī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zriik_r_s_nama_tha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śrīkṛṣṇamaṭha</i> [<a href="#entry-ma_tha" class="skt-link">maṭha</a>] <a href="#entry-zriik_r_s_nama_tha" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. np. du temple Śrīkṛṣṇamaṭha d'<a href="#entry-Uu_dupii" class="skt-link">Uḍupī</a>, fondé par <a href="#entry-Umadhva" class="skt-link">Madhva</a> au 13⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle, grand centre de débats philosophiques; il est consacré à <a href="#entry-Ubaalak_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Bālakṛṣṇa</a>; [<a href="#entry-Umadhvavijaya" class="skt-link">Madhvavijaya</a>] on dit que son idole provient de <a href="#entry-Udvaarakaa" class="skt-link">Dvārakā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zriik_r_s_navilaasa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śrīkṛṣṇavilāsa</i> [<a href="#entry-vilaasa" class="skt-link">vilāsa</a>] <a href="#entry-zriik_r_s_navilaasa" class="skt-link">n.</a> [<i>śrīkṛṣṇavilāsakāvya</i>] lit. np. du Śrīkṛṣṇavilāsa, poème de <a href="#entry-Usukumaara" class="skt-link">Sukumāra</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-zriik_r_s_na" class="skt-link">śrīkṛṣṇa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zriik_rcchra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śrīkṛcchra</i> [<a href="#entry-k_rcchra" class="skt-link">kṛcchra</a>] <a href="#entry-zriik_rcchra" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. pénitence de trois jours consistant à ne se nourrir que des excréments de la vache.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zriika_n_tha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śrīkaṇṭha</i> [<a href="#entry-ka_n_tha" class="skt-link">kaṇṭha</a>] <a href="#entry-zriika_n_tha" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. épith. de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>-<a href="#entry-Uniilaka_n_tha" class="skt-link">Nīlakaṇṭha</a> «à la Gorge splendide» <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. de Śrīkaṇṭha, philosophe du 11⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle qui développa la doctrine shivaïte de la «non-dualité distinguée» [<a href="#entry-vizi_s_taadvaita" class="skt-link">viśiṣṭādvaita</a>] dans ses commentaires du <a href="#entry-Ubrahmasuutra" class="skt-link">Brahmasūtra</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. de Śrīkaṇṭha_1 <a href="#entry-Uniilaka_n_tha_1" class="skt-link">Nīlakaṇṭha_1</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Ubhavabhuuti" class="skt-link">Bhavabhūti</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zriikaalahasti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śrīkālahasti</i> [<a href="#entry-kaala_2" class="skt-link">kāla_2</a>-<a href="#entry-hasti" class="skt-link">hasti</a>] <a href="#entry-zriikaalahasti" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. np. de Śrīkālahasti, ville sacrée au sud de l'<a href="#entry-Uandhra" class="skt-link">Andhra</a> Pradesh, à 36 km de Tirupati sur la rivière Svarṇamukhī; on y trouve le grand temple du Vent <a href="#entry-Ukaalahastiizvara" class="skt-link">Kālahastīśvara</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zriikalaapuur_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śrīkalāpūrṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-kalaa" class="skt-link">kalā</a>-<a href="#entry-puur_na" class="skt-link">pūrṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-zriikalaapuur_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. titre de Śrīkalāpūrṇa «Gloire de plénitude artistique».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zriikha_n_da">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śrīkhaṇḍa</i> [<a href="#entry-kha_n_da" class="skt-link">khaṇḍa</a>] <a href="#entry-zriikha_n_da" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zriikha_n_da" class="skt-link">n.</a> santal, bois de santal.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zriila">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">श्रील</span> <i>śrīla</i> [<a href="#entry-zrii" class="skt-link">śrī</a><a href="#entry--la" class="skt-link">-la</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zriila" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zriila" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zriilaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śrīlā</i> prospère, heureux; éminent <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. np. de Śrīla «Bienheureux», titre de respect <a href="#entry-vai_s_nava" class="skt-link">vaiṣṇava</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zriimadbhaagavata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śrīmadbhāgavata</i> [<a href="#entry-bhaagavata" class="skt-link">bhāgavata</a>] <a href="#entry-zriimadbhaagavata" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Śrīmadbhāgavata, le «Vénérable <a href="#entry-Ubhaagavatapuraa_na" class="skt-link">Bhāgavatapurāṇa</a>».<br> <a href="#entry-zriimat" class="skt-link">śrīmat</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zriimadbhagavadgiitaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śrīmadbhagavadgītā</i> [<a href="#entry-bhagavadgiitaa" class="skt-link">bhagavadgītā</a>] <a href="#entry-zriimadbhagavadgiitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de la Śrīmadbhagavadgītā, qualifiant la «Sainte <a href="#entry-Ubhagavadgiitaa" class="skt-link">Bhagavadgītā</a>».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zriimat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śrīmat</i> [<a href="#entry--mat" class="skt-link">-mat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zriimat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zriimat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zriimatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śrīmatī</i> beau, aimable, charmant, plaisant; splendide, glorieux <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> éminent, célèbre, illustre <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> bien établi; riche, fortuné, prospère — <a href="#entry-zriimat" class="skt-link">m.</a> personne de qualité — iic. adr. (honorifique) Vénérable ⟨np.⟩.<br> <i>śrīmān</i> adr. Monsieur.<br> <i>śrīmatī</i> adr. Madame (femme mariée dont l'époux est encore en vie).<br> <i>śrīmadrāmānujāya</i> <i>namaḥ</i> Gloire au Vénérable <a href="#entry-Uraamaanuja" class="skt-link">Rāmānuja</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zriimattaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śrīmattā</i> [<a href="#entry--taa" class="skt-link">-tā</a>] <a href="#entry-zriimattaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> splendeur, beauté; gloire; fortune.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zriimukha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śrīmukha</i> [<a href="#entry-mukha" class="skt-link">mukha</a>] a. <a href="#entry-zriimukha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-zriimukha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-zriimukhii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>śrīmukhī</i> qui a un joli visage — <a href="#entry-zriimukha" class="skt-link">m.</a> astr. np. de Śrīmukha «qui a une jolie frimousse», personnifiant l'an 7 d'un cycle de vie [<a href="#entry-sa_mvatsara" class="skt-link">saṃvatsara</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zriinagara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śrīnagara</i> [<a href="#entry-nagara" class="skt-link">nagara</a>] <a href="#entry-zriinagara" class="skt-link">n.</a> géo. np. de Śrīnagara «Riche Ville», capitale du <a href="#entry-Ukazmiira" class="skt-link">Kaśmīra</a>, sur les bords de la <a href="#entry-Uvitastaa" class="skt-link">Vitastā</a> (mod. Jhelum); cette ville très ancienne était un centre bd. au temps de l'empire <a href="#entry-Umaurya" class="skt-link">Maurya</a> puis de l'époque <a href="#entry-ku_saa_na" class="skt-link">kuṣāṇa</a>; le roi <a href="#entry-Upravarasena" class="skt-link">Pravarasena</a> II en fit sa capitale <a href="#entry-Upravarasenapura" class="skt-link">Pravarasenapura</a>-<a href="#entry-Upravara" class="skt-link">Pravara</a>; elle fut prise par les Huns au 6⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle, devint musulmane sous les Moghols, puis fut annexée par les Sikhs en 1820.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zriinivaasa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śrīnivāsa</i> [<a href="#entry-nivaasa_1" class="skt-link">nivāsa_1</a>] <a href="#entry-zriinivaasa" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. résidence de <a href="#entry-Uzrii" class="skt-link">Śrī</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Śrīnivāsa «Refuge de <a href="#entry-Uzrii" class="skt-link">Śrī</a>», épith. de <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zriinivaasakalyaa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śrīnivāsakalyāṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-kalyaa_na" class="skt-link">kalyāṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-zriinivaasakalyaa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> soc. cérémonie de mariage céleste entre <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a>-<a href="#entry-Uzriinivaasa" class="skt-link">Śrīnivāsa</a> et <a href="#entry-Uzrii" class="skt-link">Śrī</a>-<a href="#entry-Upadmaavatii" class="skt-link">Padmāvatī</a>; son festival est célébré not. à Tirumala.<br> <a href="#entry-zriinivaasa" class="skt-link">śrīnivāsa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zriiparvata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śrīparvata</i> [<a href="#entry-parvata" class="skt-link">parvata</a>] <a href="#entry-zriiparvata" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. np. de la chaîne montagneuse Śrīparvata, au sud du plateau de <a href="#entry-Uzriizaila" class="skt-link">Śrīśaila</a>; mod. Nallamala.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zriipati">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>śrīpati</i> [<a href="#entry-pati" class="skt-link">pati</a>] <a href="#entry-zriipati" class="skt-link">m.</a> «maître de la fortune», nom de prince <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Śrīpati, épith. de <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a> «l'époux de <a href="#entry-Uzrii" class="skt-link">Śrī</a>» <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. du mathématicien Śrīpati_1, auteur du <a href="#entry-Uga_nitatilaka" class="skt-link">Gaṇitatilaka</a> (11⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-zriipatiparizi_s_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
